DEVA KERALAM

DEVA KRERALAM
(CHANDRA KALA NADI)
BY
ACHYUTA OF KERALA
& VENKATESA
BOOK II
(Containing Slokas 2719 - 3407
and Slokas 1 to 1997)
Translated by
R. Santhanam
SAGAR PUBLICATIONS
Ved Mansion
72, Janpath
New Dethi - 110 OO1

First Published 1993
© Publishers
Published by Narinder Sagar for Sagar Publications 72, Ved mansion
Janpath New Delhi - 110001
Typeset & Printed by Impact Compuprints
Binding by A.J. Book Binding House

Preface
I have great pleasure in presenting herewith Book II of Deva Keralam.
The encouragement given by the readers following appearance of Book I hereof is
thankfully acknowledged.
Although the original is popularly attributed to Achyuta, in actuality Venkatesa’s
name also appears as author at one stage. This Venkatesa need not be confused with
Sarvartha Chintamani’s author, vide notes given under sloka 1 infra. The original
authorship is actually divided as under:
Sloka No. 1 to 3407 by Achyuta in the first phase.
Sloka No. 1 to 4775 by Venkatesa in the later phase.
Thus there is a total of 8102 original slokas for which our arrangement of presentation
1S as under:
Sloka 1 to sloka 2718 of Achyuta in Book I.
Sloka 2719 to sloka 3407 of Achyuta, and sloka 1 to sloka 1997 of Venkatesa in Book
Ol.
Rest of the work from slokas from No. 1998 to 4775 of Venkatesa is being brought
out as Book III.
In the present volumealso, there are many general principles applicable to the various
ascendants, apart from Nadiamsa discussions confined to given signs. The reader will be
able to understand the same as he progresses with the study of the text, remembering at the
same time the general outlines given in the preface for Book I.
[have every hope that the kind reader will forgive me for my shortcomings if any and
accord a favourable reception to my present efforts.
R. Santhanam
(Editor,
The Times Of Astrology)
9/1040 Govindpuri
Kalkaji
New Delhi 110 019
(India)

Co: Sy

॥ मकर लग्नम - [aera] ॥
त्रलोक्यांशे पूर्वभागे लग्ने च मकरे तथा |
नुधत्रिशांशके जातस्तृत्ीयोऽसो प्रकीर्तितः।
महानदीप्राततयदेशे दक्षिणोत्तरवीधिषु ॥२७१९ |
प्राग्भागे जननं चेति steered |
गेहेशे FATTY तद्राश्यं शत्रिकोणगे ॥२७२० ॥
तन्नवांशगृहद्ररे प्रसूतिरनात्र संशयः।
2719 - 27201/2. Trailokys Nadiamsa -
Capricorn Ascendant (Up to sloka 2769): (a) The
native of former half of Trailokya Nadiamsa in
Capricorn ascendant, in Mercury’s Trimsamsa, will
be the third issue of the family. His birth will be in the’
region of a great river, in a north-south spread street.
The house concerned will be in the eastern part (of the
town) and will be a north-facing one
(b) Find out in which Navamsa the 4th lord is
placed. The house of birth may (also) correspond to
the indications given by the said Rasi or one of its
trines.
Notes: (a) In Capricorn, Trailokya Nadiamsa
extends from 14° 48' to 15° 0.
Ignore Mercury’s Trimsamsa condition and follow only the relevant Nadiamsa stipulation.
(b) This is a general rule and can be applied to all
ascendants irrespective of the Nadiamsa, and is one
of the conditions stipulated for determining the home
of birth.
लग्नेशे धनराशिस्थे लाभेशे सुखराशिगे ॥२७२१॥
्रिशद्रर्षति परं भाग्यमरोगदृढगात्रवान् ।
2721 - 27211/2. Prosperity: If the lord of the
ascendant is in the 2nd house as the 11th lord (Mars)
is inthe 4th house (in Aries), he will attain prosperity
after the thirtieth year of his age. This yoga will also
give him a physique which is free from diseases and
13 Strong.
Notes: The effects given apply to the whole of
Capricorn ascendant irrespective of the Nadiamsa as
could be scen below.
Prosperity: Saturnin the 2nd house in Aquarius,
while Mars is in the 4th house will assure prosperity
Deva Keralam 1
right from youth. The influence of Saturn onthe 10th
house, Libra, is passed on through Mars. By himself,
Mars is a functional benefic for Capricorn. When he
is related to Saturn and the 10th house, the native of
Capricorn ascendant is sure to rise without obstacles.
Healthy Body: As the lord of the 11th house, 6th
from the 6th house, Mars has a definite say on health,
like Mercury the 6th lord. The association of Mars
with ascendant lord, Saturn, the latter placed in
Moolatrikona in the 2nd, is a plus point. Such a
person will be free from illness and will attain
prosperity.
Mercury in Aries, while Saturn is in the 2nd, will
also give similar effects for Capricorn natives. But
prosperity, though certain, will be through certain
odds caused by inimical activities as Mercury is also
the 6th lord, and gets his placement in a bitter
enemy’s sign. Certainty in prosperity is because
Mercury is the lord of the 9th for Capricorn and
bosom friend of Saturn.
युमांशकगते मन्दे मीनस्थे च प्रजायते ॥२७२२॥
बाल्ये दारिदमापतन्नो यौवने सौख्ययोगवान् |
पञ्चविंशात् परं भाग्यं गेहे लक्षमीकराक्षवान् 121623 ॥
2722 - 2723. Saturn in Pisces: Should Saturn be
found in Pisces, witha dual Navamsa, the subject will
be in the grip of penury during his childhood. He will
enjoy sound health and prosperity in his youth. After
25 years of age, goddess Lakshmi will bless his
family.
Notes : Yugmamsaka in this case is dual navamsa,
and notspecifically Gemini, as Pisces 1९885115 referred
to. Saturn in Pisces will get one of the other three dual
Navamsa, viz. Virgo, Sagittarius and Pisces.
The hints given hereof apply to Capricorn ascendant, in general. By his placement in the 3rd
house, Saturn will aspect the 9th house, denoting
father. Wealth or poverty in childhood depends on
father (or mother). Saturn in Pisces with Virgo
Navamsa will be in the 9th from natal ascendant. In
Sagittarius Navamsa, he will be in the 12th from natal
ascendant and in Pisces Navamsa, he will be in the
3rd in Vargottama. Saturn’s placement in Jupiter’s
sign or Navamsa will produce some adverse effects
about father, as Mercury, the lord of the 9th, does got

Chandra Kala Nadi
go well with Jupiter. Saturn in relation to the 9th from
a sign of Jupiter, for Capricorn, may in the initial
stage not give money through father, but is bound to
give good effects in the long run by virtue of his good
position and good rulership. For Saturn is a slow
planet in revealing good effects. As the lord of the
ascendant, he assumes the onus of protecting the 9th
house.
In my view, we should add Leo Navamsa as well
in which case father’s poverty will be intense.
In analysing a bhava vis a vis a planet’s placement we better take into note two important factors:
The occupant’s relationship with the significator of
that bhava, on the one hand. On the other hand, the
relationship of the Rasi dispositor and Navamsa
dispositor of the occupant with a given significator.
Generally, Saturn related to the 9th is notso good
as he is quite allergic to the Sun, the significator of
father.

2724. Movable Ascendant: If birth be in a
movable ascendant in the subject Nadiamsa, in Mercury’s Trimsamsa, the subject will be blood-red in
complexion. He will be of medium build with a
symmetric body.
Notes: See 2719 (a) supra regarding Trimsamsa
condition.

2725 - 27311/2. Capricorn Ascendant: (a) One
born in this ascendant, in the former half of Trailokya
Nadiamsa, will be good-looking. intelligent, eloquent, be endowed with charming face and eyes, be
fortunate, religious and will enjoy effects of Raja
Yoga. He will be fond of superior ascetics and will
worship gods and Brahmins. he will eat limited food,
will speak within a limit and will sleep for limited
hours. He will conquer his Senses, and will be skillful
in understanding others’ thoughts. He will beautify
himself with ornaments and also be fond of ornaments. He will be an evil person to the evil and will
be unkind to unkind people. He will be bereft of
paternal fortunes (or happiness) and will have selfearned wealth.
(b) Ifthe 4th lord (noted in Rasi diagram) is inthe
9th place in Navamsa (1.6 9th counted from Navamsa
ascendant), he will be skillful in law and philosophy.
The native of the former halfofthe subject Nadiamsa
(without reference to 4th lord above) will be helpful
to all, will be endowed with valour, adventure and
fame, be fond of listening to mythological stories, be
ever wealthy, well-disposcd to the poor, will worship
Lord Siva by vows and fasts, and be devoted to Lords
Vishnu and Siva. He will ever take to virtuous path
and will have excellent religious attainments. He will
gain through agriculture, quadrupeds etc. He will
lord over villages and lands.
Notes: (a) Applicable only to the subject
Nadiamsa.
(b) Skill in law and philosophy applies to the 4th
lord’s position as specified in the text while the rest
applies to first half of Trailokya Nadiamsa with the
above mentioned placement of the lord of the 4th,
Mars.
For any ascendant, the 4th lord placed in the 9th
from the natal ascendant or from the Navamsa ascendant, will be good for legal and philosophical
attainments.

2732-27331/2. Co-born: (a) If the 3rd lord Jupiter is in the 6th house from the ascendant Capricom,
ora trine thereof (i.c.2nd/10th), the subject will have
neither an older co-born, nor a younger one.
(b) If the 2nd (Aquarius) is occupied by Saturn,
he will have a sister endowed with longevity, and
anotherasynonymof difficulties, but living ina good
abode, and yet another endowed withsons and daughter.
Notes: (a) For Capricorn ascendant, if Jupiter is
placed in Aquarius, Gemini, or Libra, one will be the
lonely child of the family.
(b) Obviously this position of Satuin “enotes
three sisters, while Jupiter in 3rd altogether ceprives
one of co-born. We have no hints as to what Saturn
will do in this regard if he is placed in Gemini/Libra.
However, my suggestion is that Saturn as exalted
ascendant lord will normally not make the native a
lonely issue, nor will he do so while in Gemini, being
in direct aspect to the 3rd.

2734 - 2735. Mars: Should the 4th Jord Mars be
conjunct Saturn or be in aspect to Saturn, the native
of the subject Nadiamsa will lose his mother in his
very childhood. If Mars be with the Sun in Aries, the
latter in his sign of exaltation, the native will be
wealthy with elephants.
Notes: Mars in the 4th with Saturn’s connection
is bad for mother’s life-span, only when the ascending degree in Capricorn is Trailokya Nadiamsa.
Otherwise in general for this ascendant, Mars placed
in the 4th with Saturn’s connection cannot cause
short-life for the mother of the native.
Mars and the Sunin Aries isa yoga for wealthand
Deva Keralam 3
prosperity for Capricorn natives in general.
लग्नेशवहिशयुतेन्दुनाथो
भाग्यस्थितो वा यदि कर्मगो aT |
सामर्थ्यवान् वाहनलाभयुक्तः

2736. Mars-Saturn-Moon: If these three planets conjoin in the 9th or 10th house, the native will be
a skillful person. He will gain conveyances, be a poet,
will hold the assemblage of men in charm and be
endowed with four kinds of traditional conveyances
(viz. chariots, horses, elephants and men-borne
palanquins).
Notes: The conjunction referred to here should
occur in Virgo/Libra, and is of three angular lords. It
will be far superior for a Capricom native occurring
in the 10th, the best of the angles for that matter,
where Saturn’s exaltation will add further weightage.
Asa result, the subject will receive the above effects
in an increased manner.
This yoga is also applicable to Capricorn ascendant in general as it is conjunction of lords of the Ist,
4th and the 7th in the 9th or the 10th. But for the
Nadiamsa, poetic skills may not be so evident 17 the
10th house, Libra. Inthe 9th house, apart from giving
the results mentioned in the original, poetic skills
may be abundant as the Moon, signifying imagination, occupies Virgo, a fertile sign of intellect, and the
3 planets aspect the 3rd house, ruled by Jupiter.
"Poetic skills" denoted need not necessarily
confine to poetry. The 3rd house denotes one’s
ability as a writer and even as a singer.

2737. Jupiter in Pushkaramsa: The native of
Trailokya Nadiamsa in Capricorn having the planet

2738 - 2739. Marriage: During the rulership of
the 2nd dasa, when Jupiter transits a trine counted
from the sign occupied by 10th lord (Venus), the
native’s marriage will take place with a girl hailing
from the southern or south-eastern part of his birth
place. These results have elaborately been narrated in
Sukra Nadi in the chapter devoted to the Bhava
concerned, i.e. 7th house.
Notes: Although the text qualifies Venus by his
10th lordship, he is al!-importnat in marriage as he is
the significator in principle. For any ascendant,
Jupiter’s trina] transit is essential with reference to
the following:
a) Venus. b) 7th lord from ascendant. c) the
Moon. d) the 7th lord from the Moon.
If Jupiter transit trinally with reference to more
than one of the above four, marniage will be expedited.
There are other factors like Navamsa ascendant
involving Jupiter’s transit. But the above is the
beginning point of the probe.
The girl hailing froma given direction is peculiar
to the Nadiamsa in question.

2740. Single Marriage: If the 7th lord Moon is
ina fixed sign with a fixed Navainsa (not necessarily
Vargothama), while the significator (Venus) is also
in a fixed sign, it is said, there will be only one
marriage to the native of the subject Nadiamsa.
Notes: This clue about the native remaining with
One marriage nullifying other combinations for a
remarriage can be applied in respect of any ascendant. Venus, the Moon, 7th lord and ascendant lord
being in fixed sign and fixed Navamsa disallow a
remarriage. This however does not define the quality
and length of first marriage.

2741 - 27431/2. Other Effects: (a) If the 7th lord
Moon (counted from natal ascendant) is in the 8th
house in Navamsa (i.e. inthe 8th place from Navamsa
ascendant), he will be sinful by joining others’
women.
(b) One bom in the former half of Trailokya
Nadiamsa in Capricorn ascendant, having Venus in
Somavathi Nadiamsa will have secretive lust for
sexual pleasures. He will be so sexually excited as to
join his spouse. even on prohibited days.
(c) If Venus is in Kanthaa Nadiamsa, he will be
full of merry during sexual union.
(d) Ifthe 10th lord (Venus) is in the 4th (Aries).
the subject of the said Kanthaa Nadiamsa will perform good deeds and will have good conduct.
Notes: (a) The 7th lord from any natal ascendant

will show want of morality, more on the vart of the

(b) Prohibited days for sexual union, by Satsras
in gencra] are: 1. Ekadasi (11th lunar day) in either
fortnight.
2. Amavasya (30th lunar day).
3. Four days from the commencement of a
woman’s periods.
There are many more stipulations. The above is
a brief account.
(c) Kanthaa Nadiamsa occurs as under:
Movable signs 06° 00' to 06° 12'
Fixed signs 23° 48 to 24° 00!
Common signs 21° 00' to 21° 12.

2750 - 2752. Trailokya Nadiamsa - Capricorn
Ascendant: The subject will beget a daughter when
Jupiter transits the 8th house (Leo). After the age of
35, his prosperity will be onthe rise and will cause the
mercy of goddess Lakshmi. Either his 39 year, or 38th
year, will endanger his wife but she will recover
health early. The subject will obtain rulership, happiness to his men and increase of wealth and grains.
Sages say, the dasa of Venus, who is in aspect to a
benefic, will beget himthe mercy of goddess Lakshmi
and royal kind of comforts.

Capricorn in general. Such dasa will cause wealth,

effects as could be observed. However if the Moon be

केतुयोगे पदाधौशे वायुना बधिरामयः॥२७९७ ॥
तत्पापपरिहारार्थं शानि कुर्याद्यथाविषि |
2796 - 27971/2, Deformity: (a) For a native of
former half of this Nadiamsa, Venus in the 7th will
cause acquisition of a spouse with physical deformity.
(b) The native will be deaf if Ketu joins the 7th
lord Mercury.
(c) To overcome these ills, propitiations as prescribed be done.
Notes: (a) Physical deformity to spouse may
specially apply to Venus placed in the 7th for Pisces
ascendant, Nadiamsa cited. Even otherwise, Venus
in the 7th in debility cannot be of an asset in respect
of marriage. Such yoga will cause some notable
blemish for the spouse apart from delay in marriage.
(b) If Ketu and Mercury are in close orbs, this
conjunction - of Ketu and Mercury ~ will cause
deafness in case of a Pisces subject.
(c) The remedies are not mentioned clearly.
However, for blemish at “a”, the planet Venus, and
for blemish at “b” ihe planet Ketu should be propitiated by suitable Mantras and prayers.
उत्तरांशे धूर्तभार्या त्वरोगदृढशालिनी ॥२७९८ ॥

एकाशीतिपदं चक्रं पलेनेकेन निर्मितम् |
कल्पोक्तविधिना GA ग्रहाणां च यथाक्रमम् ॥२८०२॥
स्वराक्षरं लिखेच्चक्रे त्वभिभित्सहितं तथा |
देवज्ञाय प्रदानेन सन्तानोत्पत्तिमादिशेत् ॥२८०३ ॥
2798 - 2803. (a) One born in the second half will
beget a wicked spouse, who will however be free
from diseases, with a strong physique.
(b) His counterpart, i.e. the native of first
half of the subject Nadiamsa, Pisces ascendant, will
have two wives.
(c) Mercury in the Aroodha of the 5th, in
the company of Ketu and in Arics Navamsa will
cause obstacles in acquisition of progeny. (Sone
texts read Putrabhave while some use the expression
Putraarodhe. 5th Aroodha is correct expression, as
Mercury in Sth in Cancer does not obtain Aries
Navamsa.) The same effect will come to pass if the
ascendant Pisces begets the aspect of Saturn and [alls
in Scorpio Navamsa. To remedy this ill, donation of
an image of the zodiac be done. The image of the
zodiacand the planets be worshipped as prescribed in
the Sastras. The names of the vowels, including the

Deva Keralam 11
name of Abhijit Nakshatra be written on the image of
zodiac and be given away to an astrologer. Then the
blemish in respect of progeny will be warded off.
Notes: “a” and “b” are clear.
(c) For Pisces ascendant, the 5th Aroodha will be
So many Signs away as the Moonis from Cancer (with
exceptions mentioned by me in my notes in Brihat
Parasara Hora, Part 1, English translation). Mercury’s position should be insuch place along with Ketu.
Unless Mercury in the yoga obtains Aries navamsa,
obstacles in acquiring progeny should not be expected. Alternatively Saturn should aspect Pisces
which should ascend between 13° 20' and 16° 40’
getting Scorpio Navamsa. Remedy suggested is donation of a zodiac’s image to an astrologer. The
image should contain inscription of the 28 Nakshatras
including Abhijit.

2804 - 2806. Dasa Effects: (a) In the sub period
of an obstructive (or adversc) planet during the major
rulership of the 8th lord, the subject will lose relatives
of his age group.
(b) The 2nd half of the 4th dasa will cause
him great happiness.
(c) For birth in the former half of the
subject Nadiamsa, see if the 8th lord’s dasa is in
operation. If so, when Jupiter comes to Taurus in
transit, death of mother be declared. So also in the
intervals of Venus and Rahu (without reference to
Jupiter’s transit).
Notes: (a) Obstructive planets for Pisces ascendant are Venus, Mercury and Saturn. The sub period of
one of these planets, or that of occupants thereof will
cause danger to relatives in the main period of Venus.
The Nadiamsa need not be a considerationas the dasa
of 8th lord for any ascendant is capable of causing
grief.
(b) This axiom is a broad clue to this Nadiamsa.
(c) Linking death of mother in 8th lord’s dasa to
the transit of Jupiter applies only to the subject
Nadiamsa.

2807. Lord of the 8th: (a) Should the 8th lord
be inthe 1 1th (i.e. Venus in Capricorn) the native will
be very fortunate. In the end of such 8th lord’s dasa,
his son will fall sick.
(b) The 8th lord Venus obtaining the 4th
place in Navainsa will also give financial acquisition
apart from causing a second marrage.
Notes: Two combinations involving Venus are
discussed. (५) By being in the 1 1th house, he will give
fortunes to the native in general, and sickness in the
concluding part of Venus Dasa. Venus in the 11th
house for any ascendant except Scorpio is generally
a contributory factor for fortunes. The degree of
fortunes will depend on the circumstances prevailing
in a given case. Sickness to a son is applicable only
to the subject Nadiamsa.
(b) Without reference to Venus in Capricorn, his occupying the 4th house from Navamsa
ascendant will give financial gains and second marriage to the native. Venus being a Yoga Karaka, his
occupation of the 4th from Navamsa ascendant will
cause financial acquisition. The issue of second
marriage will get confirmed if the Navamsa ascendant is particularly a dual sign - one of Virgo, Sagittarius and Pisces. Here again, the Nadiamsa may many
times be not relevant.

2812 - 28141/2. Venus - Mars: The conjunction
of Venus and Mars for a Suprabha Nadiamsa native
will cause misfortunes, like enmity, in the dasa which
is fourth from birth. To overcome such misfortunes,
donation ofanimage of goddess Lakshmi be resorted
to. Asa result, long-lasting progress will be obtained.
In the absence of this, the person’s household will be
wholly deprived of the mercy of goddess Lakshmi.
The said remedy will bring auspicious effects in royal
service and cause destruction of enemies. Later on
due to mercy of King’s minister, the subject will
obtain progeny (?).
Notes: The conjunction of Venus - Mars for the
subject native will be destruciive in the 4th dasa, if
remedy as prescribed is not done. That is, this
conjunction will prove devastating in this context.
Obtainment of progeny after the remedy shouid
be read without “mercy of king’s minister”.

2823 - 2825. Umbilical Cord: (a) Rahu in the
9th while the Moon occupies Sundari Nadiamsa will
cause birth entangled in umbilical cord in case of
former half of Kalusha Nadiamsa.
(b) But if birth be in the second half, this

the ascendant incase of birthin former half will offset
effects of infantile mortality. The relief will however’
be less in case of second half of Kalusha Nadiamsa.

immovable ascendant with Leo/Virgo Navamsa will

2888 - 2892. Kanthaa Nadiamsa - Pisces Ascendant: (a) One born in this part of the zodiac with
Saturn in Scorpio (in the 9th house) will lose his
father in childhood and will be bereft of after- born.
He will incur grief due to loss of elder brother in the
middle of his life. He will have two sisters, out of
whom one will be long-lived, endowed with sons and
happiness. He will obtain happiness in early days by
serving a king. Afterwards he will go to forests (or
will migrate) and will serve a mean king. He will
have two wives and good (male) children.
(b) Due to Amala Yoga (caused by a benefic
being placed in the 10th in Sagittarius), he will obtain
men-borne conveyances by performing rituals as
prescribed in the Vedas.
(c) In the 4th dasa, he will be greatly happy. In

second half of the said dasa, he will beget conveyance and villages (i.e. some properties or position of
eminence). (Dhaatamse is a wrong addition in sloka

(c) The effects cited here should be read delinking Saturn in the 9th, and also Amala Yoga.

Movable sign: once from 6° to 6° 12', and again
Immovable sign: once from 23° 48' to 24° 0'" and
again from 19° 48' to 20° 0,
Common sign: once from 25° to 25° 12', and
again from 21° to 21° 12'.

His position simultaneously in Aries Navamsa will

childhood in case of birth in second half of the

Thus Venus in Aries navamsa, being in the sign
Libra, implies some danger to mother in general.

“Jyeshtaputriyutho” reads as “Seemantha
putrimaan” in another text which correctly means
that the native’s first issue will be a daughter.

2932 - 2937. Mercury in Cancer Navamsa:
Cancer Ascendant with Pisces Navamsa will lead to
following effects if Mercury obtains Cancer Navamsa simultaneously. The native will lord over many
women, be wealthy and be intent upon joining prostitutes. He will be everdevoted to pleasing his mother
but will have half-brothers (i.e. father will have
another marriage). These effects will materialise in
the dasa of the 9th lord. He will obtain conveyances
and be happy after the age of 27. Inthe dasa of 8th lord
he will befriend in royal circles and beget a chief
position. He will enjoy the mercy of Goddess Lakshmi.
In Moon’s dasa, during the sub periods of Jupiter and
Saturn, he will incur fear from enemies and destruction of peace. Further he will acquire the wrath of his
paternal uncle, urinary disorders (diabetes etc.) and
ulcer. To overcome the evils, suitable propitiations
be done.
Notes: The Rasi ascendant should be Cancer
while in Navamsa diagram, the ascendant should be
Pisces having Mercury in the Sth therefrom. As a
result, ina nutshell certain things are brought to light:
The native’s father will have a second marriage. The
subject will himself be related to many women and
will be of loose morals. He will be devoted to his
mother. The dasa of Moon will give him urinary
disorders and disputes with paternal uncle during the
sub period rulership of Jupiter and Saturn. His Mars
dasa will give him fortunes. 8th lord Saturn’s dasa
will introduce him in high circles. Remedies to
overcome urinary disorders and misunderstandings
are suggested in the following slokas.
भाग्यांशे कटके लग्ने परशुं दानमाचरेत्।
शतरुवर्गकुलच्छेदं कुठारस्य प्रदानतः NAVI? ॥
ममशतक्षयार्थाय तव पूजां करोम्यहम् |
शतुवंशक्षयार्थाय सर्वाभीषटर्थसिद्धये ॥२९३९ ॥
भयापमृत्युनाशाय कुठारं प्रददाम्यहम् |
given away to overcome this evils following which
the dangerous urinary disorder and enmity with
paternal uncle will be warded off. The following
Mantra should be recited (before the image ofa deity,
in Sanskrit): Mama Satrukshayaarthaaya thava
Poojaam karomyaham. Satruvamsa kshayaarthaaya
sarvaabhishtaartha siddhaye, Bhayaapa mrityu
naasaaya kuthaaram pradamamyaham.
Notes: “Kuthaara” and “Parasu” mean one and
the same thing, i.e. the axe of a wood-cutter. To
overcome urinary disorders and enmity with paternal
uncle, donation of an axe is suggested with recitation
of the Mantra given with Sanskrit transliteration in
the text above. The meaning of the Mantra is: To
obtain destruction of enemies, i.e. enmity with paternal uncle, I worship you (O Lord). Also fulfill all my
desires and save me from the danger of untimely
death (that could be caused due to diabetes).

2946. Movable Ascendant: One will enjoy fortunes throughout life if his ascendant being a movable one has the 9th lord in exaltation while the 4th
from ascendant is occupied by Venus.
Notes: This rule relates to Aries, Cancer, Libra
and Capricorn ascendants. Yet the following may be
noted as to how the condition forms:
1. Aries ascendant with Venus and Jupiter in
Cancer.
2. Cancer ascendant with exalted Jupiter while
Venus 15 in the 4th in Libra.
3. Libra ascendant with Venus in the 4th in
Capricorn while Mercury the 9th lord 15 required to
be in Virgo in exaltation.
4. Capricorn ascendant with Venus in the 4th in
Aries while the 9th lord Mercury is required to be in
Virgo itself, in exaltation.
Due to elongational impossibilities between
Venus and Mercury, combinations at 3 and 4 above
are not practical. Apparently the text implies only
Aries and Cancer ascendants.

मीनांशे जूकलगने तु पुद्ररंशे प्रजायते|

3007. Libra ascendant - Pisces Navamsa: One
born in this part with Mudgara Nadiamsa will be
prosperous and happy in the dasa represented by the
Nakshatra denoted by processing the Ashtaka Varga
of the Moon.
Notes; In the first instance, Mudgara Nadiamsa,
be ignored for itis a corrupt addition. We should take
only Pisces Navamsa to be valid as ascending as part
of Libra ascendant.
Secondly the clue given for analysing the dasa
with the help of Moon’s Ashtaka Varga can be
applied to any ascendant, not just to Libra with Pisces
Navamsa. The meaning is explained below:
The use of the Moon’s Ashtaka Varga is explained ina brief manner by the text without referring
to a specific house. The word “Avasisnia” meaning
remainder tells us of the involvement some process
of division. With the help of know instructions, we
can understand the same thus:
There will be Bindus in the various signs, even
as low as zero, in the Moon’s Ashtaka Varga. Since
prosperity is referred to, we can take the Bindus inthe
pre-rectified Ashtaka Varga chart prepared for the
Moon. Her Sodya Pinda be multiplied by the said
numberof Bindus and dividend by 27. The remainder
will represent a Nakshatra counted from Aswini
which will yield a certain planet’s Vimsottari dasa.
This dasa will bring prosperity.
For example there are five bindus in the 9th
(counted from natal ascendant) inthe Moon’s Ashtaka
Varga, and her Sodya Pinda is, say, 80. Thus 5
multiplied by 80 and divided by 27 leaves a remainder of 25, which represents Poorvabhadrapada
Nakshatra. Thus, Jupiter’s dasa in this case will give
prosperity.

3042---3017. The Moon’s Ashtaka Varga for

3018 - 3028. Transit in Nakshatras - General:
(a) When Saturn transits the third Nakshatra fromthe
birth Nakshatra, the native will overpower his enemics.
(b) When he moves in the second star, he will
cause great fear, danger in general and danger to life.
(c) In his movement in the fourth star, he will
give distress, quarrels and grief.
(c) In the fifth star, the planet Saturn in transit
will give mental agony and enmity with Pandya king
(i.c.problems through government) and destruction.
When Satum is in transit in the 2nd/4th stars, the king
of the country will die and the native will undergo
much grief.
(d) If the birth star or the tenth therefrom is
occupied by a malefic at birth, Saturn’s transit in this
segment will create quarrels with king and consequent distress. In the absence of these effects,
physical illness to self, and nuisance from his own
men will come to pass. When a benefic transits in the
first or tenth Nakshatra, auspicious effects will occur
and malefics according to their natural disposition
will cause mental distress.
(€) Results should be understood according to
the caste etc. (or the circumstances of the native).
(f) Should malefics occupy birth star, second,
third or eighth from it, evil transits of Jupiter and the
Sun (Kroorabde, Kroora Masage) will produce abundant difficulties.
(g) If natural benefics occupy one of these three
Nakshatras, as at “f”, these transits will cause great
happiness, proximity to king, advent of wealth, success in efforts and progress in profession.
(0) If malefics occupy first, third or seventh
Nakshatra, the above referred transits will bring
abundant difficulties. These should be deduced with
the help of Ashtaka Varga.
(i) Auspicious effects and acquisition of wealth
will take place if benefics occupy favourable
Nakshatras. Advent of wealth will occurinthe month
of 11th Jord (1.6. the Sun’s transit in any Rasi occurring on the week day ruled by the 11th lord from
ascendant) or when the Sun transits the sign represented by the ascendant’s decanate. With the Sun’s
auspicious transits, acquisition of wealth will have to
be foretold.
Notes: Results of Saturn’s transit ina certain star
should be applied to the 10th and 19th stars froma
given staras well. Forexample Janma Nakshatra (i.e.
the star occupied by the Moon, the first Nakshatra)
includes the 10th and 19th therefrom. 2nd Nakshatra
includes 11th and 20th also. So on and so forth.
These apply to any given in nativity in general.

3029 - 3031. Results of Week days: If the birth star
(containing the Moon) coincides with a week day
ruled by a benefic planet, the native will enjoy
various kinds of wealth and happiness. Birthona day
ruled by a malefic will cause physical illness, royal
wrath and financial instability. Ifit be ona Tuesday,
one will wander (aimlessly) and will incur danger
through royal anger. Monday birth will grant happiness, possession of wonderful flowers, sexual
enjoyments filled with love, and enjoyment of quality food. (Additional effects for) Tuesday birth will
be grief, self-destruction, and quarrels. Mercury’s
day, viz. Wednesday as the day of birth will cause
happiness, acquisition of (or enjoyment) intoxicant
drinks, and ghee apart from learning and wealth.
Thursday birth will cause acquisition of robes, wealth
and physical happiness. One will enjoy ornaments,
sexual pleasures, scents and a variety of clothes if the

34 Chandra Kala Nadi
birth be ona Friday - ruled by Venus. Saturday being
the day of birth will give grief, difficulties, fear,
troubles from servants and diseases.
Notes: In the first instance, week days are divided
between benefic and malefic planets, thus: Monday,
Wednesday, Thursday and Friday are benefic days
ruled in order by the Moon (irrespective of Paksha or
lunar half), Mercury (irrespective of his association),
Jupiter and Venus. Malefic week days are Sunday,
Tuesday and Saturday and are ruled by the Sun, Mars
and Saturn in that order. Now see the following
clarificatory notes.
Benefic week days will give wealth and happiness
in combination with other horoscopic influences.
“Birth star coinciding with week day ruled by.......”
is an unintelligible clause.
When the day of birth is ruled by a malefic it will
make one prone to illness depending on the other
yogas in the horoscope.
The following classical quotations may be of interest.
One born on Sunday will be endangered in either
the first month or the 6th month, or in the 22nd/231rd
year. His life span will be 60 years.
Monday-born will face danger in the 11th month or
in the 26th/27th year. His life span will be 84 years.
Tuesday-born will be in trouble in the 2nd or 22n
year. He will be always sick and will live upto 74
years.
Wednesday-bor will incur danger inthe 8th month
or in the 8th year. He will die after his 64th year.
Thursday-born will be in danger in 7th month,
16th/13th year. He will live upto 84th year.
Friday-born will never fall sick. He will die in the
60th year.
Saturday-born will incurdangerin the 1st month or
in the 13th year. His life will end in 100th year.

3034. Should the ascending degree be aspected or

Deva Keralam
occupied by a malefic planet, or by a planet that has
lost in a war with another, or be occupied by an
inimical planet, then in its periods the subject will be
involved in disputes provided the said yoga is
unaspected by a benefic planet.
Notes: “Occupation” ofa malefic is not in our text,
but is adopted from another version and added to the.
translation.
“Aroodha Bhaga” is ascending degree, that is the
sign ascending at birth.
Planetary war: When two planets (except the
luminaries) are on the same degree thee is sa ५10 be
a war between them. The one in the north is declared
victorious.
Amore clear picture is givenby C.G. Rajan. When
two planets out of the five, viz. Mars to Saturn, are in
one and the same sign with longitudes of identical
degree, minute and second, a planetary war occurs.
The victor will have higher latitude.
I give below an example.
On23rd August 23, 1992, Venus and Jupiter in Leo
attained the same degree, minute and second while
the latitude of Venus was 1° 17' North, and that of
Jupiter 1° 4. Hence Venus, with a higher latitude
should be treated as winner.
Another planet that could be connected with the
ascendant by aspect of association is one inimical to
the lord of the ascendant. Example: Capricom ascendant related to the Sun, dead enemy of ascendant
lord Saturn.
In each of these cases, the result is involvement in
quarrels, in the dasa periods of the concerned planet.
In case of the example of Capricorn, the dasa of the
Sun will give the said result.
Protection from the said adversity is a helpful
association of the ascendant with a benefic.

3035 - 3037. Dasa Effects: During the first Dasa
the native will have eating comforts, will beget
auspicious effects and various kinds of wealth apart
from grief. The second dasa will cause wealth,
happiness and apparels. Destruction of quadrupeds,
landed properties and money, and grief to one’s near
and dear will be produced by the third dasa. Additionally sickness, enmity with relatives, quarrels and
sudden outbursts of anger will follow. Inthe 4thdasa,
effects to occurare well-being, happiness, wealthand
worship. The fifth dasa will cause grief due to loss of
wealth following royal wrath. One will acquire inthe
sixth dasa happiness, wealth, success, lands, garlands
and robes. The seventh dasa will produce many a
kind of grief, difficulties and ailments due to vitiation
of three bodily temperaments viz. bile, wind and
phlegm. Acquisition of royal honours, ornaments,
conveyances, involvement in divine undertakings
and bed comforts will take place in the eighth dasa.
The last of all, the ninth dasa will cause friendliness
with the king and virtuous men, fondness for learning, obeying royal orders, arrival of sons, friends and
relatives and skill. These apart, one will be worried
on account of unwed progeny, will incur fever,
phlegmatic fevers and troubles to sitand to sleep, and
wanderaimlessly. (The word “Subham” is a corrupt
addition at the end of the sloka No. 3037.)
Notes: The above slokas give general hints about
the nine dasas commencing from the first dasa. The
relevant terms are not however exactly used. For
example, “Janmarkshe” means Moon sign and not
literally first dasa. But subsequent references are to
Sampath, Vipath etc. upto Parama Maitra. Thus
these refer only to Dasa sequences.
The summary of the effects of nine dasas given by

36 Chandra Kala Nadi
the text can be arranged as under ignoring certain
over-stated hints:
ist dasa: Happiness and grief.
2nd dasa: Financial progress
3rd dasa: Losses, grief, sickness, enmity and loss
of temper.
4th dasa: Wealth and happiness.
5th dasa: Grief, 1088 and troubles from government (or professional debacles) and other related
SOUICES.
6th dasa: Properties, wealth and general happlness
7th dasa: Various ailments and grief.
8th dasa: Royal honours, conveyances and spiritual progress.
9th dasa: Worry about progeny, illness, royal
association etc.
Refer also to Phala Deepika etc.

3038 - 3039. (a) Saturn transiting in the 10th house
(read “Karmarkshe” instead of “Janmarkshe”) will

3040. Saturn’s Transit: The signs that are weak,
when transited by Saturn, will be a cause of a great
deal of difficulties. The specific evil months (should
be traced with the help of adverse solar movements).
Notes: "Tarabala Viheenarksha" has been translated as weak sign So as to be practical in interpretation. Otherwise, this term means: the signs that have
weak (of afflicted) constellations.
Weak signs referred to here are those Bhavas
which fall short of Shadbala (or six source strength,
viz. Kala Bala etc.) and those that are deprived of
minimum Ashtaka Varga dots, and the effects of
relevant transits of Saturn will be only adverse. The
minute period of adversity will be specifically traceable with unfavourable transits of the Sun, narrowing
down to a month.
Prastharashtaka Varga is another method.
The Sun is adverse when he transits the following
places with reference to concerned natal positions,
according to Sphuji Dhwaja:
From ascendant: 3rd, Sth, 6th, 9th, and 12th.
From Saturn: 3rd, and 12th.
From Jupiter: 151, 3rd, 7th, 8th, and 12th.
From Venus: Ist, 3rd, 4th, 5th, 8th, 9th, and 10th.
From Mars: 3rd, 5th, 6th, and 12th.
From Mercury: Ist, 2nd, 4th, 7th, and 8th.
From the Moon: Ist, 2nd, 4th, 5th, 7th, 8th, 9th, and
12th.

i. , given to sexual pleasures, will have limited

mean. Later he will have worries about progeny and
be emaciated. His brother will be initiated with
Pranava Mantra (the sacred syllable "Om" - taught
to Lord Siva by His Son, Kartikeya), be bold, splendorous, be a pleased soul, (one time) a sinner, given
to anger and credited with valorous acts.

3073 - 3075. Nara Yoga: If Saturn in the 7th house
without the aspect of a benefic planct joins an exalted
planet, a yoga called Nara Yoga is produced. The
native will be, as a result, a great sinner, will torture
and cheat others, will do base acts and kill (or trouble)
Brahmins. On account of these sins, he will incur
diseases and loss of sons and be emaciated.
Notes: This yoga does not apply to Aquarius
ascendant although it appears in this part. No planet
is exalted in Leo. This is a yoga generally applicable
to all ascendants but Aquarius, Taurus, Leo, Gemini
and Sagittarius. No planet is exalted in the 7th in
these cases.
“Uchcha Yoge” is a corrupt addition in sloka 3074
referring the source to be another authority.

3076. Rahu & Guru Chandala Yoga: (a) Should

pleased, be a killer (or of ruining disposition), and
will do sinful acts.
(b) The conjunction of Jupiter and Rahu produces
Guru Chandala Yoga which denotes that the person
will commit a hundred sins.

3080. Jupiter in Aquarius: Should Jupiter be in

of children to the native. Scorpio should not be

centiped sign in the zodiac. Canceris another watery

जन्माग्निषटसप्तगतश्च TE: |
वेदागििष्टस्थबुधोऽपि चेवं
नवाद्विभावे शुभकृदुरुः स्यात् ॥२०८२ ॥

3082 - 3083, Benefic Places - General: The Sun
is auspicious in the 6th, 3rd and 10th houses. The
Moon 15 favourable in ascendant, 3rd, 6th and 7th
houses. The 4th, 3rd and 6th houses are beneficial for
Mercury. Jupiter is favourable in the 9th and 7th
houses. All the planets are favourable in the 11th
house. These apply to transits.
Notes: As for the Sun, the Sanskrit word “Dik” is
translated as the 10th instead of 8th. Actually the
directions are ten including skyward and earthward.
Otherwise these are only eight.
“Agni” means three, denoting three sacrificial
fires.
“Adri” means seven denoting seven hills. Other
terms are simple enough.
The hints given here apply in general to transits of
planets.
The generally favourable positions for planets in
transit with reference to the Moon could be found in
transit-chapters of standard textbooks.

3086 - 3089. Saturn’s Effects According to Others: If Saturn transits the natal ascendant, one will be
involved in quarrels and will incur grief. In the 2nd,
it will cause fear from enemies and destruction
wealth. One will enjoy food comforts, and happiness
of progeny and relatives with Saturn moving in the
3rd. Further, success in war (litigations etc.) and
various kinds of comforts and fulfillment of desires
will also materialise. The transit of Saturn in the 4th
will destroy friends, progeny and those that are dear
to the native. Loss of quadrupeds, lands, calling and
honour will follow. The 5th house having Saturn in
transit will cause wavering mind, and entanglement
in many (impracticable) thoughts (or plans). Destruction of enemies and great comforts will result 6th
house Saturn in transit. This planet moving in the 7th
will harm the native’s spouse, progeny and servants.
In the 8th destruction, enmity and vices will result.
Attachment to sinful deeds and destruction of fortunes and auspiciousness will be revealed by transit
Saturn in the 9th house. Obstructions to financial
gains and destruction of purpose will be brought
about by Saturn in the 10th. An intelligent astrologer
should declare such results for Saturn’s transit in
ascendant etc.
Notes: The concerned 11165 dealing with Saturn’s
transit in 11th and 12th from ascendant are missing,
as attributed to another source consulted by the
author. The deficit can be made up by sloka 3084
supra.

3090 - 3091. (a) Father: When Saturn transits the
9th from the Moon sign, predict loss of father. This
will apply to his aspect on the said 9th house as well,
or to the time of his conjunction with natal Sun. (In
coincidence with one of the above conditions), destruction of father will take place when Saturn attains
the decanate, Navamsa or Dwadasamsa ruled by the
Sun. Further transit Sun will also be in adverse
placements.
(b) When Saturn transits the sign represented by
the Navamsa position of the 12th lord counted from
the ascendant, quarrels and destruction of suns (progeny) will come to pass.
Notes: In the first instance, three clues regarding
the transit of Saturn in connection with fatality to
father are discussed. Saturn should move in or aspect
the 9th counted from the Moon (or ascendant), or he
should come to the sign occupied by the natal Sun
(representing father). This is not enough. Saturn
should simultaneously be in Leo’s decanate or Navamsa or Dwadasamsa. Leo Dwadasamsa is available
ineachsign. Leo decanate applies to the signs Aries,
Leo or Sagittarius. Leo Navamsa does not occur for

44. Chandra Kala Nadi
the signs Gemini, Libra and Aquarius.
The third condition is the Sun in undesirable
houses.
Only then death of father be predicted.
(b) See in which Navamsa the lord of the 12th is
placed at birth. The transit of Saturn in the sign
denoted by the said Navamsa will promote quarrels
and will endanger one’s progeny.

त्तद्धावाष्टमेशांशदृष्टिसंभवराशिषु ॥३०९२ ॥

THT योजयेत् कालवित्तभः॥२०९२ ॥
3092 - 3093, (a) When transit Saturn attains the
sign represented by the Navamsa position of the 4th
lord from natal ascendant, the person will have
quarrels with the king (or employer), and also difficulties.
(b) The Bhava concerned will incur destruction
when Saturn transits (1) the sign equal to the Navamsa position of the ruler of the 8th from the bhava
under consideration; (2) the sign that is in aspect to
the lord of the 8th from a given bhava.
(c) See if a Bhava lord attains more and more
divisions of the planet ruling the 8th therefrom. In
that case destruction of the original Bhava in question
will occur in the dasa of the (lord of) original Bhava.
Notes: (a) Difficulties and professional problems
will surface with Saturn’s movement in the sign
denoted by the Navamsa occupied by the lord of the
Ath at birth.
(b) Three clues are given in respect of transit
Saturn’s ability to destroy the indications ofa house.
Firstly, in the sign denoted by the Navamsa position
of such a planet which rules the 8th from a given
house, Example - the 9th lord rules the 8th from the
2nd. Hence the sign denoted by the Navamsa position
of the 9th lord will be the target of Saturn’s transit.
This will affect family happiness, financial progress,
eyes, education etc.
Secondly Saturn should transit such a sign that is
aspected by the loid of the 8th from a given Bhava.
Example: The 2nd rules the 8th from the 7th. Hence
Saturn moving in the sign denoted by the Navamsa
position of 2nd lord will affect spouse, partnership
efc.
(c) This part is translated after supplying certain
missing links. The ideas the lord ofa bhava should not
beget many divisions of the planet ruling the 8th
therefrom. Then the dasa of the original bhava lord
yield adverse effects. For example in case of Aries,
the 6th is ruled by Mercury. Mars rules the 8th from
Virgo. Mercury remaining in many of Martian
divisions will in Mercury dasa cause ill-health, loss
in litigations, enmity etc. - adverse effects concerming the 6th house.

position of wide presidentship (or headship). Goddess Lakshmi will shower Her blessings on him.
sub periods of the lords of the 6th, 8th or 12th in the
dasa of the Moon will also give same effects - enmity,
distress and quarrels.
(d) Saturn’s transit in the Moon sign, or in the sign
equal to Moon’s Navamsa at birth, will produce
danger from an inimical king (or powerful people).
Remedies as prescribed be sought. (Then) due to the
pleasure of kinsfolk (i.e. favourably disposed kinsfolk) enemies will turn into friends, and great happiness will come to pass.
(€) The native of Mudgara Nadiamsa, Gemini
ascendant, should donate an axe (as a remedy vide
“d” above) as a result of which the band of enemies
will be destroyed.
Notes: (a) Mars, the 6th lord, denotes thefts for
Gemini ascendant. His occupation of exaltation
Navamsa will lead to such losses.
(b) Royal enmity could be interpreted as enmity
with powerful people in connection with marriage.
Thus there will be stiff opposition to marriage if
Saturn and Jupiter are in Libra in the 5th, with
identical longitudes.
The original instructions contained in “c” to “e” are
also applicable to Gemini. These are clear and need
no further clarifications.

3134 - 31351/2. (a) When Saturn transits the sign
Scorpio, the native of Libra ascendant will develop
physical ineptitude or laziness.
(b) Should the lord of the ascendant be in the 6th
house while its lord is in the Sth in association with
the 8th lord, one will have diseased stomach.
(c) Should Venus be in the 7th from the ascendant with Saturn’s aspect or association, one will incur
phlegmatic disorders and illness caused duc to excessive heat. Further he will be troubled by impure
excretion and urinary disorders.
Notes: (a) This rule applies to Libra ascendant,
a sudden shift by the text from Gemini ascendant.
Satum moving inthe 2nd from Libra ascendant is said
to cause bodily inability.
(b) This is a general rule, not for Libra ascendant
alone. With the lord of the ascendant in the 6th while
the lords of 6th and 8th are in the 5th house, abdominal disorders are to be foretold.
(c) Phlegmatic disorders relate to lungs and
heart. Rectal and unnary disorders are also part of
Venus in the 7th linked to Saturn by aspect or
conjunction. This axiomis more effectively applicable to Libra ascendant, because of Saturn’s rulership
of 3rd (lungs) and 4th (heart). Venus rules the 8th
house in this case, and is the natural ruler of kidneys

(urine etc.) whose placement in Aries is bound to
create problems generated by excessive heat.

3143 ~ 31481/2. Jupiter Dasa - Libra Ascendant: The sub period of the Sun in the major period of
Jupiter will cause worries on account of royal sources.
Happiness will be restored later on. The sub period
of the Moon (in Jupiter dasa) will give a mix of good

relatives will be in trouble while the king’s family (?)

troubles while his daughter will beget married. There

Jupiter’s dasa will endanger his maternal uncle in

person will himselfenjoy great happiness. (Jeevamsa
Nadiyogena is not of proper composition, not amenable for translation.) Acquisition of a child in the
family, omaments and marriage function will also

without involving the Nadiamsa. A rough overview

50 Chandra Kala Nadi
is depicted below.
Venus' sub period - troubles to relatives.
Sun’s sub period - initial troubles from government which will soon be sorted out.
Moon’s sub period - indigestion, dysentery, enmity with relatives, danger to maternal relatives, and
distress to relatives in general. The subject will
receive a mix of good and bad, but be generally
happy.
Mars’ sub period - troubles to son, wedding of a
daughter (if of marniageable age).
Rahu’s sub period - troubles to wife.

3169. Aswini & Its Trines: Should a malefic be
in Aswini, or Makha or Moola, the period of Moon’s

3173 - 3176. (a) Jupiter in strength placed in the
12th, or in aspect thereto, denotes that the native of
Mudgara Nadiamsa willattain Heavens after quitting
his mortal coil.
(b) The lord of the 12th being related to Jupiter
by aspect orconjunctionand occupying the Navamsa
of the 7th lord’s dispositor, will also cause attainment
of Heavens due to the mercy of Lord Siva. Due to the
merits of present birth, he will next time have an
excellent rebirth. Later he will be freed from rebirths.
(c) Only those planets that are in Upachaya
houses will have full strength and can bestow health
and longevity.
Notes: Instructions noted in “a” and “b” are
clear.
(c) This is a general rule applicable to any
ascendant. Planets with strength in 3rd, 6th, 10th or
11th can promote one’s health and life span.
However based on my experience, a strong
planet in the 6th house 15 found to be a source of
misery for physical well-being.

3177 - 3180. According to Rudra Yamala: If
the Sun is in the Sth house in aspect to his son (i.c.
Saturn), donation of gingili cakes will cause relief
from abdominal disorders. The image should be
obtained made of gingili cakes, with two diamondstudded hands, three eyes and covered with red robes.
The Sun God should be worshipped as prescribed by
Sastras, before donating gingili cakes. This secret is
mentioned in Rudra Yamala. This will increase the
years of life span and will grant health and give good

Deva Keralam 53
fortunes.
Notes: There is some confusion in composition
of the relevant part of the text about the image. The
God concerned is not mentioned. Three eyes do not
denote the Sun-God. Could it be of Lord Siva?
The essence of the other portion is: The Sun in
the 5th associated with Saturn is capable of causing
dangerous abdominal disorders to the extent of curtailing life-span. This can be overcome by suitable
worship of the Sun God, and donation of gingili
cakes. Gingili comes under Saturn’s domain.

3200 - 3206. Jupiter’s 3rd Round of Transit:
For a native of Mudgara Nadiamsa, during the third

Deva Keralam 55
round of transit, Jupiter will give following effects: In
the 1st and 9th, he will give wealth. Inthe 11th - there
will be fear from enemies. Happiness, comforts and
auspiciousness will prevail during his transit in the
twelfth. When in the 8th he will cause birth of a son,
acquisition of jewels and loss of a boy for one of his
kinsfolk. Due to an afflicted Saturn in transit in.
Scorpio, royal enmity will come to pass. Later happiness, royal favour and wealth will be acquired.
When Jupiter comes to-the sign represented by the
Navamsa occupied by the 11th lord, the subject will
obtain invaluable articles, conveyances etc. When
Jupiter is in the 3rd house in transit, he will acquire
cows and clothes. Inthe 11th and 12th, the planet will
give more gains. In the 7th it will bestow rulership of
a region apart from a high degree of scholarship.
Again in the 3rd there will be some distress in general,
but auspicious results will mature during the Sun’s
transit in evil signs. Jupiter ina trine to 3rd will cause
unhappiness among relatives. In the 4th, he will give
happiness and birth of a son. Transiting in 5th, the
planet will cause great distress and involvement in
blameworthy deeds. Later happiness will prevail.
Notes: Jupiter in the 3rd round giving favourable
effects while in transit on the Moon sign is noteworthy. So also bad effects while transiting the 11th from
the natal Moon.
When Jupiter transits the 8th from the Moon, as
Saturn transits the 6th from the ascendant, will cause
professional debacles.
While moving inthe 11th fromthe Moon, Jupiter
is adverse in the third round; he will be auspicious
when he moves in the sign represented by the natal
Navamsa of the 11th lord from the ascendant, that is
Mars.
Even in the 12th from the Moon, transit Jupiter
will give gains.
In the 3rd from the Moon, the planet Jupiter will
give gains apart from distress. But he will prove
favourable during the adverse movements of Saturn.
Transiting in the 7th/11th, he will cause troubles
to relatives.
While Jupiter is in the 5th from the Moon, one
should protect himself from questionable deeds.

3207. Jupiter’s 4th Round of Transit: For a
native of Mudgara Nadiamsa, the 4th round of transit
Jupiter will cause following results. In the sign Pisces
and in the initial portion of Aries, he will cause
mental agony, and in Taurus distress of a great
magnitude.
Notes: In this round, the whole of Pisces will be
adverse for Jupiter’s transit. As for Aries, with his
advancing movements, he will become mild. But
again with transit Jupiter in Taurus in the 12th, the
person will be highly troubled.

The ascendant Leo should fall in Virgo Navamsa

3227 - 32291/2. Moon in Mohana Nadiamsa:
(a) The Moon in Mohana Nadiamsa and conjunct
Saturn will produce the following effects for a Leo
native. His parents will be long-lived. His father
would have two marriages. The person will gain great
fortunes in his old age and will enjoy comforts like a
king.
Mohana Nadiamsa may be referred to in the
notes under sloka 1599, Book I.
(b) The Moon in Mohana Nadiamsa (without
reference to Saturn) will give the undermentioned
results for one born in Leo ascendant. The person will
be devoted to serve Lord Siva. He will have three
paternal uncles endowed with long lives. According
to some it will be four. His maternal aunt’s daughter
will be endowed with sons, limited fortunes and
auspiciousness. After some interval she will lose her
brother, and will predecease her husband.
Also see sloka 3235.

3250 - 3253. Maternal Kinsfolk: So should be
decided about paternal and maternal uncles. If the
first dasa lord is a malefic, is conjunct the 10th lord

3254 - 3255. The Moon in Mohana Nadiamsa:
This position of the Moon will not cause any blemish
to paternal relatives, but some blemish in maternal
families. However, the native’s father will be distressed in the native’s first dasa. He himself will be
troubled in evil months (Sun’s adverse transit) in the
first dasa and in the sub period of the lord of the
ascendant (i.e. the Sun).

3263 - 32641/2. Sickness to Mother: When
Saturn transits the Rasi identical with the Navamsa
position of the 11th lord (Mercury), or the sign
aspected by the 11th lord, the native’s mother will
incur sickness. It will cause distress to mother and
evil to her kinsfolk.
शिवदांशकगे जीवे मोहनांशे निशाकरे ॥३२६५ ॥
तुधभुक्तो चिरायुष्यसरप्रजाबहुलाभवान् ।

3265 - 3266. Progeny: Fora Leo native, Jupiter
in Sivada Nadiamsa, and the Moon in Mohana
Nadiamsa will cause many long-lived daughters.
Male issues will be limited. He will have only one
wife (meaning combinations for remarriage will be
nullified).

3267 - 3268. Other Authorities: According to
other authonities, the native of Sivada Nadiamsa in
Leo will have long-living step-brothers. The person
may have two wives, virtuous sons and men-borne
conveyances. He will be dear to his father. His elder
brother will be destroyed in one of the sub periods,
viz. of the 8th lord, 2nd lord and 11th lord, during the
major period of the 6th lord.

3269 - 3270. Step-brother: During the sub
period of the 5th lord when Satur arrives in transit
the Rasi represented by the Navamsa position of the

Deva Keralam
Sth cord, the person’s step-brother will be troubled
(or endangered). This effect can also come to pass

Mercury sub period can alternatively trouble the

शुक्रान्ते सर्वदाये स्वपिता बहुभाग्यवान् |

3271. Venus Sub Period: In any dasa, the sub

तत्तद्धावाष्टमेशांशदशायां वा स्वभुक्तिषु।
तत्तद्धावप्रपीडादि योजयेत्कालवित्तमः॥२२७२ ॥
3272. General Clue: Take a Bhava. The dasa of
the lord of the 8th therefrom will adversely affect that
bhava in his very sub period.
Notes: This is a general rule. It is not restricted
to Leo alone.
The major period of the 8th lord from a given
Bhava will adversely affect the Bhava concerned.
While in some cases, this holds good in some it does
not. Sufficient skill is needed to apply this aphorism.
In case of Aries, the 3rd is Gemini. The 8th lord
therefrom is Saturn whose dasa can cause troubles to
one’s brother. For this ascendant, Sth house is Leo.
Jupiter is the 8th lord from Leo. His dasa affecting
progeny may not be that simple.
Similarly the reader can work out details for
other ascendants.

3273-3275. Saturn in the 3rd: For Leo ascendant, Saturn endowed with full strength in the 3rd
house in the company of the Moon will give three
(younger) brothers. Two sisters will be long lived.
After some time, mother will be destroyed. Children
born to him will not survive. To overcome this evil,
remedies be performed following which children
will be endowed longevity.

3277 - 3278. Capricorn Ascendant - Kalikz
Nadiamsa: The native of first half of this Nadiamsa
will be a Soodra, 2nd or 3rd issue of the family and
will be destructive for his older brother. His fathe1
will be present at the time of his birth. During birth,
the child will be entangled in umbilical cord.
Notes: The subject belonging to Kalika
Nadiamsa’s first half in Capricorn ascendant cannot
be the first issue of the family. He will be either the
2nd or 3rd issue only.
His older brother/s will die prematurely.
His birth will be with some complications. There
will be no such complication if the conditions laid
down in sloka 3285 are available.
Kalika Nadiamsa rules as under: from 14° 2410
14° 36' in movable signs; from 15° 24 to 15° 36' in
fixed signs; and from 29° 24' to 29° 36' in common
signs.

3281 - 3282. Second Half: The native of second
half of Kalika Nadiamsa, Capricorn ascendant, will
be born near a temple dedicated to Mother Kah, ina
dilapidated house, near the region of a river, with his
father not being on the spot. His father will be a
Mantrika (sorcerer), be libidinous, will wander in
many countries (or regions), will have a healthy and
sturdy body, but will be short of means of living.

3286. Maternal Uncle: Should the Moon be in
debility in Rasi as well as Navamsa while Saturn is in

effects. The Moon in this case will be in the 6th from
the ascendant lord, Saturn.

3289 - 32921/2. Infantile Mortality & Remedies: For birth in the first half of Kalika Nadiamsa,
the 8th lord in the ascendant in a benefic’s Navamsa
and in the company ofa benefic will cause Balarishta
or infantile mortality. For two or three weeks in the
first dasa, orin the firstsub period ofthe 2nd dasa, this
fear will exist. To overcome this, worship of animage
of the Moon god made of silver placed ona bell-metal
vessel and the image of Sun-God made of gold robed
with red apparels be done. Donations after worship
should be given away. Following these measures,
health and longevity will improve.

3297 - 3299. Second Half: (a) For a native of
second half of Kalika Nadiamsa, Capricorn ascendant, childhood will commence with happiness (without Balarishta). He will be endowed with brothers
and sisters and will have a difficult calling.
(b) If the dispositor of the 8th lord is exalted and
strong while Jupiter occupies ascendant with a benefic Navamsa, the subject will enjoy longevity.
(c) If the said Jupiter placed in the ascendant is
in a malefic’s Navamsa, life-span will be limited.
Notes: (a) Professional upheavals are indicated
in this case.
(b) The Sun’s dispositor should be exalted. Jupiter though in debility in the ascendant should be in
a benefic’s Navamsa. With these two conditions lifespan will be long.
(c) Short life will come to pass if Jupiter in
Capricorn ascendant is in a Navamsa of a malefic
planet.

3302 - 33041/2. Paralysis & Breathing Disorders: Note if birth is in the former half of Kalika
Nadiamsa in Capricorn ascendant. In that case Jupiter should be in the ascendant aspecting the luminaries (the Sun and the Moon). The Sun should be in
Halaahala Shashtiamsa. This yoga will cause the
following effects. The person will possess a bloodred and symmetric body, broad eyes and a charming
nose. During childhood, he will suffer from breathing
disorders and paralysis. In the 2nd year he will be
greatly in danger. After 3rd or Sth year he will be
happy with a strong physique with sound health.
Notes: There is no Shashtiamsa as Halaahala
(great destructive poison emerged at the time of
churning Milk Ocean by gods and demons, and
swallowed by Lord Siva). There is however Garala
(poison) Shashtiamsa which extends from 4 deg ()' to
4 deg 30' of odd signs, and from 25 deg 30' to 26 deg
0' of even signs. Perhaps our author used this synonym.

3305 - 3309. Saturn & Mars: Saturn in own
sign, own Navamisa or in exaltation in aspect to Mars
will cause a happy childhood. The person will be
happy after eight, in particular. He will eat limited
food, will be given to enjoyment of pleasures, be
charitable, famous, devoted to Lord Siva, be skillful
in writing and learning, be fond of Panchakshari
Mantra, be disposed to sing the praise of the Lord, and
be honourable. He will be attached to Lord Siva, be
charitable, be helpful to others, be passionate and will
be more intelligent than his father. He will have two
maternal uncles and two paternal uncles and be
attached to latter two. He will have a paternal aunt
who will have a long-living husband and be auspicious for the family.

3311 - 3316. Jupiter in the 7th: For birth in first

Deva Keralam 65
half of Kalika Nadiamsa, Capricorn ascendant, Jupiter in the 7th will give following effects. Three of the
native’s paternal uncles will be long-lived. One of
their spouses will be barren and the uncle concerned
will remarry and obtain a son. Through the good
offices of a paternal uncle, the subject will earn
honours in royal circles. The native will be inwardly
cunning, passionate, miserly and virtuous. He will be
helpful to others and honour Brahmins and gods. He
will have three brothers, two wives, good children
and horse-drawn conveyances. He will incur enmity
with brothers after some interval and undergo a
family division and distress thereof. During his childhood, a paternal uncle will beget a son. The uncle in
question will be initially grieved (on other counts) but
be later on happy.

3322 - 3324. Saturn & Jupiter: Saturn in Libra
while Jupiter is in Vargottama in Cancer in the 7th
will make the native charming among his family
members. He will be skillful, learned (Vipra denotes

fond of jokes, will have constantly evil designs in
mind and will perform such acts that will please his
men. He will be an expert in mathematics and will be
in the service of (or will be associated with) mean
men. He will have lordship over lands and villages.
His prosperity will be ina foreign land. He will bean
achiever.

3328 - 3331. Three Planets in the 3rd: In case
of birth in Kalika Nadiamsa, Capricorn ascendant,
three planets located in the 3rd house indicate the
following. The native will have 3, 4, or 5 brothers.
One sister will have long-living husband. The middle
brother will be destroyed. Mother will be long-lived
but she will be emaciated due to loss of her brother.
According to other authorities, the native’s mother
will be troubled by sickness. The subject will be
always in grief, and be troubled by fever. His paternal
` and maternal grand-fathers will be destroyed. Due to
death of his maternal uncle, his mother will be greatly
in grief.

3333 - 3339. Family Lineage: One born in the
second half of Kalika Nadiamsa will not have his
lineage grow (i.e. will be childless). The Sun in
Halahala Shashtiamsa will help the paternal uncle of
the native of former half Kalika Nadiamsa grow his
family. Further the native will enjoy many pleasures
after 22 years ofage. The king of his country will live
10112) The native through his paternal uncle will
enjoy comforts of many women and children(?) His
prosperity will increase after 27 and he will be much
happy. He will be learned, will be ingenious, knowledgeable about Siva Purana. His marriage will occur
in the 25th year. His 16th year will endanger his
father. The sub period of a malefic in the dasa of 8th
lord from the Moon could (also) endanger the father.
(Or) When Saturn arrives at the sign represented by
the Navamsa occupied by the 4th lord, father will be
destroyed. Saturn moving in the 8th sign will cause
sickness to the native’s paternal uncle. In the absence
of this effect, the native will incur royal enmity in his
first dasa. Later he will recover health and be happy.
In the junction period of first and second major
periods, he will be distressed.

3366 ~ 33671/2. Sun - Mercury Conjunction:
For birth in Sama Nadiamsa, if the Sun is in the 10th
house, joining at the same time Mercury in a dual
Navamsa, the person wil] be given to anger and be
pleased instantly; he will have two wives and be
passionate. Instead of dual Navamsa, if the Sun so
placed joins Mercury in Taurus Navamsa, there will
be two long-living sisters and a long-living older
brother. The two sisters will enjoy abundant prosperity.
Notes: The Sun in this case in 8 dual Navamsa
denotes two wives to the native, while in Taurus
Navamsa two sisters are indicated, instead of two
wives. Thus the Sun in Taurus eliminates the possibility of a second marriage. A shrewd astrologer can
profitably understand different uses of Navamsa.
Also see sloka 3371 infra.

Deva Keralam
(Chandra Kala Nadi)
Part Two
by Venkatesa
अभिवन्द्य मतङ्खाख्यं श्रीमद्वडुटनायकम्।
काश्यपो वेद्भटेशोऽहं फलं वक्ष्यामि भूभुजाम् ॥१॥
1. I, Venkatesa, belonging to Kasyapa 6018,
respectfully salute Lord Venkateswara, the Glorious, and narrate effects (of astrological combinations) for the (pleasure of the) king.
Notes: For the part from sloka 1 above till the
end, 1.६. sloka No.4775 appearing hereafter, the
author's name is givenas Venkatesa. The name of the
author in the earlier portion is noted as Achyuta. We
do not know whether it is the same author with a
different name, or an entirely different author. But
the language used in the original seems to be from one
and the same person.
In the present context, the Gorra of the author is
given as Kasyapa and hence he was a descendant of
sage Kasyapa.
[Kasyapa was a mythical sage, husband of Aditi
and twelve other daughters of Daksha. By Aditi, he
was father of Adityas and of Maha Vishnu. By his
other twelve wives, he was father of demons, Nagas,
reptiles, birds and all kinds of living things. He was
one of the seven Great Rishis (Sapta Rishis) and 11651
of Parasu Rama and Lord Sri Rama Chandra.]
The author Venkatesa was apparently enthused
bysome royal patronage to compile the present work.
He should not be confuséd with Sarvartha
Chintamani's author, Venkatesa Daivagna, who in
fact belonged to Bharadwaja Gotra.
Lord Venkatesa invoked by our author is an
incarnation of Lord Vishnu and resides on Spatagins
the Seven Hills, in Tirupati

देवज्ञो विजितक्रोधः सत्यवाननसुयकः॥३ ॥

be passed on to wicked students, nor to those that are
childless or fickle-minded. The astrologershould be
one who has won anger, is truthful and is nd of

5 - 91/2. Note: The concept given here is not full
and thus not clear. Unless relevant sutras are supplied, the meaning is difficult to grasp. The author
quite briefly introduces Jaimini's school.
From Sri I. Rangacharya's Jaimini Sutramritam
necessary quotations and clarifications for this part of
slokas help our job.
Firstly the relevant original portion is fully reproduced below as our author's Sutras are incomplete.
"Chakram Chaturvidham gneyam aadyam
audayikam smritham /
Dwiteeyam thu _—prakrithaakhyam
vikruthaakhyam truteeyakam |
Graha chakramchaturthakhyam evam chakrasya
nirnayah |
Bhaavaa dwaadasa lagnaadyaah krama
maudayikam bhaveth /
Yatra krama vyutkramaabhyaam gananam
thvojha yugmayoh /
Prakrutyaakhyam hitachchakram yatra
yugmauyjha yosthathaah |
Gananam tadvikruthaakhyam
chakramathochyate /
Lagnaadheesam samaarabhya choththaroththarathah sthithaan |
Gnaatwa grahaan chara vasaadraaseen chakra
kramenavai |
Grahaa evam hi bhaavaah syuh lagna vittadi
sangnitaah /
Evam navasu bhageshu vinyasya navasah
grahaan /
karma sthaane apilagnesa nyasya laabhedhana
sthitham |
Vyayethu shahsta rasistham vinyasyokta
vidhaanatah |
Graha chakramitham proktham phala gnanaaya
surtbhih // ^
graha
This relates to computation of Jaimini Dasa and
is a general yardstick for the various kinds of special
Dasas enunciated by the sage. For odd signs, viz.
Aries, Gemini, Leo, Libra, Sagittarius and Aquarius
the counting is in the straight ascending order. For
even signs, viz. Taurus, Cancer, Virgo, Scorpio,
Capricorn and Pisces, the counting is to be reversed.
This method of counting, that is in straight order for
odd signs and in reverse order णि even signs, is called
Prakrutt Chakra. |
Sometimes a converse method is to be adopted.
To wit, the counting is in the reverse order in case of
odd signs, and in straight order in case of even signs.
This is called Vikruti Chakra.
Whether odd sign or even sign, the counting is
universally in straight order. This is called Audayika
Chakra.
The 4th one is Graha Chakra, This is to be
counted, in a straight manner as per the rules prescribed, from the sign occupied by the lord of the
ascendant. The next planet inthe Rasichartis the 2nd
Bhava, its next is the 3rd Bhava. This way after
exhausting the 9 Bhavas with 9 planets, the lord of
the ascendant himself becomes the 10th bhava, the
2nd again becomes the 11th bhava, and the sixth
becomes the 12th Bhava.
From the Rasi of the Dasa concerned, the number
of signs counted up to the sign occupied by the lord
of the Dasa Rasi, minus one, will be the number of
years of the dasa. The Dasa years will never exceed
12. If the dasa lord is in the 7th from the dasa Rasi,
consider 10 years. The dasa years divided by twelve
will yield the durations of the various Buktis. According the Rasi being odd or even, the distribution
should be in direct or reverse order.

10 -22. Longevity Computation: One's longevity should be found based on:
(a) lords of ascendant and the 8th;
(b) ascendant and the Moon;
(c) ascendant and Hora ascendant.

76 Chandra Kala Nadi
In case of mutual contradiction, the span given
by the pair of natal ascendant (Janma Lagna) and
Hora ascendant (Hora Lagna) should only be considered. The duration of day or night, as the case may
be, should be divided by 12 and the resultant number
should be counted from natal ascendant in direct or
reverse manner according to its being odd or even
signto get Hora Lagna. Two ghatikas anda half make
for one Hora Lagna. The ghatikas elapsed from the
time of sun rise orsunset to birth be distributed @ 2.5
ghatikas per sign from the ascendant in direct or
reverse manner depending on the ascending sign
being odd or even.
Should the lords of the ascendant and the 8th be
in movable signs, the life span will be full. So also
when the ascendant lord is in a fixed sign while the
8th lord is ina common sign, or the 8th lord ina fixed
sign while the lord of the ascendant is in a dual sign.
Life span will be medium if the two are in dual
signs, or one in fixed sign and the other ina movable
sign.
Short life will result if both of them are in
immovable signs, or one in movable sign and the
other in a common sign.
If these (lords ofascendant and 8th) are placed in
the initial portion, life span will be full. In the
conclusive portions of the signs concerned, these
minimise their contributions. If placed in the middle
portions, the contribution should be ascertained by
rule of three process.
One hundred years of life span is full. 66 yrs of
life span is medium. Short life is for 33 years. In
between the classification should suitably be understood.
Another Method: The ascendant is Dharma.
The 2nd is Artha, The 3rd is Kama. And the 4th is
Moksha. Their respective trines are also known by
similar names (and are called Dharma Artha Kama
Moksha Trikonas). If the Ayur Lagna (explained
below) is in Dharma Rasi as Chaturthamsa Rasi is in
a Moksha Rasi, full length of life will be granted,
When the Ayur Lagna is in Dharma Rasi while
Chaturthamsa Rasi is ina Kama Rasi, life span will
be medium. The Ayur Lagna and Chaturthamsa Rasi
failing in Dharma and Artha Rasis in that order will
give short life. Thatis if Chaturthamsa Rasi is in the
4th from Ayur Lagna , full life will come to pass; in
the 3rd medium life span, and in the 2nd short life be
predicted. Should the Ayur Lagna and Chaturthamsa
Rasi fall-in the same kind of sign of triad, 1.6. both
being Dharma, or Artha, or Kama, or Moksha, the
length of the life will be the least.
Ayur Lagna: Note the Navamsa occupied by the
Moon in a horoscope. Commencing from there
distribute five gahtis persign till birth ghatis from the
Sun rise are exhausted. The final sign arrived by this
count is called Ayur Lagna.
Chaturthamsa Rasi: 1.25 ghatis make up for for
one Chaturthamsa Rasi. The remainder obtained in
the process of Ayur Lagna evaluation should be
divided by 4, and distributed at the rate of 1.25 ghati
per sign commencing from the Navamsa of the Moon
to get the designated Rasi.
The sign occupied by the lord of the 8th is also
called as Hora Lgana and the one occupied by the lord
of the ascendant is Rasi Lagna. If the lord of the
ascendant becomes Karaka for longevity, the computation will be specially revealing. Otherwise, the sign
occupied by the lord of the 8th be considered as
instructed earlier.
Counting of 8th House - Fixed Signs: The 8th
house concerned for the four fixed signs are - Gemini
for Taurus; Sagittarius for Scorpio; Virgo for Leo;
and Pisces for Aquarius. That is the 8th house for
birth ina fixed sign is counted from the oppositesign
thereof in a direct manner.
Destruction of A Bhava: If the dispositor of a
Bhava is in the 6th, 8th or 12th counted from the
ascendant, the original Bhava in question is destroyed, .
The Three Khandas: The foursigns commencing from the ascendant form first Khanda; the next
four the 2nd Khanda, and the last four the 3rd
Khanda. The respective Khandas occupied by benefic or exalted planets will be congenial for the
incarnation ofan emperor. For others, the consideration should be according to the horoscope concerned.
Malefics or planets in debility in these Khandas will
cause difficulties.
Aroodha Lagna: Note the number of signs the
lord of a Bhava is away from the Bhava in question.
Count so many signs from the lord concerned to get

Deva Keralam 77
its Aroodha. In case of odd signs the counting is from
that very sign. Ifeven sign is involved, the counting
is from the opposite sign.
Notes: In these slokas, firstly the well-known
principle of life span being long, medium or short is
incompletely conveyed in the first instance. In the
second instance calculation of Savayana Hora Lagna
is prescribed.
(a) Use of lords of ascendant and 8th based on
Jaimini's Sutra "Aayuh pitru dinesaabhyaam": If
both these lords are in movable sign/s, long life is
indicated. If one is ina fixed sign while the other is
in a dual sign, then,again full life span will come to
pass. Medium life span will result if one is in a
movable sign and the other is inan immovable sign.
Short life is denoted by both these planets being in
immovable signs. The same is the case when one of
them is ina movable sign and the otherina dual sign.
(b) Use of ascendant and Moon sign should also
be done with the same guidelines, replacing the pair
of planets at "a".
(c) Again with the help of Janma Lagna (natal
ascendant) and Hora Lagna, similar assessment be
made.
If the indications as per the three methods are
identical, itshould be accepted as final. Should there
be contradictions, the indication given by the pair of
Janma Lagna and Hora Lagna 15 alone final.
(There is a school of thought that if two pairs
yield identical hints, that alone be considered.)
The method of Hora Lagna calculation given by
our text is used to find out what is called Saavayava
Hora Lagna.
Other matters discussed in the slokas are clear.

23 - 24. Matta Matanga Yoga: The lords of
angles and trines should be in their own signs, or in .
a sign that exalts the lord of the ascendant, or should
be in angles/trines (i.e. angular lords in trines and
trinal lords in angles). Then the yoga produced goes
under the name of Matta Matanga Yoga. One bornin
मरत्तमातद्गयोगस्य लग्नेशे रिपुनीचगे |
Ws स्यात्तत्रजः श्रीमानयुताधिकवित्तवान् ॥२५॥
25. The said Matta Maatanga Yoga will be
nullified if the lord of the ascendant occupies an
inimical sign or the sign of his debility. One born in
the said yoga (when in tact) will be wealthy owning
limitless sums.
सवोचचेसव्ष्रकद्रे बलवति भृगुजे मालवीसंज्कोऽसौ
