26. Malaviya Yoga: Venus in strength in an
angle identical with his own signor signof exaltation
will cause what is called Malaviya Yoga. The person
born in this yoga will be the lord of a number of
elephants, horses, chariots and palanquins, apart
from lands, forts and the like. He will be an ocean of
numerous virtues, be endowed with an excellent
wife, attached to other women, will win over the lord
of men (i.e. the king) and will enjoy pleasures.
Notes: Malaviya yoga is formed in the following
ways.
Venus in Pisces in case of Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius and Pisces ascendants (dual signs).
Venus in Libra in case of Aries, Cancer, Libra
and Capricorn ascendants (movable signs).
Venus in Taurus in case of Taurus, Leo, Scorpio
and Aquarius ascendants (immovable signs).
Thus al] the 12 ascendants enjoy this yoga with
Venus in a specified place.
It comes to our notice, in the course of our
studies, that Venus in debilitation Navamsa is greatly
crippled of its possible superior role through Malaviya
Yoga. So is again the case if he joins Mercury closely
in Pisces, oris combust in any of the signs concerned.
In case of the yoga formed by exalted Venus, his
dispositor, Jupiter, should not be in the company of
Saturn. If that be the case, the person will be in the
receiving end, instead of enjoying Maha Purusha
Yoga effects. (Refer to sloka 27 infra.)

78 Chandra Kala Nadi
When Venus in Libra or Taurus forms this yoga,
the question of dispositor does not arise. Here
Saturn’s influence onJupiterdoes notspoil the Yoga.
As per our text, Malaviya Yoga gives rich conveyances, landed and housing properties, association
with royal (or powerful) men and enjoyment of
pleasures.
Other effects stated elsewhere are as under. The
person with this yoga will have thin lips. He will have
wheatish complexion, thin waist, and will shine like
the moon in appearance. His voice in respect of
majesty will be comparable to an elephant’s voice.
His teeth will be white. He will obtain rulership of
certain kingdoms. His life span will be 70 and death
will be in a shrine.
His earnings will be by his own efforts.
जीवे शनियुते दृष्टे माक्वीयोगभङ्गवान् |

27. Cancellation: In the process of the above
yoga being present, Jupiter joining Saturn will nullify
the yoga whereupon the exaltation (note the term
“exaltation”) effects of Venus will go in vain. And
the person will be troubled due to planetary blemishes.
Notes: It is to be noted that Malaviya yoga
caused by Venus being in Pisces in an angle will be
nullified by Jupiter’s association with Saturn. Thus
the underlying concept is Malaviya yoga caused by
Venus in Libra or Taurus, being angular, will remain
unaffected by Jupiter-Saturn conjunction.

31. Three planets in their respective own signs
will produce pleasures. Their being in the Navamsas
owned by Yogakarakas (significators of prosperity)
will give a comfortable life. Their weakness and
occupation of Navamsas owned by Avayoga Karakas

will make living difficult. Two planets in own signs
will (also) give pleasures; in the Navamsas of Yoga
Karakas, one will be blessed with Raja Yoga. Four
planets in exaltation signs will usher abject poverty.

32. (a) Ifa planet is debilitated in Rasi but begets
his exaltation Navamsa, the person will be rich and
will enjoy many pleasures. If the said debilitated
planet is in the Navamsa of a Yoga Karaka, birth in

Notes: (a) Richness and pleasures will follow a
planet that occupies its exaltation Navamsa though
debilitated in Rasi. Example: Venus inthe sign Virgo
with Pisces Navamsa.
Again, being in debility, if the planet occupies a
Navamsa owned by a favourable planet with reference to a given ascendant, one’s birth will be ina
prosperous family. Example: Cancer ascendant, the
Moon in debility in Scorpio, but with a Jupiterian
Navamsa. Advanced students will well realise,
Scorpio Navamsa, though ruled by Mars, the best
planet for Leo, is not a welcome position in this case.

33 - 34. Adhi Yoga: In the formation of
Chandradhi Yoga, should the Moon be endowed with
full rays, the person concerned will be the ruler of the
earth, be strong, will enjoy pleasures, will own
wealth, elephants etc. With benefics in the 7th, 6th
or (and) 8th from the Moon (orascendant, as “Tasmin”
reads as “Lagnam” in another version), Adhi Yoga 18
formed. As a result one will be an army chief,
minister or king and be endowed with wealth, comforts, might and vanquished enemies. He will be
long-lived and will be free from fear of illness.
Notes: The text refers only to Chandradhi Yoga.
Lagnadhiyoga being equally important and similar,
suitable addition in brackets is made by me.
Normally, commentators interpreted that
benefics (that is the three natural ones) in the 7th
produce an army chief, in the 6th - a minister, and in
the 8th - a king. One way they may be right. Then
the inference is that the 8th having the three benefics
produces the best Adhi yoga followed by 6th and 7th,
in that order.
Whether itis Lagnadhiyoga or Chandradhiyoga,
the Moon should have undiminished rays. In the first
line of sloka 33, we find the term “Poornamsabhaage”.
I have not tried to enrich the interpretation with some
new meanings. Removing redundant possibilities, 1
have given a practical interpretation.
Also see sloka 36 and others infra.

43. If all the planets occupy variously their own
signs, own Navamsas, friendly divisions, or be in
Deeptamsa (i.e. Deeptavastha), own decanates, angles, or be in Muditavastha, Moolatrikona positions,
or endowed with temporary strength (strength at the

Notes: Deeptavastha: A planet in his exaltation
Sign attains this Avastha or state. (Deeptha = blazing.)
Muditavastha: Being in a close friend’s sign.
Mudita denotes happiness; also knownas Pramudita.
These two Avasthas or states are among nine
suchstates relating to the positions of various planets.

Deva Keralam
Temporary strength = Strong in Shadbala or six
sources of strength. These are Sthaana bala (positional
strength), Digbala (directional strength), Kaala Bala
(temporal strength inclusive of Ayana Bala), Cheshta
Bala (motional strength), Naisargika Bala (natural
strength) and Drikbala (aspectual strength), For
details consult relevant standard text books describing this aspect.

48 - 51. Chaamara Yoga: (a) The lord of the 9th
in the company of the lord of the 4th and being in
exaltation while Venus is in the 5th from the ascendant, will cause Chaamara Yoga. The native of Aries
ascendant with this Yoga will enjoy prosperity (in
general) (and more so) after the age of sixteen. He
will be a king, or be rich and happy.
(b) In case of Chaamara Yoga occurring to
Cancer or Aries ascendant, with Venus inan angle or
a trine, there will be a “Raja Yoga”. The person
belonging to Chaamara Yoga willenjoy Yoga effects
fromthe age of 12. Butthe twelfth year will usher fear
from untimely death. If he survives this danger, long
life is assured.
Notes: (a) According to our text, the 9th lord
should be exalted and be with the lord of the 4th.
Venus should also be inthe 5th from ascendant. Then
the yoga in question forms.
Two more kinds of yoga with this name is
explained by Parasara thus.
First, the ascendant lord should be exalted in an
angle and be in aspect to Jupiter. Saturn, Mars, and
Mercury can be exalted this way; that is the ascendants concerned, according to this axiom, could be
Anes, Gemini, Virgo and Capricorn.
Parasara’s second type of yoga is two benefics
jointly should be in the ascendant, 7th, 9th or 10th.
The effects for either kind of Chaamara Yoga are:
royal status, royal honour, long life, scholarship,
eloquence and high rate of knowledge in all arts.
Mantreswara gives yet another situation for this
yoga. The ascendant should contain a benefic while
its lord be ina good house (Bhava) or in his exaltation
sign. The effects are long life, increasing prosperity,
fame, virtues and leadership.
Thus the Chaamara yoga is described in four
different ways by different authorities.

55 - 59, Lakshmi Yoyva: The Moon in her
exaltation sign with radiant and multiple rays, Mercury in the 7th, the Sun in the 6th, Venus in the Sth,
Jupiter in the ascendant, and Saturn with Mars in the
3rd - the native with this planetary array will enjoy
Rajya Lakshmi Yoga and will be the lord of the earth.
Further he will be a learned person, owning lands, be
dear to the daughters of the king, and will be ever
blessed with the mercy of Goddess Lakshmi. After
the age of 25, his prosperity will be on the rise;
particularly after 30. He will acquire huge wealth in
the 16th year which will also be a year of fear of
untimely death and his body will be grounded to the
earth in a war. If he survives he will live happily
without diseases and with a strong physique. In his
71st year or in 61st year, he will incur death without
fail.
Notes: Minus the position of the nodes, this
seems to be a specific horoscope taken by the author
froma Nadi work. Calling the entire yoga as Lakshmi
Yoga or Rajya Lakshmi yoga is just not that important.
अखण्डसप्राज्ययोगः -

60. Akhanda Samrajya Yoga: Out of the lords

Deva Keralam 83
of 11th, 10th and 2nd houses counted from the
ascendant, even if one is inan angle to the Moon, the
native will be the ruler of an undivided kingdom (i.e.
will be an emperor of large area). Simultaneously,
Jupitershould be the ruler of one of 2nd, 5th and 11th
houses counted from the ascendant.
Notes: Akhanda Samrajya Yoga giving rulership
of a vast united land is applicable to all the four
immovable ascendants (not tc Scorpio and Aquarius
alone as commonly taught by many writers, ancient
as wellas modern) if we carefully study the rulership
required for Jupiter. He can rule one of the threc
houses, viz. 2nd, Sth and 11th for Taurus, Leo,
Scorpio and Aquarius, For Taurus, he is the 1 1thlord.
For Leo he rules the Sth. For Scorpio he rules two
houses, viz. 2nd and Sth. For Aquarius, he is the lord
of 2nd and 11th.
Now, this yoga can form in one of the following
four manners.
(a) For Taurus ascendant, one among the three
planets, Mercury, Saturn and Jupiter should be in an
angle to the Moon.
(b) For Leo, one of the two planets, Mercury or
Venus should be in an angle to the Moon.
(c) For Scorpio, one of the three planets Jupiter,
Sun and Mercury should be in an angle to the Moon.
(d) For Aquarius, one of the two planets, Jupiter
and Mars should be in an angle to the Moon.
Akhanda Samrajya Yoga thus applies to neither
a movable ascendant nor a dual ascendant. That is,
Aries, Gemini, Cancer, Virgo, Libra, Sagittarius,
Capricomand Pisces do not obtain Akhanda Samrajya
Yoga.
लग्नाद् व्ययगते पापे स्थिरलग्नचतुष्टये ।
साग्राज्ययोगभ्गः स्याच्चद्धलग्नाष्टमे व्यये UGS
दुः खजीवी दुरात्मा वा किविदङ्तिकाखान् |
साप्राज्योगे दाद्द्िययोगहीनं प्रकल्पयेत् ॥६२ ॥
61 - 62. Cancellation of Akhanda Samrajya
Yoga: This yoga will stand nullified, if a malefic is
in the 8th or 12th from the immovable ascendant
(concerned), In that event (apart from not getting the
good effects) the native will live a grief-ridden life
and will have somewhat defective a physique. The
formation of a (true) Samrajya Yoga will nullify
other yogas causing penury.
Notes: When the said yoga is formed as persloka
60, there should be no maletic planet (Saturn, Mars,
Rahu, Ketu, weak Moon, ill-associated Mercury) in
the 8th or 12th from the said immovable ascendant.
The prohibition of a malefic in 8th or 12th should be
applied to only that immovable ascendant which
forms the basis of this Yoga. Looking fora maleficin
Sth/12th from all the four immovable ascendants will
be too much.
The Moon being weak if placed in the 12th or 8th
from the ascendant, while the other condition of this
yoga remains fulfilled, will nullify this yoga.
Samrajya Yoga is a short-term for Akhanda
Samrajya Yoga. As a result of this yoga, the person
will be wealthy and highly prosperous, if not literally
an emperor. This is a good yoga for occupying
prominent positions of national importance, political
or apolitical.

63 - 68. (a) Yogas With Reference to Moon:
Four different yogas named Uttama (excellent),
Madhyama (moderate), Adhama (mean) and

84 Chandra Kala Nadi
Adhamaadhama (the meanest) are produced (when
planets are in the 10th, 7th, 4th and the Ist with
reference to) the Moon. With the last mentioned
yoga, (other) yogas in the horoscope become weak;
ineffectiveness thereof should be understood with the
help of directional influences.
(b) Uttama Yoga: One born in this yoga will be
a Satvika, be intelligent, honourable, will have wealth
eamed by fair means, will take to good path and be
orthodox.
(c) Madhyama Yoga: This native will serve a
king, be predominantly passionate, will mdulge in
both good and bad acts, be educated and intelligent.
(d) Adhama Yoga: One born in this yoga will
ever besickly, be attached to performing unrighteous
deeds, libidinous, unprincipled and will live a
wretched life.
(e) Adhamaadhama Yoga: He will be inflicted
by शारा, libidinous, mean, be given to severe anger,
fond of promoting quarrels, ever sinful, attached to
the wicked, will have an eye on others’ food and their
spouses and be given to faulting others.
Notes: In a way it can be said that the original’s
spirit is not fully maintained in these six slokas for it
has been recomposed by a later composer without
having any regard to fullness of the conditions and
without giving heed to proper astrological terms.
First of all there is no clear reference to the four
houses involved. These have been supplied by me in
line with the effects enlisted. Further I have my own
doubts about effects given for Adhamaadhama Yoga.
The reader will do well to consider natural
benefics and malefics that form the yoga and take
only relevant principles.
I do not know how the reader will react to my
suggestion that benefics form the firsttwo mentioned
yogas while malefics form the penultimate and last
yoga.
Phala Deepika’s Uttama, Madhyama and
Adhama Yogas may also profitably be seen in its
sixth chapter.

69. Three Planets: With three planets in exaltation, one will be highly intelligent. If three are inown
signs, he will be a minister. A dull-witted person is
born with three planets in debility. He will have gnefridden livelihood if three are exalted but in degrees
causing an adverse yoga. If three planets are in
debility butare indegrees causing a favourable yoga,
one will enjoy prosperity and pleasures.

70-71. Maha Panditha Yoga (Yoga for Great
Scholarship): If the Sun and Mercury join ina sign
which is the ascendant and is the place of exaltation
for one of them, or if Jupiter (alone) is in the
ascendantin exaltation, the yoga called Maha Panditha
Yoga is produced. As a result, the person will be a
scholar, ruler of men and land, wealthy, will be
scientific (ormaintaina scriptural viewpoint), learned,
be a good speaker in the assembly of Brahmins, a
protector of secrets, and be wise and virtuous.
Notes: This yoga forms in three ways.
Firstly, the Sun and Mercury can be in Anes
ascendant, where the Sun is exalted.
Secondly, the Sun and Mercury can be in Virgo
ascendant, where Mercury is exalted.
Thirdly, Jupiter can be alone in Cancer rising.

72-78. Jupiter’s Navamsa Positions: (a)Should
Jupiter be in the 10th or 6th with benefic Navamsa,
-the person will be virtuous and highly intelligent.
(b) Jupiter in Sagittarius with Cancer Navamsa,
or in Capricorn with Leo Navamsa, or in Pisces with
Libra Navamsa will cause grief, and deprive the
native of learning and happiness.
(c) Jupiter in the 2nd house obtaining 8th Navamsa while the Moon is in Scorpio Navamsa will
from the age of sixteen cause the effects of “one
yoga”. (This part in sloka 74 is not clear, and is
defective in composition. The meaning is - it will
give effects of one yoga. Obviously this position of
Jupiter is mixed up with the Moon’s Navamsa. The
text wants apparently to suggest that Jupiterin the 8th
Navamsa, placed in the 2nd is not welcome, which
will be clear from “b” above and “d” below.)
(d) Avoiding the 8th Navamsa, Jupiter placed in
the 2nd will give happiness, education, sweet speech,
modesty and fondness for religious merits.
(£) Jupiter in the 2nd house or in the 8th house
with a benefic’s Navamsa will grant learning, cleverness in royal circles, and intelligence.
(f) Jupiter placed in an angle or ina trine, with
Pushkara Navamsa will bestow great prosperity. Ifin
the process the planet is weak, or is in 6th/8th/12th
sign from Navamsa ascendant, he will cause grief on
account of a son (or progeny) following the 21st or
31st year. The trinal signs from Aries to Pisces (i.e.
trinal signs counted from the ascendant) should similarly be estimated.
Notes: Here the text describes Jupiter in certain
Navamsas.
(a) Being in the 6th or 1Uth in Rasi, he should
occupy his own Navamsa, or that of Mercury/Venus.
He can also be in exaltation Navamsa if the Moon Is
full. As a result, the subject will be endowed with
Virtues and a high degree of intelligence.
(b) Jupiter in his Moolatrikona sign - Sagittarius
- (from 10° 0' to 13° 20') with exalted Navamsa is not
welcome for happiness and Icarning. So also his
position in the sign Pisces (13° 20' to 16° 40)’ with
Libra Navamsa, and the sign Capricorn - debilitation
sign - (23 deg 20 to 26 40') with Leo Navamsa.
Interestingly, Jupiter in the sign Cancer (23°20!
to 26° 40") with Aquarius Navamsa (the 8th Navamsa) is excluded. Obviously this does not stand in
the way of one’s learning and happiness unless the
Moon is in debility in Navamsa. Please see notes in
“ce” below.
See slokas 546 - 553 for effects of Jupiter in
Pisces with Libra Navamsa.
(©) Jupiterinthe 2nd house with eighth Navamsa
(23° 20' to 26° 40') while the Moon is in debilitation
Navamsa will from the age of 16 till the end cause
such effects as to lead to unhappiness and deprival of
learning. The mention of “one yoga” seems to be
wrong and the above interpretation makes sense
keeping the earlier sloka, No.73, in mind while the
8th Navamsa of Cancer (i.e. Aquarius Navamsa) is
not included. In the present context, the Moon in
specified Navamsa position is an additional requirement.
(d) Should Jupiter be in the 2nd (in any sign) he
should not be between 23° 20' and 26° 40'. Then he
wil] grant the specified results. That is, if Jupiter is
in the 2nd with eighth Navamsa, happiness, education, speech etc. will be affected adversely.
(£) If Jupiter in the 2nd/8th house gets his own
Navamsa or that of Mercury or Venus, or Cancer
(while the Moon is full in case of Jupiter in Cancer
Navamsa), the person will be well-educated, intelligent, clever enough to enter into high societies.
(f) For Pushkaramasa, see page 239 of Book 1,
Deva Keralam.

` 79, Pushkaramsa: If all the planets from Sun
onwards are in Pushkara Navamsas the person will
enjoy the blessings of Goddess Lakshmi (i.e. be
opulent), and will enjoy pleasures, fame and happiness.

80. The Sun’s Navamsa Positions: Short life
will come to pass if the Sun js in:
(a) the sign Gemini with Capricorn Navamsa.
(b) the sign Sagittarius with Cancer Navamsa.
(c) the sign Virgo with Aries Navamisa.
Notes: This is a general rule applicable to any
ascendant meaning the Sun in dual signs, excepting
Pisces, has a certain role to play in respect of one’s
life-span.
(a) The Sun in Gemini 10° 0 to 13° 20' getting
Capricorn Navamsa will reduce life-span.
(b) So does he when in Sagittarius 10° 0' to 13°
20' getting Cancer Navamsa.
(£) Again life will be short if the Sun is in Virgo
10° 0' to 13° 20' obtaining Aries Navamsa. Please
note Aries, though the exaltation Navamsa of the
Sun, is prohibited being 8th from Virgo.
In these cases, the Sun’s Navamsa positions will
be 8th counted from his sign positions.
Why the Sun in Pisces - another dual sign - with
Libra Navamsa is not added to the list merits special
attention while Aries Navamsa of Virgo is. Practical
horoscopic studies can give us suitable guidance.

81. Danger to Father et cetera: Should the Sun
be in the 12th house, or if Saturn is in Aswini, or in
the 7th or its trine, or in the 10th there will be danger
to father etc. in the childhood of the native.
Notes: Father ef cetera includes paternal relatives.
The position of the Sun and Saturn are discussed
in the present case.
The Sun in the 12th will cause danger to father
and others in the form of sickness, not necessarily
death. Bhavartha Ratnakara’s dictum that a
significator in the 12th contributes to the growth of
the indications concerned, thus, gets a limited exception.
Saturn in Aswini Nakshatra will cause the same
effect.
Again Saturn features inthe 3rd, 7th, 10th or 11th
in respect of sickness to the said relatives. From the
3rd and 7th, he aspects the 9th. The 11this the house
of some paternal relatives. Why the 10th comes into
play could be understood with some examination.
The 10th is the 2nd from the 9th which denotes
father’s original family before his marriage.
Of course the two rules involving the Sun and
Saturn are quite general in nature. In the concerned
places, they should not enjoy strength so as to be
adverse.

82. Evils to Father: If the Moon is in the 5th
house in aspect to Saturn and Mars, the first dasa of
the native will endanger the father. The relevant sub
period will be that of the Dasamsa dispositor of the
first dasa lord.
Notes: Regarding the sub period concerned, we
should verify the Dasamsa chart and find out the
dispositor of the lord of the first dasa. In case that sub
period had already expired before birth, the sub
period of the Moon, Mars or Saturn may be relevant
in the first dasa.
The importance, inthis yoga, is for father’s death
in the very first dasa itself.

83 - 85. Amala Yoga: (a) Ifthe 10th from lunar
ascendant, i.e, the Moon sign, is in occupation by a
benefic planet, the yoga going under the name Amala
Yoga is formed, giving indestructible and life-long
wealth to the person. The 2nd, 4th and 6th from the

Deva Keralam
Moon should be free from malefic occupation in this
yoga. So also the 9th sign in Navamsa.
(b) Jupiter (too) in the 10th house from the Moon
produces Amala Yoga. Inthe Amala Yoga caused by
Jupiter, if Venus aspects the ascendant, one will beget
abundant wealth, be famous and happy.
Notes: (a) Although it is a questionable practice
to find out Amala Yoga from the natal ascendant,
effective one is doubtlessly the one formed by a
benefic (viz. Jupiter, Mercury or Venus) in the 10th
from the Moon.
Two conditions are stipulated by the text which
are not found inothertexts. These are freedom forthe
2nd, 4th and - please note the word “and” - the 6th
from a malefic occupation, while the 9th from Navamsa ascendant should also be free from a malefic.
(Some may possibly interpret that all these four
places are in Navamsa chart.)
Parasara does not allow any other planet, 1.6. a
malefic, inthe 10th in the formation of this yoga. His
condition is described by the words “Kevalaischa
Subhairyuthe”, that is only benefic in the 10th, to the
exclusion ofa malefic. In the list of acquisitions, the
sage does not include wealth while Phala Deepika
does. Our text further enhances the value of this yoga
by the expression - indestructible and life-long wealth.
(b) Our text specially takes the Amala Yoga
caused by Jupiter in the 10th from the Moon in which
case the aspect of Venus on the ascendant causes
much wealth apart from fame and happiness.
Increased wealth, fame and happiness are additions to be caused by Venus in simultaneous aspect to
the ascendant.

86 -88. Neecha Bhanga Raja Yoga: If the Ras!
lord, or the planet that will be exalted in the sign
containing a debilitated planet, occupies an angle
with reference to the Moon, the subject will become
a king or munificent emperor. One with a planet that
has cancelled debility will become an emperor and be
greatly happy. His prosperity will be on the nse from
his 33rd or 35th year. In the dasa of such a planet,
other rulers will adore the feet of the native, and he
will be plentiful, and his fortunes will consist of gold
and precious stones.
Notes: Although this yoga is quite popular, some
of the effects mentioned here are noteworthy.
The angular position of the planet concerned is
only from the Moon, to the cxclusion of the natal
ascendant, as per our author.

89 - 91. Chatra Yoga: The Moon in the 2nd
house while Venus is in the 9th, 2nd, 3rd, 12th or 11th
from the ascendant will cause Chatra Yoga. The —
person concerned will be happy, eloquent in speech,
soft-bodied, rich with royal features, learned, intelligent, will have stirring appeal for women, be radiant
akin to the Sun, endowed with longevity, will become
skillful in worshipping Lord Siva after the age of
twenty, be well introduced in the study of various
Mantras and Agama Sastras, will obtain a son in the
old age, be wealthy, will attain fame through journeys and be worshipped by the king.
Notes: "Chatra" means umbrella, rather a traditional royal symbol.
While the Moon and Venus form Chatra yoga as
per our work, this yoga is treated as part of Nabhasa
yogas by Parasara, Varaha Mihira etc. According to

88 _ Chandra Kala Nadi
them all the (seven) planets should be in seven
continuous houses commencing the 7th house. The
results thereof are the person will be long-lived, be
happy in the beginning and ending parts of his life,
will help his kinsfolk, be a king and be close to many
kings (i.e. will be opulent etc.) and be intelligent.

92. Royal Favours: One will beget favours from
the emperor if Venus is in the Sth or in the 2nd while
his dispositor is strong.
Notes: “Rajapoojyamsage” does not convey any
sensible meaning. It simply means “in the Amsa or
degree of royal honour”.
Other confusing terms of this nature, viz.
Rajayogamsa (the degree of Raja Yoga) and
Rajanganamsa (the degree of royal woman) appear
infra.

93. The 2nd lord endowed with strength should
be inthe 7th house while the Moonis inthe 2nd house.
Mercury should be ina sign ruled by Mars (i.e. Aries
only). The planet Venus should be in Pisces in the
company of the Sun. All these existing at one and the
Same time will bless the native with longevity, wealth,
two wives, lordship over villages, scholarship, sacrificial disposition, pleasures and gaiety in an assembly.

94. The 8th lord in the 8th while Mercury is inthe
6th will give the following effects. The subject will
be ever anxious to receive charities, be blessed by the
goddess of beauty, be troubled by penury, will undergo numerous difficulties, will attain prosperity
later on, will have fluctuating prosperity, and be quite
famous in the world.
Notes: This is not a good yoga and gives many
adverse effects. On the positive side, the person will
be beautiful and quite famous. He will have belated
prosperity.
TAY व्ययषष्ठगे रिपुपतौ wt व्यये वा स्थिते
रिफिशेऽपि तथेव सध्ररिुगे यद्रा त्रयः स्वर्षगाः।

95. Evil Lords: The 8th lord in the 6th or 12th,
the 6th lord in the 8th or 12th, and the 12th lord in the
6th or 8th, or these three planets in their abovespecified own houses in mutual aspects only between
them (to the exclusion of other association) - this
yoga will make one a king, endowed with distinct
wealth and be praised by the king of kings.
Notes: The yoga simply requires evil lords in
evil houses.
In this context, Uttara Kalamrita’s much णण
lar Vipareeta Raja Yoga is to be noted.
Lord of 8th in 6th or 12th, lord of 6th in 8th or
12th, or lord of 12th in 6th or 8th or the said three evil
lords connected in any way by exchange, aspect or
conjunction, and simultaneously having no such
relationship with other planets - produces Vipareeta
Raja Yoga. The formation of this yoga is not that
simple; so also its effects. The person concerned will
be “the king of kings”. He will have fame and wealth.
Mahadeva in his Jataka Tatva lays down a
simple rule to the effect that the 8th lord and the 6th
lord interchanging places, or being angular, produce
a mighty and powerful person. This aphorism of
Mahadeva is somewhat akin to sloka 96 infra.

96. Evil Lords Related: Should (two of) the
lords of 6th, 8th and 12th mutually aspect each other
or should these three planets be in one another such

97. Jupiter & Venus: IfJupiterisinRajayogamsa
(?) while Venus is in the 2nd or 8th, the person will
be lustful, charitable and will indulge in wicked (or
questionable) deeds.
Note: Rajayogamsa is not a standard term.
षष्ठाष्टमाधीशयोगे त्वसितेन युतेक्षिते |
अल्पायुर्दायमाप्नोति सर्वनाडीफलं वदेत् ॥९८ ॥
98. Short Life: The lords of the 6th and 8th
joining together in aspect to, or in association with,
Saturn will reduce life-span.
Notes: The 8th lord alone related to Saturn gives
longevity. It is only when the 8th lord joins the 6th
lord and gets related to Saturn, that life span will
receive a reduction. Thus the culprit is the 6th lord.

99, Yoga fora Paramour: If Venus is inthe 7th
ora trine thereof in aspect to a malefic, the subject
will be the paramour of a married woman and will
indulge in buying females for his livelihood.

100. Favoured by a Princess; The two planets,
Moonand Venus, joining in the 10th ora trine thereof
denotes that the subject will live through the favour
of a king’s daughter. He will enjoy pleasures, be
lustful and will always be happy.
Notes: One with the Moonand Venus in the 2nd,
6th or the 10th will receive help from women of high

102 - 103. Rajapooja Yoga: If the 9th lord is in
exaltation with exceeding strength, and in aspect to
the Moon, while an angle or trine from the ascendant
is occupied by Mercury, the yoga called Rajapooja
Yoga is produced. One born in this yoga will have
even limbs, will have wealth exceeding ten thousand
(gold coins), (i.e. be very rich), will have no brothers,
be famous, long-lived and will be endowed withsons.
Notes: The name suggested should be Rajapoojya
Yoga instead of Rajapooja Yoga. However royal
honour - true to the name - is not part of the effects,
which are otherwise quite interesting.
“Exceeding strength” refers to a higher number
of units in Shadbala.
Having no brothers (thus becoming the sole
inheritor of ancestral properties) and begetting male
offspring may be noted with special emphasis.

104. Should the lord of the 1st house be in
exaltation or be in Vargottama Navamsa, and be in
aspect to Venus, the native will have self-realisation
through the school of Non-dualilty, be devoted to
Vedantha doctrines, and be a knower of the Almighty.

90 Chandra Kala Nadi
Notes: Advaita Philosophy = Identity of Brahma
(supreme soul) with Jeevatman (human soul).
Vedantha = complete knowledge of the Vedas,
the second and the most important part of Mimamsa,
or third of the three great divisions of Hindu philosophy.
An average reader not properly initiated into
astrology may run the risk of involving the planet
Venus ina lower level of material enjoyments. The
higher plane of Venus should never be missed, as he
is noted to be the able preceptor of demons knowing
many things that were not known even to Deva Guru
or Jupiter. Venus or Bhrigu is said to be the exclusive
knower of imparting life into the dead, known as
Mrita Sanjivani Mantra. He is an unparalleled expert
in Muhburta.

Navamsa Lagna. The 9th house denotes Vignana or
scientific temper and achievements of a person. We
may not always be nght in taking out such meanings
for such terms, because some times they seem to be
wanting in proper editing in the original.
The following slokas containing such unusual
terms,for which we have no clear clues, will confirm
our doubts.

मणिप्रवाठयोगः -
उच्च द्वितीये सोम्ये वा पञ्चमे वा तथा शुभे ॥१२६॥
अष्टमे च तथा पापे नवमे च तथा YA
श्रीमन्तः पुरुषा लोके विपरीते विपर्ययः,
मणिप्रवाक्योगोऽयं तत्र राजा भवेन्नरः ॥१२७॥
126 - 127. Mani Pravala Yoga: (a) This yoga is
formed in one of the following three manners:
(1) A benefic exalted in the 2nd or the 3rd while
a malefic is in debility.
(2) A benefic exalted in the 4th or in the 5th.
(3) A malefic exalted in the 8th while a benefic
is so placed in the 9th.
As a result, the person will be wealthy and be a
king.
(b) A contrary situation will produce opposite
effects, that is the subject will be poor.
Notes: (a) Some may prefer to interpret that all
these three conditions should be simultaneously
present to form Mani Pravala Yoga. The condition
“Vipareethe Viparyayah” (vide condition at “b”
above) does not allow that interpretation as a contrary
situation is impossible to exist.
(1) The 2nd house or the 3rd house from the
Ascendant should have an exalted planet while a
malefic planet should be in debility anywhere. Thus,
two planets feature in this condition.
(2) Here only one planet features - that is the 4th
or the 5th should have an exalted planet.
(3) Here two exalted planets feature - benefic
exalted in the 9thand malefic exalted in the 8th house
from the Ascendant.
In any of the above cases, the subject will enjoy
a royal status with wealth and paraphernalia.
(b) Contrary: Now, a contrary situation in each
of the above three individual conditions should be
drawn thus:
(i) Contrary condition for “a1” above is: The 2nd
or the 3rd having a debilitated planet while a malefic
is exalted anywhere. This will deprive one of wealth
and status. In other words, in a horoscope where a
malefic planet is exalted, the 2nd or3rd should be free
from occupation by a debilitated planet.
(ii) Contrary for “a2” above ts: the 4th or 5th
from the Ascendant should have a debilitated planet
as a result of which one will be poor, without any
mentionable status.
(111) Contrary for“a3” above is: 8th house having
a debilitated malefic planet while the 9th house has
a debilitated benefic planet. This native will also be
in the grip of poverty and will have no important
position in life.

शुक्रे भोगी भानुजे शोकतप्तः॥१२८ ॥

129 - 130. Good Features, Long Life et cetera:
(a) A strong benefic in the Ascendant while maletics
are devoid of strength, will produce a person complete with pleasingly excellent and auspicious features (befitting physiognomically favourable descriptions). Further, he will be long-lived, worshipped
by the king and be happy. In this yoga, the Ascendant
should however be a benefic’s sign.
(b) The Moon in the Ascendant aspected by her
dispositor produces one who will be wealthy, whose
intelligence will be as sharp as the point of Kusa
grass, and who will be of a famous lineage.
Notes: (a) If malefics placed anywhere enjoy
strength, they will prove an obstacle for the benefic
in the ascendant in delivering the results suggested.
Comparatively malefics should be stripped of their
vigour. If malefics are stronger than the benefic inthe
ascendant, opposite effects will come to pass. If the
ascendant is ruled by a natural benefic, the advantages will be sure.
Ifthe sign ascending is a malefic, the disqualification can be offset by more than one benefic therein,
i.e. two or more benefics placed in the malefic sign
ascending; not that those who are born in Leo, Anes,
Capricorn etc. will be backward in these respects.
(b) This is acommonly found yoga and needs no
detailed explanation.

131 - 133. (a) A malefic in the ascendant will
make one sickly, weak and troubled by enemies.
(b) A malefic in the ascendant in aspect to the
lord of the 8th house will cause penury and sickness.
(c) If the aspecting planet in the above case is the
Ath lord, instead of 8th lord, one will be happy.
(d) If the aspecting planet in the above case is the
6th lord, poverty will result.
(€) Saturn in the Sth or in the 9th house, with a
Navamsa which denotes barrenness (i.e. Leo Navamsa) will make the native marry a barren woman.
Alternatively, he may beget dead children.
(f) The 7th lord ina Navamsa owned bya eunuch
planet while the 4th house is occupied by Rahu, and
the 2nd by Saturn - this yoga will destroy two or three
children.
Notes: (a) If the occupant of the ascendant is a
major planet, being malcfic, like Saturn, Rabu, or
Ketu, sickness and inimical troubles will be perennial, and will not end soon. A benefic highly afflicted
wil] also give similar effects.
(b) This can affect the length of the life as well.
The person will be in perpetual sorrow.
(c) A malefic in the ascendant in aspect to the
lord of the 4th is said to give happiness. Perhaps it is
because the lord of the 4th holds key to one’s general
happiness. That way Venus will also do thesame job
like the 4th lord.
(d) The aspect of the 6th lord will further trouble
one with litigations, debts (different from poverty)
and incurable diseases.
(€) Classical texts describe Leo as barren. Practical experiences have generally not proved this
point.
(f) The 7th lord from the natal ascendant should
not be in Gemini, Virgo, Capricorn or Aquarius in
Navamsa. The 2nd from the ascendant denotes
fanuly. But the reader may be wondering why the 4th
house is involved in matters related to birth of
progeny. Although general text books like Jataka
Paryata, Saravalt, Parasara Hora, and Phala Deepika
have not referred to the 4th in respect of acquisition
of progeny, Nadi text books give importance to the
occupation of the 4th as well. Of course they have not

Deva Keralam 95
over-rated the 4th in preference to the 5th, nor did
they state that the 4th rules progeny.
For example see sloka 136 infra where the 4th
house is referred to in respect of children.

134 - 135. Concerning Wife: (a) Should one
have a malefic in the 7th house while the 7th lord is
conjunct another malefic, his wife will incur disorders of stomach and worm-infected womb. In the
absence of these, her conceivability will be in doubt.
(b) Ifthe 9th lord is inthe 7th house identical with
a sign owned by Jupiter, while the dispositor Jupiter
is in the 10th house with a malefic, the native will
marry a physically disabled woman.
Notes: (a) This is a simple yoga easy enough to
be understood. This yoga will cause obstacles in
begetting progeny. Else, the person’s spouse may
have abdominal disorders.
(b) The 7th house is ruled by Jupiter in case of
Gemini or Virgo rising. For Gemini, the 9th lord
Saturn in the 7th house while the 7th lord Jupiter is in
Pisces in the company of a malefic will cause marriage with a physically disabled girl. In case of Virgo,
the 9th lord Venus should be in Pisces, in exaltation
while Jupiter is in Gemini with a malefic to cause
marriage with a woman with physical defect.
There is also a rule that Venus in debility in the
7th will give a physically defective spouse.
पापान्तरे सुखे जीवे पञ्चमे वाल्पपुत्रवान्।
द्वितीये दारहानिः स्यान्मदे द्वित्रिकन्प्रवान् 1236 ॥
136. The 4th House: Ifthe 4th house is hemmed:
between malefics, Jupiter in the 5th will cause limited number of children. Instead of 5th, if Jupiter be
in the 2nd, he will harm the native’s wife. Lastly if he
be in the 7th, while the 4th is hemmed between
malefic,s the subject will have two or three marnages.
Notes: Three yogas are discussed here involving
the 4th house and Jupiter. In each yoga, the common
feature is that the fourth house should be hemmed
between malefics.
In the first place, Jupiter in 5th will cause limited
number of children. In the 2nd house, he will adversely affect the wife, normally will curtail her lifespan. In the 7th house, Jupiter will give two or three
marriages. All these, only when the 4th house has
malefics on both the sides. Otherwise, Jupiter alone
in such position should not be expected to give these
adverse results.

planet.
The above instructions at S.No.1 to 9 apply to
Kalachakra Dasa analysis, while 10 and 11 are as per
Vimsottan dasa.
A discerning reader can extend these suitably to
the various other ascendants.

endowed with progeny and grand-children, will possess eyes that are peculiar to women with eyelashes
resembling an arck, and will have a very beautiful
appearance. If this yoga is aspected bya malefic (than
Mars), questionable birth and sinful disposition will
come {0 pass.
Notes: The Yoga - Parijatha - applies irrespective of Nadiamsa. The yoga given hereof apparently
forms when the ascendant lord joins the lords of 2nd
and 10th in aspect to the lord of the 9th.
There is another kind of Parijatha Yoga given by
Jataka Parijatha of Vaidyanatha which could be
found in its chapter VII, sloka 156.
In our text, it is stated that Parijatha Yoga
aspected by a malefic denotes a questionable birth,
and the native will commit sins. The aspect should
be to the Sun and his associates, viz. Mercury and
Venus.

320 - 323. The natural and temporal strengths of
lords of the various Bhavas and their significators
Should be studied before declaring effects. The
dispositor of the lord of a Bhava becomes its permanent (or chief) signficiator. If the said dispositor is
related to a benefic by aspect, he will be auspicious;
and if to a malefic, he will turn inauspicious. The
house in which the lord of a Bhava is placed becomes
its Anitya (or temporary) Karaka. If this temporary
house is related to malefic planets by aspect or
occupancy, the original Bhava suffers annihiliation.
वर्गोत्तमे वा स्वगृहे as

324. Ifthe significatorofa Bhava isin Vargottama,
or in own sign or in exaltation, or become victorious,
auspicious results concerning the house will follow.

325. The subtle nature of a Bhava should be
understood after evaluation of the Nadiamsa, planetary motions and their various divisions in an elaborate manner. Planetary Cheshtas, dasas etc. should
also be suitably studied before arriving at a true
conclusion.

328 - 330. Importance of Dispositor of Bhava:
He, 1.¢., the dispositor of a Bhava lord alone is the
giver of effects (concerning the original Bhava). If
the Karaka is in own sign, or in Vargottama, or in
exaltation, or be placed amidst benefics, he will cause
more auspicious effects providedd he is nog in debility, combustion, and malefic aspects. The lords of
these Bhavas (?) placed between the lords of the 9th
and the 4th or in debility (weak degrees) will cause
the end of the subject prior to that of his parents
provided he is endowed with prosperity. (That is, if
prosperity is absent, death as above will not occur.)
परमोच्चगते सुता धिनाथे
ओजांशके वा शुभदष्टियुक्ते |

वदन्तिसन्त बहुपुत्रमेनम् 332 I
331. The learned declare birth of many male
issues for one having the lord of the Sth in deep
exaltation in aspect to a benefic and at the same time
Chandra Kala Nadi
occupying an odd Navamsa. It is however essential
that the Sth house should be free from malefic
aspects.
Notes: Discussions on Leo end here for the time
being.
From here upto sloka 335, the rules are general
and not for a particular sign ascending.
In the present aphorism, the lord of the Sth can
be replaced by Jupiter.
Either of the two in deep exaltation, in aspect to
a benefic (Mercury or Venus, or Jupiter in case of 5th
lord’s deep exaltation) will give many sons. The
important condition is the said exalted planet should
be in odd Navamsa. By extension, if the deeply
exalted planet begets even Navamsa, daughters will
be numerous, instead of sons.
Aspect of a malefic on the 5th house (or the 5th
lord or Jupiter) will vitiate the suggested effects.
्रतृस्थाने सूर्यपुत्राधिरुढे

332. Vamsa Vichcheda Yoga: There will be no
birth ofa male issue causing Vamsa Vichcheda Yoga
(discontinuity of dynasty) if Satur is found in the 3rd
while the 8th lord is in Vandhyavastha and the Sth
house is in aspect to or in occupation by a malefic.
Notes: Vandhyavastha is not a standard astrological term like Deepta, Mudita etc. Apparently, the
text stipulates the 8th lord to be in such a position to
deny progeny like being in a barren sign, Leo.
Vamsa Vicheda Yoga specially denies birth of
male progeny.

335. Remedies: To overcome the blemish of
absence of progeny (vide sloka 332), donation of a
goldenimage of Kalpa Druma or the golden image of
a cobra orasplendorous Linga or the image ofa fourarmed Vishnu be done. This will rid one of sins
relating to absence of progeny.
Notes: Kalpa Druma = the Heavenly Tree granting fulfillment of all desires.
Cobra = Image of Nagaraja, preferably with
seven hoods.
Linga = Image of Siva Linga.
Four-armed Vishnu = Chaturbhuja with Divine
Disc capable of destroying evils wholly (1.e.Sudarsana
Chakra) and Divine Mace.
One of the above four images, made of gold,
according to one’s resources be given away in donation.

336 - 337. The 5th lord causing Vandhya Yoga
(i.e. 5th lord involved in a yoga denying progeny)
could be remedied by donating the image of Santhana
Gopala (i.e. Lord Krishna). If the yoga of the 5th lord
denotes acquisition of dead progeny donate the
Sisumara (explained elsewhere in relevant notes)
thrice. Gold weighing 6 units (Pala, a traditional
weight), or half of it, or one fourth of it should be
given in donation. Donating a quantity lesser than
this will not achieve the purpose according to sages.
Notes; Vandhyamsa Yoga and Mritha Prajamsa
yoga are improper terms. They denote childlessness
and dead progeny inorder. Itis a different matter that
some may interpret these as relevant degrees to which
interpretation I am not able to subscribe.

व्ययाधिपस्य स्फुटयोगकाले

सहोदरीभर्ववियोगदुःखमन्तः स्फुटेनापि विचिन्तनीयम् ॥३७९ ॥
379. Grief to Sister: When Satum in transit
attains the (natal) degree of 12th lord (Venus) or the
sign occupied by the 4th lord (Saturn), the native’s
sister will be grieved on account of separation from
husband.
Note: The reference to 4th lord and 12th lord
could at best be put as above in the rendering. The
fourth line is not clear. It seems to me that the
longitudes of (Saturn) 4th Jord, and Venus (12th lord)
should be added. On this degree Saturn’s transit will
cause the same effect - grief to sister.
From the above statement we can conceive the
following instructions.
(a) Saturn’s transit on natal Venus’s degree.
(b) Satum’s transit on his own degree at birth.
(c) Saturn’s transit on the resultant longitude
arrived at by adding those of Venus and Saturn at
birth.
In each case the possibility is sister begetting
grief on account of marital tragedy.

383 - 388. Kutila Nadiamsa - Former Half:
This native of Scorpio will lose bis children as they
are born apart from (himself) suffering from phlegmatic disorders. By donating a specified plant, the
native will be happy obtaining a surviving child. In
the absence of this remedy, evils to progeny will
exist. Other remedies suggested are bathing in the
Sethu and worshipping the golden image of Nageswara
and giving chanity to astrologer. These will bless one
with long-lived progeny. Phlegmatic disorders could
be avoided by worshipping the image of the zodiac.
By donating the image (of the zodiac), tuberculosis
(a phlegmatic disorder) will be destroyed. Worship
of Nava Grahas and Rudrabhisheka (rituals prescribed for Rudra, a form of Lord Siva), (untimely)
death will be surmountable.
Notes: Here no planetis involved, butthe degree
of birth alone.
Phlegmatic disorder indicated is to the tune of
killing the person untimely. Remedies for obtaining
long-lived progeny and for overcoming death are
suggested in the above verses.

when Saturn in transit retrogrades, or is bright with
rays (i.e. uncombust in the Sun).

भाग्येशांशे त्रिकोणे स्फुटयोगं गते गुरो ।

400 - 401. (a) Navamsa of 9th Lord: It will be
a time of prosperity when Jupiter transits the sign
identical with the Navamsa occupied by the 9th lord
at birth, ora sign trinal thereof, or the sign(s) aspected
by the 9th lord.
(b) So also in respect of Jupiter’s transits in the `
following places:
(1) The Navamsa Rasi denoted by the occupation of the ascendant lord, or a trine thereof.
(2) the Rasi occupied by the lord of the ascendant.
(3) the Rasi aspected by the lord of the ascendant.
Notes: The combinations given are quite simple.
The planets involved are the Moon (9th lord) and
Mars (ascendant lord). Jupiter is one of the best
planets for the ascendant in question.
Since ten particular signs can be generated for
Jupiter’s transit to be favourable, a liberal approach
is not suggested. One should be quite selective,
keeping directional influences and other relevant
transits in mind.
Also see the following verses, for specific effects.

408 - 4111/2. Scorpio Ascendant - Kutila
Nadiamsa: Due to blemishes caused by adverse
placements of planets in the zodiac, the native of
Kutila Nadiamsa, Scorpio ascendant will during
Saturn’s movement in Libra, incur evils to his kinsfolk. During Saturn’s journey in Scorpio, his prosperity will be checkered and he will be in distress. At
forty-eight he will face (timely) death. However after
forty, he will be in the grip of fear of untimely death
after which time he will survive due to favourable
planetary combinations contributing to life-span
(Yogayu). At the age of forty-eight, evil planets will
overtake him. There will be danger to him ona high
road during his fortieth year. If he survives this
danger, he will be famous, happy and prosperous in
his birth country.
Notes: Froma study of relevant verses of Kutila
Nadiamsa, Scorpio ascendant, we can gather that the
subject will be endangered in his forties. The life will
not be quite long.
तुलाशनो तु सन्तानं दीर्धमायुश्च विन्दति ive? ॥
412. For Scorpio ascendant, Kutila Nadiamsa,
Saturn placed in Libra at birth will grant long lives to
the person’s progeny.
Notes: As is being seen by us on various occasions inthis work, the 12th denotes death of children.
Saturn, signifying longevity, in the 12th will confer
long-lives to children of the native.
Thus, an adverse or weak planet in the 12th will
cut short progeny’s longevity.

413. The death of the subject native (at 48) will

414. Venus in Scorpio: Should Venus be in the
divisions of Aries, while placed in Scorpio, the
subject of Scorpio ascendant will take to ascetism due
to a family calamity. He will further win his five
senses,
Note: “Meshamsa” is not translated as Aries
Navamsa because Venus in Scorpio cannot get such
Navamsa. Then it denotes the other possible division
of Aries.
Winning the senses is essential for a true Sanyasi
with fully severed mundane bonds. Venus is the lord
of the 7th, ruling one’s senses. His occupation of
Aries Vargas or divisions, placed in the ascendant
Scorpio, Should allow one’s senses to move toward
self-realisation.
In otherwise an afflicted horoscope, this position
of Venus will only misdirect the subject to enjoying
material pleasures of various kinds, instead of granting him true ascetism.

418 - 420. Rectified Ashtaka Varga: (a) When
Saturn transits the signs that have 1, 2 or3 Bindus in
` the Ashtaka Varga of the ascendant, intense difficulties will come to pass.
(b) Abundant wealth will be acquired if the sign
containing five Bindus in Ascendant’s Ashtaka Varga
is transited by benefic planets.
(c) During the currency of favourable dasa periods, if the signs containing five or more bindus are
transited by malefic planets, great prosperity will
occurapart from acquisition of wealth of quadrupeds.
Notes: The authordeals with use of Lagnashtaka
Varga (rectified with Ekadhipathya and trinal cortections).
(a) This rule is clear and applies to the various
rectified Ashtaka vargas, the net essence of which is
adversity.
(b) Benefic planets in transit on their own accord
can improve good effects even if the dasa periods are
somewhat adverse.
(c) As for malefics, to give the good effects cited,
the additional requirement is operation of favourable
dasa periods.
The reader will note the difference between
malefic and benefic transits.

423 - 426. Dasa Periods: (a) If however, the dasa
lord be seen with mediocre strength the undertakings
commenced by the person will incur slow effects;
obstacles of strong nature, and (even) destruction.
(b) If the dasa lord be located with Jeast strength,
one’s wealth will be destroyed; he will face loss of
position, calumny from others, loss of quadrupeds
etc., diseases to spouse, progeny and the like and
abundant grief. Further his parents and other kinsfolk
will fall prey to diseases and he himself will be
subjected to great distress.
(c) Should the dasa lord be deprived cf strength
in toto, one’s physical felicity will be completely
void.
(d) The lord of the dasa provided with full
strength, or being witha benefic planet, will increase
the auspicious effects.

432. Jupiter in Pisces: One having Jupiter in
Pisces will be liked by his king, be righteous, acquainted with higher knowledge, passionate, peaceful and virtuous.
Notes: As per Kalyana Varma, Jupiter in Pisces

will make one dear to king.

Notes: (a) This position of Jupiter in the 10th

about progeny.

440 - 441. Early Death of Father: The Moon
should join Saturn and Mars and be in the Nakshatra
represented by the remainder in the process of multiplying the rectified Bindus in the 9th place in Sun’s
Ashtaka varga by the Sun’s Sodhya Pinda and dividing by 27. Furtheras a result of this yoga, the person’s
mother will be distressed. He will have no after-born.
His prosperity will be of a great order in the dasa of
the Sun, ina foreign land.
Notes: “Suryashtakavarga Samsodhya
Nakshatra” on its own has no specific meaning, as
given above. But such an interpretation is a part of
chapters related to use of Sodhya Pindas, Nakshatras
etc. in the Ashtaka Varga method. There may be
scholars who can give better interpretation to the
above condition.

461 - 463. Saturn in Svathi: When Saturn
transits Svathi, there will be destruction of enemics,
and incurring fear. One will befriend enemies from
the time the Sun moves in Capricorn through Pisces
(coinciding with Saturn’s stay inSvathi). Should the
Sun, then, be found in transit in the signs commencing from Aries, one’s enemics will be destroyed.
However with the Sun in Gemini, there will be some
distress and later on health will prevail. With the
Sun’s change of course in Pisces, one will attain
success in undertakings while happiness will prevail
with the change of Sun’s course in Cancer. In
between there will be distress.
Notes: Sloka 463 has an incompatible term
“Meenayana”, The Sun does not change his course
in Pisces. Hence this is a wrong term. His transit in
Meena or Pisces is part of his northerly course or
Uttarayana.
For ready information of the reader, the Sun
changes his course twice in a year. When he enters
Sayana (tropical) Capricom, he commences his northerly course, i.e. Uttarayana. When he enters Sayana
Cancer, he commences his southerly course,
Dakshinayana.
For Hindus' religious purposes, these courses are
detected on Nirayana (sidercal) basis.

465 - 470. Saturn’s Transit: When Saturn moves
in the third Nakshatra from the birth Nakshatra, the
person will seize his enemics but he will also have
fear of a great order; if it is the second Nakshatra, he
willbe in the grip of fear. In the fourth Nakshatra, the
transit of Saturn will cause distress and quarrels in
native country, trouble to populace (?), mental worries and enmity with the king of Pandya country. This
transit will also cause the native’s destruction. The
2ndand 4th Nakshairas being in transit by Saturn will
cause death of the king (2), and trouble to relatives.
When malefics transit natal ascendant, 10th house
and Adhana ascendant, the subject will incur
misunderstanding with royal circles, or physical
illness to self, and troubles to his kinsfolk. If these are
in transit by bencfic planets, auspicious results will
come to pass, and if by malefics, mental distress will
be incurred. The wise should deduce the effects
according to caste, community ete.

effects. Final effects will depend on the age of the
native concerned.

476 - 478. The Janma Nakshatra coinciding with
a week day ruled by a benefic planet will cause
various kinds of wealth and happiness. Physical
distress, royal wrath, and infirm disposition will
follow birth ona week day ruled by a maletic planet.
Sunday birth denotes (aimless) wandering, royal
wrathand (futile) arguments. Happiness, acquisition
of wonderful flowers, affectionate cohabitation, and
good food will result birth on Monday. Grief, selfdestruction, quarrels, physical ailments and endless
wanderings are the effects for birth on Tuesday. One
born on a Wednesday will be happy and derive
pleasures through honey, ghee, learning, and listening to mythological stories. Jupiter’s day being birth
day will bless one with robes, fruits, honey, wealth (or
articles) and attending to temple festivals. Fridayborn will obtain ornaments, enjoyments from females, various kinds of clothes and lovely scents.
Grief, difficulties, fear, diseases and servitude will be
the effects for birth on Saturday.
Notes: Reference to Janma Nakshatra on a particular week day is made in the first line of sloka 476.
Whereas without a reference to Janma nakshatra,
these results could have directly been attributed to
week days. For there is no specific Nakshatra involved with a given day.

482 - 485. (a) Effects of Saturn in Transit in
Various Nakshatras: Saturn transiting the Janma
Nakshatra will produce royal wrath, grief and infirm
finances. If the transit be in the 2nd Nakshatra,
Chandra Kala Nadi
wealth, comforts, ornaments and robes will be galore. There will be destruction of quadrupeds, lands,
and wealth, grief, diseases, separation from relatives,
quarrels with servants and a temperament to lose
temper instantly, when it is the third Nakshatra. One
will be happy and rich, will enjoy food comforts and
will receive a messenger (with good news) if the 4th
Nakshatra is transited by Saturn. The 5th Nakshatra
in this respect creates royal anger, financial pressure,
obstacles to workand grief. Attainments, acquisition
of progeny, financial success, lands, wealth, garlands, and robes will be produced by Saturn in transit
in the 6th Nakshatra. Much grief, difficulties, and
diseases caused by the co-operative vitiation of the
three humours of the body will be the result of Saturn
transiting the 7th one from Janma Nakshatra. His
transit in the 8th Nakshatra will cause union with
friends, honours from royal circles, ornaments, conveyanices, devotion to one’s calling and bed comforts. If the 9th Nakshatra from Janma Nakshatra is
transited by Saturn, the subject will befriend the king,
will move with virtuous men, be engaged in learning,
will carry out royal orders, will have visits by his
progeny and relatives and be endowed with wellbeing.
(b) Saturn’s Transit in 12th House: [fSaturn be
in transit in the 12th house which happens to be his
close friend’s place, the subject will be engaged in
thoughts ofa low caste woman ("Vrishalu" apart from
meaning “low caste woman” also means an unmarried girl of 12 years of age whose menstruation has
just commenced, or a woman who is the mother of a
still-born child). He will think also of his progeny
(i.e. will be worried about them), will incur fever, will
wander aimlessly, will incur phlegmatic disorders,
will hate food and will obtain comforts galore.

The adverse effects for Saturn and other planets
will be revealed by the Sun in adverse transits.
Similarly the good effects denoted will materialise with the Sun moving in favourable places.

authorities one will lose household articles ina theft
and will incur destruction and difficulties. Further he
will be in debt and troubled by ulcers. In the sub
periods (of adverse planets) in the dasa of Saturn’s
dispositor, he will be troubled by debts, theft and
ulcer. The dasa of the dispositor ofa benefic will give
auspicious effects. Similarly the dasa of the dispositor of a malefic planet will give inauspicious effects.
The sub period of the dispositor of the 12th lord will,
in the dasa of the dispositor of the 8th lord, cause fear
of untimely death, and also grief on account of loss
of one’s kinsfolk. Among the dasas of the Rasi/

लग्नादिद्रादशभावसंज्ञाः।
लग्नात् स्वकी्तितनुरूपगुणस्वभावाः
स्वर्णादिलोचनकुटुम्बमुखाशनानि ।
युद्धप्रभावबलवीर्य कपोलकर्णवक्षस्थलादिमुखमन्दिरयानभोगान् ॥४९४ ॥
कुश्षिप्रदेशमतिपुत्रभु जादिशिष्य
सापलशत्रुवरण रोगविषं च नाभिम्
स्रीवस्तिमूत्रमदमन्मथतल्पमारगाः॥
श्रीधर्मदेवशुभभाग्यगुरुस्तपांसि
मानप्रवासक्रतुवृत्तियशोऽभिमानात् ॥४९५ ॥
मेषुरणास्पदजयादि च जीवनानि
निर्विघ्नसिद्धिविभवंभव कर्णदेशो |
विघ्नार्थनाशशयनादि सुखं च नाशं

लग्नादितः सुमतिमान् कथयेत् फलानि ॥४९६ ॥
494 . 496. The wise should deduce the following
fromthe 12 houses of a horoscope and declare effects
accordingly.
The ascendant - Fame, physique, figure and
disposition.
2nd house - Gold (i.e.wealth), eyes, family, face
and food.
3rd house - Ability in war, prowess, cheek and
ears.
4th house - chest, comforts, residence, conveyances and pleasures. (“Mukha” should be read as
“Sukha” to mean comforts. “Mukha” means mouth
which is ruled by the 2nd house and not by the 4th
house.)
Sth house: Stomachial region, mind, sons, hand
(?) and disciples.
6th house: Step-mother, enemies, ulcers, disease, poison, navel.
7th house: Wife, the lower belly or pelvis, urine,
sexual desire, amorous passion, sexual enjoyment.
8th house: Hole (Randhra itself means the 8th
house), calumny, death, lack ofcleanliness, secretive
diseases.
9th house: Fortunes, righteousness, gods, auspicious acts, prosperity, preceptors, and penance.
10th house: Honour, foreign residence, Yagnas
(Vedic rituals), profession, fame, self-conceit, authority and success.
11 thhouse: Attainment without obstacles, wealth
(or exalted position) and well-being.
12th house: Obstacles, loss of wealth, destruction and bed-comforts.
Notes: As for the8th house, which is called
Randhrasthaana, hole means the cavity through which
the life force leaves the body at the time of death.
There are nine holes in the body. Some recognise 10,
including the cavity at skull. That is why the 8th is
related to end of life. The ancients believed thatwhen
the life force left the body through the hole of the skull
- called Brahma Randhra - one attained Sathya Loka,
the world of Brahma and got rid of further births.
Should the life force leave the body through the hole
of rectal orurinary passage, the dead would go to hell
and would have rebirths.

तद्द्रावकारकफलानि च चिन्तयित्वा
योगानुगुण्यफलमत्र विचारणीयम् ॥४९७ ॥
497. The good effects of a Bhava depend on the
strength of the lord of that bhava; a weak planet will
give converse results. In analysing the effects of a
Bhava, the yogas (association, aspects etc.) involved
thereof should be given due consideration.

2nd, 9th and Sth give auspicious effects. All the
planets will prove favourable in their transits in the
11th. However when Saturn joins or aspects such a
planet (moving in the 11th), there will be danger.
Two planets joining in the 11th will give wealth.
Notes: From here onwards, general rules without considering the ascendant are formulated.
The transit effects are to be counted from the
Moon sign unless and otherwise stated.

532 - 536. Saturn’s Transit Effects: (a) Danger,
damage, wealth, abdominal disease, great fear, destruction of enemies, great courage, death, physical
decay, suffering, gains and 1085865 are the effects of
Saturn moving in 12 places in order.
(b) Saturn in the Ist (i.e. on the Moon sign) will
cause dispute and argument.
In the 2nd - loss of money and troubles from
enemies.
In the 3rd - eating comforts, happiness to progeny and kinsfolk, success in war, many kinds of
happiness and fulfilment of desires.
In the 4th - destruction of friends, progeny,
wealth, quadrupeds, lands, undertakings and honour.
In the Sth - mental aberration, various kinds of
worries, and faults.
In the 6th - advent of wealth aplenty and happiness.
In the 7th - destruction of wife, progeny and
servants.
In the 8th - destruction in general, trouble from
enemues, loss of weight on account of grief.
In the 9th - attachment to sinful acts, and sudden
destruction of auspiciousness and prosperity, pitiable
State or miserliness, and troubles on account of sexual
unions.
Chandra Kala Nadi
In the 10th - loss of fame (or honour), fear of
strife and blemish.
In the 11th - acquisition of wealth and installation as a king (i.e. getting superior position).
In the 12th - destruction of figure and limbs,
experiencing abundant grief, irritation, humiliation
in the hands of servants, victimisation through severe
diseases.
Seeing the transits of Saturn, one can off-handedly
declare effects.

clues will not emerge from certain areas in the text.

from the following sloka, No.560.

627 - 6361/2. The abdominal sickness caused by
Saturn-Sun conjunction in the 5th can be warded off
by donating gingilee cakes ("Tilapishtarkadanena"
should read as "Tilapsiita pradanena"). An idol
made of gingilee cakes, with three eyes and two
diamond-studded hands, with red robes and surrounded by horses be obtained. Homa (fire sacrifice)
as prescribed be done and charities given, which will
serve as the most excellent remedy according to
Rudra Yamala. The (worship of the) image of the Sun
God made of gingilee cakes will increase life span.
“Grant me health, prosperity and progeny.” (This is
the meaning of the mantra given inthe text.) The Sun
god should be placed in the middle of the image of the
zodiac. At the end of the Pooja, charity be given
away. This will increase health, prosperity and will
stall untimely death. To donate the image of the
zodiac to the astrology is among the most meritorious
Chandra Kala Nadi
deeds. Due to the mercy of the Nava grahas, he will
be endowed with longevity and health. His desires
will be fulfilled. The pot decorated as per Vedic
injunctions should be placed in the midst of Nava
Dhanyas (nine kinds of grains) along with the image
of the zodiac and be worshipped. At the end of the
Homa, charities be givenaway whereupon longevity,
health and fulfillment of desires will come to pass,
according to (Rudra) Yamala.

cakes and worship of Sun God. Asa result, abdominal diseases will not trouble the native.

711 - 7141/2. Effects stated about paternal uncles
will bear truth. So also about maternal uncles. The
Moon in the ascendant (Leo) while Mars is in the 4th
house will cause evil effects for maternal relatives,
will trouble maternal uncles and maternal grandfather. In the absence of these, evils to maternal
uncles (if not to other maternal relatives) will come
lo pass. To overcome these evils, suitable remedies
be resorted to, As prescribed by Sastras, Homa (fire
sacrifice) for Mars and Rahu should be performed.
Donation of Nagapasa (a sort of magical noose)
should also be done. After these, auspiciousness in
maternal relatives’ familics will prevail.
Notes: “Janma Pake Nisa Nathe” in sloka 711
means Moon in Janma Dasa which does not convey
a proper meaning. It should be MooninJanma Lagna,
j.c. natal ascendant, i.e. Leo. The translation is
accordingly rectified.
In the yoga for evils to maternal relatives, the
Moon in the ascendant and Mars in the 4th only
feature. But in remedy, Rahu is included and Moon
excluded. Rabu is Sarpa (or snake); hence donation
of Nagapasa is prescribed.
Donation of Nagapasa 15 notclearly understandable. Nagapasa is a divine noose used in the remote
past in large-scale battles which would bind the
inflicted by venomand deprive him of consciousness.
This weaponis attributed to Varuna by ourscriptures.
For example, Naga Pasa (or Nagaasthra) was used on
Lakshmana in Ramayana era whereupon Lord
Hanuman had to bring the Sanjivani plant.
After performing the prescribed Fire Sacrifice
(or Homa) for Mars and Rahu, one may donate an
image of Cobra, preferably golden, ifnota Nagapasa
precisely.

732 735. (4) The sub periods of lords of 8th/1 1th
in the dasa of the 6th lord will destroy the person’s
elder brother.
(b) During the operation of the Sth lord’s dasa,
Saturn in transit in the sign identical with the Navamsa occupied by the 5th lord at birth or a trine,
thereof will cause diseases to elder brother.
(c) In this dasa of Sth lord, the sub periods of
Saturn, Mercury and Mars will cause sickness to
elder brother or paternal uncle. (d) The sub period of
Venus in any dasa will cause distress to father who
will also have fear of royal wrath in the sub periods
ruled by Saturn in general.
Notes: (a) The sub period of Jupiter or of Mercury in the major period of Saturn will harm older
brothers of a Leo native. Jupiter is the natural
significator for older co-born while Mercury is the
11th lord, having a sway over older co-born.
(b) Sth lord’s dasa is Jupiter’s dasa.
(c) The sub periods of Saturn, Mercury and Mars
in the major period of Jupiter are said to be adverse
forelder brother or paternal uncle. Mercury involved
is understandable. But Saturn and Mars playinga role
needs a special note.
(d) The sub period Venus and of Saturn in any
dasa is said to be generally adverse in certain respects, because they are evil lords for this ascendant.

736. General: When the dasa and (or) sub
period ruled by Saturn be in operation, the transit of
Saturn ina sign will destroy the good effects of that
Bhava with reference to which the transited sign is the
8th one. So say the learned in astrology.
Notes: Take the case of Leo ascendant for
example, having Saturn in transit in Taurus ata time
when Saturn dasa, or Saturn’s sub period in another
dasa is in operation. Taurus 15 the 8th from Libra.
Libra denotes brothers, sisters, journeys, writing
activities, arms, legs, lungs etc. for Leo ascendant.
Thus the transit of Saturn as above will play
havoc in these spheres.

737 - 740. Sivada Nadiamsa - Leo ascendant:
Saturn in the 3rd in exaltation, in full strength, and
being with the Moon will give three brothers and one
sister, all with long life spans. After some time, the
person’s landed properties will be destroyed. So also
his children being born. To overcome the blemish of
progeny, donation of (the image made out of) a
specified plant (named Sisumara, cited in Varaha
Mihira’s Brihat Samhita, wherein it is referred to as
Sisumar1) should be given away. This remedy will
increase the life span of progeny. Because of Saturn
and Moon as above, the subject following his marriage will incur destruction of wealth in his in-laws’
family.

Deva Keralam
731 - 760. Sivada Nadiamsa - Leo Ascendant:
The Sun in the 3rd house in debility will cause
following effects. When Saturn comes to that degree
(occupied by the Sun), there will be drought(?)
Further, the person’s income will be obstructed to
enmity in royal circles. (Without referring to Saturn),
this native will be happy after 30 years of age, in
Mercury’s sub period (of 2nd dasa). He will be
famous, but will incur some enmity with coborn.
Blemishes will be destroyed in the 3rd dasa during the
sub period of second lord (Mercury). In the sub
period cited earlier, the head of the country will be
endangered(?) The idols kept in worship will be
destroyed. There will be fear of obstructed rains (2)
The 4th dasa will cause mental agony. The first haif
of this dasa will produce much distress while the later
half will bestow happiness and a prosperous calling.
The 5th dasa will be somewhat auspicious while the
6th dasa will (indeed) be auspicious. His death will
occur in 56th year, failing which 71st year will be
fatal. In his second dasa he will visit Sethu along with
his father. His coborn will be long lived. The native’s
third dasa will bring prosperity forthe brother. After
30, prosperity will be on the rise and auspicious
effects of Raja Yoga will be experienced. He will
visit shrines and become famous in royal service.
Fortieth or forty-second year will cause fear of
untimely death and abundant difficulties.

761, Mercury in 12th: Mercury in the 12th for
a native of Sivada Nadiamsa of Leo ascendant will
cause attainment of good worlds after death So also
for his father. This yoga will cause fortunes throughout the life of the native.

775 - 777. Infantile Mortality & Remedies:
Should the 8th lord Sun be in the ascendant, with a
benefic’s aspect, and a benefic’s Navamsa, the child
will incur Balarishta or infantile mortality, to some
extent, for two or three weeks in the first year, or in
the first sub period of the 2nd dasa. For this, remedies
be done in the form of worship of the image of the
Moon god made of silver installed ona copper vessel.
Suitable charities be also given away (at the end ofthe
worship).

778 - 782. For birth in the former half of Kalika
Nadiamsa, Capricorn ascendant, with the Sun in
Capricorn, the blemish of infantile mortality could be
overcome by propitiating the Sun God thus: An
image of the Sun God made of gold, decorated with
red apparels be worshipped at home with Japa (recitation of sacred spells) and Homa (fire sacrifice) as
prescribed by the Sastras. Pacifying the Sun God
thus, the blemish can be warded off. The Moon god”s
image decorated with robes should be worshipped as
prescribed by “Chandra Kalpa" which will bring
happiness to maternal relatives, and will remove the
balanishta effects of the child as well.

Deva Keralam
783. One born in the second half of Kalika
Nadiamsa, Capricorn ascendant will be wanting in
energy. He will lead a life marked by troubles and be
endowed with brothers and sisters.
Notes: In the first line “A /asyaadisusaukhyavan”
appears. Alasya means want of energy. The effect of
being happy denoted by saukhya does not go well
with “want of energy and troubled life”. Hence such
an effect is not included in the rendering.

784. Life Span: The dispositor of the 8th lord
being in exaltation and with strength will cause
longevity to the native of Capricorn, Kalika Nadiamsa.
Jupiter in the 8th, with a benefic’s Navamsa will also
bless one with longevity.
Notes: Some may like to club both the above
conditions into one to obtain longevity. However, a
purposeful interpretation does not deter splitting this
into two principles, each giving longevity. Because
each on its own merit can confer a long life.
पापांशे पापदृष्टे वा ह्यल्पायुर्दायमादिशेत् |

785. Short Life: Astrologers predict short life if
Jupiter in the 8th is aspected by a malefic, and
occupies debilitation Navamsa, or a Navamsa ruled
by a malefic planet.
Notes: This does not apply to Capricom ascendant as Jupiter in Leo in the 8th will not obtain
debilitation Navamsa. This should be applied tosuch
ascendants only where Jupiter in the 8th obtains
debilitation Navamsa.
मृतावस्थायां कींशे ax पापनिरीक्षिते ।
शुभदृष्टिविहीने च arene विनिर्दिशेत् ॥७८६ ॥
गुरुदष्टियुते चन्द्रे शुभखेचरसंयुते |
जीवितावस्थगे az वालारिष्टं न विद्यते isc |
786-7 Capricorn Ascendant - Infantile Mortality: For a native of Capricorn ascendant, Kalika
Nadiamsa, the Moon in extremis, with Scorpio Navamsa, and in aspect to a malefic planet, without that
of a benefic, will cause infantile mortality. Should
the Moon be in association with Jupiter or another
benefic, and be InJeevitavastha, there will be nosuch
effect.
Notes: The Moon will be in Mritavastha or in
extremis in the last six degrees ofan odd sign and the
first six degrees of an even sing.
“Jeevithavastha” is not a standard astrological
term, and hence defies proper identification. At best
it can be interpreted that the Moon should not be in
Mritavastha or in extremis. Opposite of Mrita (dead)
is Jeevitha (not dead, alive).

791 -794. Kalika Nadiamsa - Saturn in Aspect
to Mars: Saturn being in aspect to Mars and placed
in Libra, with his own Navamsa will give following
results for a Capricorn native. He will be happy after

Siva and righteous. Further, he will be ever ready to
help others, and be more intelligent than his father.
He will have two maternal uncles, be passionate, will
have three paternal uncles, and be pious.

863 - 865. Bhava Lagna: Venus should become
the lord of Bhava Lagna, and, being in aspect to the
9th lord Saturn, should beget his own Navamsa. Then
the native will reap abundant fortunes in the 3rd dasa.
The 2nd dasa will (generally) give great distress. But
its first half will cause much auspicious effects and

Notes: Bhava Lagna is one of the special ascendants. From Sun rise to the time of birth, every five
ghatis (or 120 minutes of clock time) constitute one
Bhava Lagna. In other words, the ghatis and vighatis
elapsed from sun rise to birth should be divided by
five and the quotient in terms of signs and degrees
should be added to the Sun’s longitude prevailing at
the commencement of the day.
Example: Sun’s longitude on the starting of the

day concerned = Virgo 15°.
Time of birth = 20 ghatis. Divided by 5 this
yields foursigns or 120°. When 120° are added to the
Sun (i.e. 165°), we get Bhava Lagna as 285° or
Capricorn 15°.
This is as per Brihat Parasara Hora.
For further details the reader may refertoJaimini
literature.

Notes: "Nishaada" in sloka 881 means a nonAryan tribe regarded as hunters, robbers, fishermen
etc.
“Madhyame Kale” vide sloka 881 denotes the
middle portion of Chitra Nakshatra, 1.6. ending portion of second quarter and beginning portion of third
quarter. It practically means the 3rd quarter of Chitra
Nakshatra where the Moon is required to be. This
gives her Vargottama position in Libra.

896 - 905. To get rid of the said blemish, the

notes in the present volume), should be obtained (andworshipped) following which one will be relieved of
bondage. On the left side of Varuna, the image of

in His hand, adoring the crescent Moon on His head,
along with Uma, His consort, should be installed and
worshipped. Worship, Japa, and Homa for Durga
will increase the effects three-fold. At the end of the
ritual, charities should be given away. Goddess
Durga's both sides should be occupied by Varuna and
Rudra. The image of Durga should be provided with
red robes. Prayerto Uma (i.e. Parvathi) willelminate
enemies and will bless the aspirant with health,
fortunes and wealth.

preceding verse (1.6. sloka 895). As such we should
take it to be general involving the worship of Varuna,
Rudra and Durga. For further details, see Rudra
Yamala.

925 - 937. Ona day of Ashtami, Chaturdasi,
Dwadasi, or Parva (the days of four changes of the
Moon; also Ashtami and Chaturdasi of either fortnight) coinciding with the native's birth star or its
trines, and the week day ruled bya "malefic", the wise
should choose a temple located ina shrine on the bank
ofa river. There should be erected a pavilion on the
ground in the midst of which a wooden vessel be
placed containing de-husked rice grains. This should
be covered with a new cloth. An axe with a silver
handle and a sum of 100 or 50 or 25 gold coins should
be given away in donation in order that the enemies
are destroyed. The donor should take his seat facing
the east and recite "Mama satru kshayarthaya thava
poojam karomyaham | Sarva satru kshayakaraayethi
madhyamam partpujayeth" (I worship you inorderto
have my enemies destroyed.) Parasu Rama, who was
fierce, who saw the blood of his enemies, who offered

their flesh, who destroyed members of royal clan,
who was chiefamong Brahmins and dearto Lord Siva
and who weore red robes, who adored elephant's skin
and who used the axe to destroy enemies. This ritual
will destroy enemies and save the subject from fear
of untimely death.

Chirajiva Nadiamsa extends thus: from 12° 24'

of immovable signs; andd from 27° 24’ to 27° 36' in
