941 -943. Chirajiva Nadiamsa - Former Half:
The native of Gemini ascendant, of this degree, will
be blood-red in complexion, and be valorous. His
fortunes will be through his in-laws (or he will have
fortunate in-laws). He will have three sisters, plenty
of wealth and grains. He will be a student of the
Vedas and be learned (in general). He will be a
presiding person in the reign of a great Saiva teacher.
A person enjoying freedom, he will love his family,
be honourable and respected in royal circles.
Notes: “Mahakaala Gurudvare Sarvadhyakshah” needs some clarification. Mahakaala is
a form of Lord Siva in his character of "great destroyer". Thus, the native will hold a position with a
preceptor who propagates Saivism. In essence the
subject will be engaged in spreading Lord Siva’s
school of thought, distinctly opposed to Vaishnavism.
For those who want freedom to interpret this
complicated expression, the other meanings of
Mahakaala are supplied below.
Mahakaala = A place sacred to the destroyerSiva; a mythical mountain; a teacher so named;
Durga in Her terrific form; one of Herattendants; one
of the sixteen Vidya-devis; the Linga in Ujjayini; a
Goddess who executed the commands of fifth Arhat
of the present Avasarpini.

second one, 7th from the 5th the third one, and so on
and so forth.)

ईशानांशगते सोम्ये भ्राताप्राबल्यंसयुतः॥१०८८ ॥
पाण्डयदेशे पितुः सौख्यं राजसन्मानमादिशेत् |
केतुभुक्त्यन्तमारभ्य शुक्रभुक्ताविदं फलम् ॥१०८९ ॥
जीवे शुक्रान्तरे काले विप्लवेन प्रजादिषु |
पश्चात् स्वस्थं प्रकुर्वीत पित्रादीनां शुभावहम् ॥१०९० ॥
गुरुराहुयुते काले राजामात्यजनावधि |
राजन्यजनसन्तापो ब्राह्मणानां महद्धयम् ॥१०९१॥
पश्ादधूपतिनाशः स्यात् सिहादित्रयगे शनो |
पोष्णश्रवणहस्ताद्रा भरणीवायुभेष्वपि ॥१०९२ ॥
WAR नृणां दुरिक्षभयदः स्मृतः।
तुलाशनिस्थिते काले राहुवक्रंगते गुरो ॥१०९३ ॥
मरुदवृधाप्रान्त्यदेशे नृपाणां समये महान् |
कोल्लापुरीप्रान्त्यदेशे ह्यथवा चोलदेशके ॥१०९४॥
मण्डलधिपतेरमल्ु्जीविते कलहो महान्।
1085 - 10941/2. (a) Prior to this the marriage of
the ruler will have occurred. These are the effects for
beginning and concluding portions of sub periods of
Ketu and Venus. There will be drought conditions in
the country. One of the friends of father, a Soodra,
will obtain a son. The king, served by the person’s
father will beget married. (b) In the sub period of
Venus, his father will visita king and will gain wealth
through the good offices of the army chief. (c)
Mercury in Isaana Nadiamsa will cause prosperity of
coborns. (d) The person’s father will be happy in
Pandya kingdom and will receive royal honours. (e)
These are effects for sub periods of Ketu and Venus.
(f) In the sub period of Venus during Jupiter’s dasa
people will be troubled by revolt. Stability will result
later on. (g) Father and others will receive auspicious
effects in the said dasa periods. (h) The time of
Jupiter - Rahu conjunction will cause distress to king
and his ministers, and fear to Brahmins. Later on the
king will be destroyed with Saturn moving in Leo
through Libra. (i) Saturn’s movements in Revathi,
Sravana, Hastha, Arudra, Bharani and Svathi will
cause drought. (j) While Satur is in Libra, retrograde

Deva Keralam
Jupiter joining Rabu will cause martyrdom ina war
to the king of Marudh, Kolhapur or Chola. Ifthe king
survives in the war, the war will assume greater
proportions.
Notes: In these slokas, a lot of irrelevant material
has crept in. The reader will reject such instructions,
and will take those shown at: c, d, and g only.
शुक्रान्तरे पितृव्यस्य विवाहोत्सवमादिशेत् ॥१०९५ ॥
सोदरोत्पत्तिमाप्नोति जनकस्य महत् सुखम् |
जीवे सूर्याने काले पितृसोख्यं धनागमः॥१०९६ ॥
राजप्रीतिश्चय भवति स्वक्े्रादेर्धनागमः।
1095 - 10961/2. The sub period of Venus in
Jupiter dasa will promote marriage to a paternal
uncle. The native will beget a coborn and his father
happiness. In the sub period of the Sun, father will be
happy, will gain wealth, will satisfy the king and will
gain wealth in his region.
ईशानां शके सोम्ये जीवे केत्वन्तरान्तरे ॥१०९७ ॥
सर्यान्तरेऽथ वा चेति गृहदारवेशाद् व्यथा |
स्वराजराष् कलहो भृत्यामात्यजनेषु च ॥१०९८॥
पश्चात् सोयं च लभते शुक्रभुक्तौ शुभादिकम् |
कन्यामासे चाष्टमब्दे मास्सु कुम्भादिषु त्रिषु ॥१०९९ ॥
अतीव क्लेशकार्याणि जातकस्य ज्वराद्विपत्।
शुक्रान्तरे जीवदाये वेदाध्ययनमादिशेत् ॥११०० ॥
aa मिश्रफलं सोदयोतपत्तिमादिशेत् |
जीवे भोमान्तरे काले पितुपीडा धनक्षयः ॥११०१॥
अथवा सोदरश्चैव पितृव्यसुतनाशनम्।
1097 - 1101 1/2. Mercury in Isaana Nadiamsa:
(a) One born in Gemini ascendant with Mercury in
the specified Nadiamsa will incur troubles on account of home and spouse in the sub periods of Ketu
and the Sun, in the major period of Jupiter.
(b) There will be a civil war involving servants
and ministers after which normalcy will restore (?)
(c) In between the phase of distress indicated at
“a”, the sub period of Venus will give happiness and
abundant auspicious effects.
(d) During the eighth year, with the Sun moving
in Virgo, Aquarius, Pisces and Aries, the person will
be endangered by fever.
(€) The sub period of Venus in Jupiter dasa will
initiate him into study of the Vedas.
(f) The Sub period of the Moon will give mixed
effects, and birth of coborn.
(g) Jupiter dasa, Mars sub period will danger
one’s father and will destroy wealth. Or it could
destroy one’s coborn, or the progeny of a paternal
uncle.
चापे शनिस्थिते काले स्वप्रभोश्च महोत्सवः॥११०२ ॥
तुलाशनौ विवाहः स्याद् वृश्चिके व्याकुलं महत् |
जन्मनाथदशायां तु दाराधिपतिदायके ॥११०३ ॥
ईशानांशप्रजातस्य प्रथमोद्राहमादिशेत्।
रन्ध्राधिपदशासन्धिकाले मृत्युज्वरार्दितः॥११०४ ॥
दोषपरिहारार्थं शानि कुर्याद्विचक्षणः
स्वमातुलसुतारिष्टमथवा बन्धुनाशनम् ॥११०५ ॥
1102-1105. (a) Irrelevant: During the transit of
Saturn in Sagittarius, the ruler of the country will be
engaged in great celebrations.
(b) Gemini ascendant - Isaana Nadiamsa:
With Saturn in Libra in transit, the subject of Gemini
will beget married while the said planet in Scorpio
will give much distress. Marriage for the first time
can (also) take place in the sub period of the 7th lord
in the first dasa.
(c) One will be inflicted by deadly fever in the
junction period of the dasa of the 8th Jord Saturn. To
overcome this evil, necessary propitiations be resorted to. In the same phase of directional influences,
destruction of relatives, and danger to maternal uncle’s progeny will also come to pass.
जीवे राहृन्तर काले Beare विनिर्दिशेत् |
रन्धाधिपत्रिकोणक्षं रप्रेशांशत्रिकाणगे ॥११०६ ॥
सषुटयोगं गते मन्दे पितृव्यारिष्टमादिशेत् |
तदभावे पितृव्यस्य विभागमिति चादिशेत् ॥११०४॥
1106 - 1107. Rahu’s Sub Period: During the
main period of Jupiter, the sub period of Rahu will
cause grief as well as happiness. Evils to paternal

uncles be foretold when Saturn transits the sign
identical with his natal Navamsa or Rasi position, or
a trine to either of these. In the absence of expected
evils, the paternal uncle will be a part of family
division.
Notes: The words “Kheda Modam” in the first
line of sloka 1106 should actually read as “Kheda
Bhedam” meaning griefand enmity (or division etc.)
which are part of the last sub period, of Rahu, in the
dasa of Jupiter. Because happiness is something rare
in this phase of Vimsottari which can be verified in
practical horoscopes.

1108. (a) Jupiter’s Dasa: When the dasa of
Jupiter happens to be the dasa opening at birth, the
effects stated for Gemini ascendant will bear truth
and the father will receive auspicious effects. Butthe
spouse of a paternal uncle will fall sick; grief to
relatives will come to pass - both these effects will
occur in the sub period of Rahu in the dasa of Jupiter.
(b) The Luminaries’ Bhuktis: The sub period
of the Suninthesaid dasa will inthe commencingand
concluding phase cause mental distress, and the
(whole of) sub period of the Moon troubles.

तत्द्धावविनाशादि योजयेत् कालवित्तमः॥११०९ ॥
1109. Astrologers declare destruction ofa Bhava
when the lord of the 8th thereof is involved thus -
Saturn should transit the sign identical with the
Navamsa position of the said planet.

1110 - 1112. Isaana Nadiamsa - Gemini Ascendant: The person will enjoy a Yoga leading to
prosperity in his eleventh year. His marriage will
occur in the firstdasa. He will be very intelligent and
his father will prosper. During his second dasa, he
will firmly involve himself in the study of the Vedas.
He will learn Sastras. His coborn will get married.
The king will lose his spouse(?)
अन्तरान्तर्दशामारगे प्रस्ताराष्टकवर्गके |
ईशानांशे प्रजातस्य फलं विस्ताप्मादिशेत् ॥१११३॥
1113. Using the sub periods and the Ashtaka
Varga method, results for this native of Isaana
Nadiamsa, Gemini ascendant, be declared in detail.
तुलागुरुदशारम्भे तुलाचन्द्रे च संस्थिते |
स्वभृक्तौ व्याकुलं चेति स्वपितुर्मित्रवर्गके ॥१११४॥
1114. If Jupiter is placed in Libra at birth (in the
Sth), then the transit Moon found in Libra at the
moment of commencement of Jupiter’s dasa will
cause distress for the friends of father, in the first sub
period (of Jupiter).
Notes: The exact moment when a dasa or sub
period begins will reveal many important hints about
the events to come by in that dasa or sub period.
Detailed tables and method of such calculations
could be found in my English translation of Hora
Sara, by Pnthu Yasas. Forwant ofspace these are not
being reproduced here.

1117. Paternal grand-mother will be destroyed
when Saturn comes in transit in the sign identical with
the natal Navamsa position of Jupiter, or a trine
thereof.

1118. Paternal grand-mother’s sister or brother
will be destroyed in the native’s first dasa when
Jupiter’s natal Navamsa position’s trine is in transit
by Saturn.
Notes: “Jasyaah” is replaced by “Sahodare vaa

ate fate सध्राधिपतिभुक्तिषु।

1119. Danger to Father: During the 32nd year,
(or) in the sub period of 8th lord (Saturn) (in the dasa
of Saturn itself), the native of Gemini ascendant will
see his father in danger. The dasa of the 4th lord will
cause evils to maternal relatives.
लग्नाद्रा चद््रलगनाद्रा सुखेशांश त्रिकोणगे |
स्फुटयोगं गते मन्दे मातृनाशं विनिर्दिशेत् ॥११२० ॥
सहोदरभ्रातृशोकं कर्मेशांशत्रिकोणगे ।
स्फुटयोगप्रकारेण योजयेत् कालवित्तमः॥११२१ ॥
1120-1121. Destruction of Mother & Coborn:
When Saturn comes to the sign identical with the
Navamsa position of the lord of the 4th counted from
the natal ascendant or the Moon sign, or to a trine to
one of the said Navamsa positions, the person’s
mother will face her end. Similarly brothers will be
destroyed when Saturn’s transit involves as above the
Navamsa position of the lord of the 10th (Jupiter).
तत्तद्धावाष्टमेशांशद्रेकाणे द्रादशां शके ।
स्फुटयोगं गते मन्दे तत्तद्भावविनाशनम् ॥११२२ ॥
स्फुराशकं गते जीवे तत्तद्भावेमहत् सुखम् |
भावाभिवृद्धिमापनोति योजयेत् कालवित्तमः॥११२३ ॥
1122 - 1123. (a) Special Rule of Transit: In
respect of a Bhava, note the Navamsa, Drekkana and
Dwadasamsa positions of the lord of the eighth
therefrom. When Saturn transits in a sign identical
with any of these divisional positions, the Bhava in
question shall be destroyed.
(b) Jupiter (instead of Saturn) in transit in an
above place will cause great happiness in respect of
the Bhava, and the Bhava effects will progress.
Notes: (a) For example, take the 2nd house in
case of Capricorn ascendant. The 8th therefrom (i.e.
Virgo) is ruled by Mercury. The Navamsa, Drekkana
and Dwadasamsa positions of Mercury be noted.
And when Saturn comes to the Rasi identical with one
of these divisional positions of Mercury, the indications of the 2nd house (viz. family, finance, eyes etc.)
will be destroyed.
(b) Conversely when Jupiter transits such a
position relating to Mercury, the 2nd house will
reveal its effects favourably and ina great measure.
कर्मृशांश्रिकोणे वा कर्मेशद्रादशांशे |
स्फुटयोगं गते मन्दे राज्याधिपतिनाशनम् ॥११२४॥
सुतेशांशत्रिकोणक्षे दरेक्काणांशे च राशिगे ।
Spear गते मन्दे लोके दुरभिक्षज भयम् ॥११२५॥
कर्मेशांशत्रिकोणे वा गोचरेऽमरपूजिते ।
राजा राष्ट सुखं चेव दु्क्षभयनाशनम् ॥११२६ |
1124 - 1126. Irrelevant: Saturn moving in the
sign identical with the Navamsa or Dwadasamsa of
the 10th Jord, ora trine to one of these, will destroy
the ruler. Famine will result the transit of the said
planet in a sign identical with the Rasi, Navamsa, or
Drekkana position of the 5th lord, ora trine to one of
these three places. When Jupiter transits the sign
identical with the Navamsa position of the 10th lord,
or a trine thercof, happiness for the king and the
country will exist, destroying the fear of famine.

1127 - 11281/2. One born in Isaana Nadiamsa,
Gemini ascendant will be fortunate and happy from
birth to death. He will have fear of untimely death in
the end of the 4th dasa. The 5th dasa during the sub
period of the eighth lord will bring his timely death.
His death will be in the 67th year. He may take to
ascetism following a family calamity.
कमलांशे सुते मन्दे भूसुतेन निरीक्षिते ॥११२९॥
युग्मलग्ने प्रजातस्य बाल्ये मातृविनाशनम् |

placed in Libra’s Kamala Nadiamsa, tn aspect to
Mars, will destroy mother in the native’s childhood.
Further he will be devoid of a coborn, but will have
older 6000. Two of his sisters will be long-lived.
His father will have two wives.

बुधक्षेत्रे कुजांशस्थे मन्दे कुजनिरीश्षित ॥११३५॥

1172 -11731/2. The Kala Chakra Dasa periods of
the Rasis denoted by the sign, decanate and Navamsa
occupied by the 8th lord (Saturn) will give grief and

Notes: Here we get one of the most precious
clues in the direction of using KCD effectively.
The. dasa periods denoted by decanate or Navamsa occupied by a certain planet - good or bad - will
give good or bad effects. For example 10th lord ina
given case is in his exaltation decanate or exaltation
Navamsa. The dasa indicated by the Rasi concerned
as per KCD will give excellent effects concerning the
10th house.
Similarly for other Bhavas. This clue can be
applied for the various ascendants.

1183 - 1187. (a) Saturn should be placed in
Kamala Nadiamsa in Libra so that death of father
occurs inthe first sub period of the dasa of the 2nd lord
(Moon). Failing which grief to (full) sister, and stepbrother will show up. Urinary disorders (to self) and
marriage of the native’s son will follow.
(b) The first sub period of the 5th dasa will cause
prosperity to the native in his birth country.
(c) Saturn moving in Virgo, during the first sub
period or that of Mercury in the fifth dasa will
endanger the father. If not, the period will destroy a
step-brother.
(d) The sub period of Saturn in the fifth dasa will
cause the death of a brother-in-law during Saturn’s
movements in Virgo.
Notes: "Kanya Chakre" should read as "Kanya
Sanau", meseems, to have a meaningful instruction
as noted in “c” and “d” above.

1188 - 1189. General: Even a single powerful
yoga for prosperity will be capable of giving wealth,
will incessantly cause the progress of coborns and
will give an excess of money and red robes. The
wealth will be acquired by the powers invested by the
ruler. The person will beget a powerful position of
rendering justice. He will give happiness to men. His
entire household will spill with wealth and grains.
With the advent of Jupiter’s dasa these effects will be
specific.
Notes: Since the author has taken the verses for
Gemini ascendant, Kamala Nadiamsa, from Guru
Nadi, specific importance is laid on Jupiter’s dasa. It
is commonly to be understood that any relevant dasa
will give the good effects cited, the sum total of which
is wealth, position, happiness et cetera.

1191 - 1194102. Death: The native of Kamala
Nadiamsa, Gemini ascendant, will die with Saturn
moving in Cancer. But Saturn moving in the end of
Capricom, or in Aquarius, with the Sun in Leo, will
usher fear of untimely death. In Scorpio, Saturn in
transit, will cause pains of the body, and destruction
of men equal to the subject (in age). Ina trine to
Scorpio (i.e. in Pisces/Cancer) Saturn’s movement,
obtaining at the same time the Navamsa of Cancer,

canalso cause timely (insurmountable) death. Saturn
in Cancer in transit will cause death. Probability of
death will also exist with his movement in the end of
Virgo, orin the end of Libra, in the month denoted by
Sun in Virgo. The subject of Kamala Nadiamsa will
pass away in one of the three months commencing
Leo (i.e. the transit Sun in Leo through Libra), in a
dark fortnight, on a Wednesday, due to dysentery.

Sanau etc. and the translation is for suggested corrections. This is done in order to keep a flow with the
sequence. In some places these of course refer to
respective Kala Chakra Dasa periods.

1195 - 11981/2. (a) Aquarius Ascendant -
Champaka Nadiamsa: The lord of the ascendant
(Saturn) with Ketu in the 6th house, with Sagittarius
becoming the Aroodha ascendant (as per Jaimini
school), will make the native wealthy right from birth
and happy.
(b) Jupiter in Aroodha Ascendant: In this yoga
if Jupiter also happens to be in the Aroodha ascendant, Sagittarius, the person will be wicked, honourable, will be the leader of bad men, be interested in
achieving difficult things, fair-complexioned, beautiful-eyed, be a king, minister, leader of an army, be
highly honoured, soft-bodied and very intelligent.
He will be endowed with all kinds of wealth, be of
peaceful disposition, will indulge in various kinds of
charities and be a lord.
Notes: (a) For Aquanius ascendant, its lord Saturn in the 6th therefrom in Cancer causes Sagittarius
to become the Aroodha ascendant. Saturn and Ketu
Chandra Kala Nadi
inthe 6th (Upachaya house) will thus give riches right
from birth, and the subject will enjoy happiness in
general.
(b) With the combination cited at “a” being fully
present, the Aroodha ascendant’s lord Jupiter in
Sagittarius itself will produce the additional effects
listed. The only adversity is wicked disposition.
Also see slokas 1203-1205 infra for a modified
version.
For Champaka Nadiamsa details, refer to notes
under 8018 1412. Book I.

भुपालसेवी स्वकुले प्रसिद्धः॥१२०२॥
1201 - 1202. Champaka Nadiamsa - First
Half: Birth in this space in Aquarius (without reference to Saturn etc.) will make the native famous in
royal assembly. He will be an expert in giving
advices related to use of Mantras (sacred spells), and
be dear to the ruler. He will be able to understand
others’ thoughts by their mere gestures, be intelligent, will enjoy powers bestowed upon him by the
king and will receive great respect and reverence. He
will be endowed with longevity, will serve the ruler
and be famous among the men of his race.
Note: “Sripankajamse” meaning birth in
Sripankaja Nadiamsa in sloka 1202 is an unwanted
addition here oritshould read as “SriChampakamse”.
It was a custom for ancient writers to add the prefix
“Sri” to auspicious Nadiamsas and such other terms.
Any way the results mentioned in the sec~ .J half of
sloka 1202 should also belongto Champaka Nadiamsa
when carefully compared with the rest.

1203 - 1205. Aquarius ascendant, Saturn in
Cancer, Jupiter in Sagittarius in aspect to Venus and
simultaneously getting Leo Navamsa - together these
will cause a Raja Yoga, giving three conveyances.
The person will have some fear from mean people.
During his childhood he will incur disorders caused
by indigestion. Although irascible, he could be
instantly pleased and be charming in appearance.
(Breaking the flow, Saturn in 6th should not be
isolated to cause charming appearance. This position
is part of the Aroodha ascendant condition.) He will
be ever valorous, will have a bilious and windy body
(prone to disorders like abdominal illness, premature
gray hair, yellow sweating, jaundice, rheumatism,
and arthritis), and will enjoy pleasures of the world.
Notes: The Navamsa position in Leo is. for
Jupiter in the sign Sagittarius. That is Jupiter should
be between 13 deg 20' and 16 deg 40' of Sagittarius.
Ketu’s position is excluded and Venus is introduced in this yoga - a different version of what is
given in “b”, 1195 - 11981/2 supra.
“Enjoying pleasures of the world” shows a high
degree of material enjoyments accruing to the subject.

1220 - 1224. Results Concerning Father: The
Moon in Swaaha Nadiamsa will give following effects to the father of the native belonging to former
half of Champaka Nadiamsa, Aquarius. The father
will be a righteous person, wealthy and will be
affectionate to living beings. (Jeevapreetikarah need
not be interpreted as: pleasing the planet Brihaspathi.)
He will religiously recite the Rudra Japa (prayer to
Rudra, Lord Siva), be an expert in righteous and
meritorious acts, and be learned in Puranas (Hindu
mythology), and will construct temples, villages,
Brahmins’ colonies and tanks. In the middle part of
his life, fortunes will abound. He will begeta position
of authority under the king, will become an army
head, minister and will involve in political administration of the country. He will gain happiness in his
country and be enthusiastic in destroying the wicked
(or unlawful). His wealth will be acquired through
various means. He will accomplish an ५ ५ ,ertaking
with suitable expedients, and will be a skillful
gatherer of wealth. His fame will extend to many
countries during the early days of the native.

1263 - 1264. Karma Samrajya Yoga for Immovable Ascendant: The lord of the 10th, positioned in an angle to the Moon, and aspected by the
lords of the 2nd and 11th (both counted from natal
ascendant) will cause Karma Samrajya Yoga. Asa
result, the person will be rich, valorous, widely
famous and will be wealthy with a lot of possessions.
He will adopt a virtuous path and will serve the king
and his minister. He will be lustful, be ever righteous
and will enjoy much pleasures.
Notes: The yoga given above applies to an
immovable ascendant, viz. one of Taurus, Leo, Scorpio and Aquarius.
Example: Take Taurus as the ascendant. The
lord of the tenth from ascendant is Saturn. Lords of
‘2ndand 11th in orderare Mercury and Jupiter. Hence
the yoga is formed thus for this ascendant - Saturn in
the 10th from the Moon sign, and in joint aspect to
Mercury and Jupiter.
This yoga may not exactly apply to Leo whose
2nd and 11th lord is Mercury. Here instead of his
aspecting Venus, the 10th lord, it should be relaxed
to be a conjunction.

1270 - 1278. Aquarius Ascendant - Champaka
Nadiamsa: (a) The 2nd dasa will give sadness ina
limited way while the 4th dasa will cause much grief
(in terms of family deaths).
(b) Sakata Yoga: Due to Sakata Yoga the dasa
of 10th lord will give some distress.
(c) Fourth Dasa: The 4th dasa will cause increase of prosperity, but the first sub period will
indicate much distress. The said sub period will
promote enmity with the king and Vaishnavites
(those belonging to the school of Lord Vishnu).
Gradually prosperity will be on the increase from the
middle of the 4th dasa. In this dasa, there will be a
family marriage, but children born (to the subject)
will be destroyed. His wife will incur evils.
(d) Jupiter’s Dasa: In the sub period of the lord
of the ascendant, during the dasa of the 2nd lord
(Jupiter) the person will acquire wealth, grains and’

ornaments. He will visit pilgrimage centers and
beget auspicious effects. In the sub period ruled by
the lord of the 8th (Mercury), he will incur physical
ailments. His spouse and progeny will face distress.
After a while happiness will revive. The sub period
of Ketu will destroy the wealth gathered. In the
months denoted by adverse solar transits, in the said
Ketu bhukti, he will incur fear from king, thieves and
fire. The sub period of the 9th lord (Venus) will
increase prosperity apart from happy events relating
to progeny; but there will be evils in the family of inJaws. In the Sun’s sub period due to royal assignments (rather wrath), he will have to leave the
country. More specially in the sub period of the
Moon. Further in the Moon’s phase, he will have
illnesses of the body.

1288 - 12931/2. Damini Yoga: (a) There should
be Damini yoga in the horoscope while the ascendant
lord Saturn is in extremis (Mritavastha). Such a
person will beget much fortunes in his early childhood. He will be somewhat dusky in complexion, be
crafty, an expert in use of weapons, a poet, be
honourable and will have many brothers and many
sisters. Out of the many sisters, all will die (prematurely) but one. He will have only one daughter
whom he will get married in 25th year. During 20th
year, his trial for her marriage will face obstacles due
to his (earlier) sins. His father will die en route during
his (native’s) 22nd year. During his 16th year he will
have abundance (of everything). During his 20th
year he will lose his mother in a foreign land.
(b) With Damini Yoga, Saturn placed in the 6th
with debility in Amsa (not Navamsa but other applicable Aries divisions) will cause a long-lasting fever
for the mother of the native. He will lose his father
during his boyhood and will incur much grief.
(c) Kedara-Damini Yoga will give wealth in the
16th year. After30 prosperity will be on the rise. His
happiness follow the destruction of his relatives.
Notes: (a) Damini yoga as per standard prescription occurs whenall the 7 planets are insixsigns. This
is a Sankhya Yoga. Saturn also being a part of the
yoga, Should be in Mritavastha, that is in the last six
degrees of an odd sign, or in the initial six degrees of
an even sign.
(b) In this Damini Yoga, Saturn instead of being
in Mritavastha, should be in Cancer with Aries
divisions.
(c) Kedara-Daminiis not one yoga. Kedara yoga
occurs when all the 7 planets are in four signs.
Apparently the author had some thing else in mind or
there is some compositional error.

1336 - 1340. Mudrika Nadiamsa - Former
Half: (a) One born ina fixed sign in this space of the
zodiac, with the Moon in Sumathi Nadiamsa, and
Jupiter in Mohana Nadiamsa will lose (one of) his
older brother(s). His elder brother will be quite a
righteous person while father will never be relieved
of debts. The subject will be endowed with two sons,
be honourable, will worship Lord Siva, will honour
the words of his father and will commitsome sins due
to ignorance. Due to the curse of a Brahmin in an
earlier birth, he will be troubled by tuberculosis.
(b) Many Deaths in 3rd Dasa: He will die in his
third dasa following the death of his father. He will
incur grief on account of the destruction of his elder
brother. His step mother, wife and two paternal
uncles will also pass away (during this dasa).

1364 - 1369. Mohana Nadiamsa - Fixed Sign:
(a) Ketu in Aries with the Sun will restrict the number
of issues. The subject will obtain a son in his fourth
dasa.
(b) The Sun in the 5th in aspect to or company
of Mars will prove an obstacle in respect of acquisition of progeny. Anyway, after a long interval, ason
will be obtained. As per Garga, this yoga will kill the
issues as and when they are born. To overcome this
evil, a Linga (idol of Lord Siva, or of Naga Raja, the
king of Cobras) made of gold be given away in
charity.
(c) Progeny’s life-span will be increased by
aonation of a golden (Naga) Linga if Saturn is in the
Sth house from the natal ascendant 1n the company of
the Sun, and begets a Navamsa ruled by Jupiter. The
suggested donation of Naga Linga should be givenon
a Monday evening (Pradosha). In the absence of this
remedy, the person will beget many daughters while
sons born to him will only find their instant exit from
this mortal world.
Notes: (c) Pradosha = the first part of the night;
evening personified as a son of Dosha, Blemish, and
associated with Nisitha and Vyushta. Vyushta is daybreak personified and is the son of Pushparna and
Dosha, or the son of Vubhavasu and Ushas.

1374 - 1378. Mohana Nadiamsa - Fixed Sign:
With the Moon in Libra with Pisces Navamsa while
Jupiter and Ketu are in the sign Scorpio, following
results will come to pass. The first two dasas will give
happiness to the subject. His mother’s sister will be
endowed with longevity. His maternal grand-father

Notes: From sloka 1443 to 1508, there is confusion in the original as to which ascendant these
discussions belong to. Moreover, along witha giver

additions are corrupt and hence excluded from translation as and when necessary. As far as possible, 1
tried to sort out these contradictions and explainina
suitable manner.
Generally these slokas belong to Aquanius ascendant, according to my understanding. The reader
will find clear reference to Maalini Nadiamsa,
Aquarius ascendant, in slokas 1505 - 6 infra.

of paternal happiness. His mother will live with him
(i.e. not with the father) and he will enjoy fraternal
happiness. He will have two honourable brothers and
one auspicious sister. Another sister will be in
troubles. The person will be grieved on account of
loss ofa son. He will be excellent among his co-bom
and his progeny will prosper. To overcome the
blemish in respect of progeny the native should
donate a specified plant called "Sisumaara", (whose
scientific name is Delphinus Gangeticus) 1 «wing
which his children will be blessed with longevity.
Note: Sisumaara also means a collection of stars
resembling a dolphin and held to be dear to Lord
Vishnu. The north-east point in the Heavens is also
called Sisumaara.
The plant in question seems to be in wide use in
traditional medicine and seems to grow abundantly in
Gangetic plains.

1588 - 1592. (a) Dead Children: Saturn in the

effects due to transits should be enumerated with the
help of directional influences.

acquisition of wealth and a life-partner.

general to the various ascendants and not confined
only to the current Nadiamsa.

1657 - 1658. Sickness: He will fall sick in the

Notes: Setting in the heavens means remaining

Deva Keralam
मुदररंशे विष्णुयोगे wat मोहनांशके |
विष्णुभक्तिरतो नित्यं मध्वाचारप्रियः सुखी ॥१६७९ ॥
1679. One born in the said Nadiamsa, having
Venus in Mohana Nadiamsa will ever be devoted to
Lord Vishnu. He will follow the school of
Madhvacharya.
Notes: Madhvacharya was the founder of a sect
of Vaishnavites in the south of India. He was a
Kanarese Brahmin called Ananda Tirtha
Bhagavthpada, or Madhu, said to have been born
about 1200 A.D. His doctrine is commonly called
Dvaita - i. e. “Duality” - (in opposition to the
“Advaita”, i.e. Non-duality of the great Vedanthist
Sankaracharya) and his sectarians are called Madhvas.

asynonym, “Kshitija” - born of the earth. Perhaps the
text means that Venus should be with Mars and be in

the supposed version.

therefromand so onand so forth. The 13th, 25th, 37th
and 49th of a sign will again be ruled by the lord of
that sign itself.
Example: The 1st Shastiamsa of Aries is ruled by
Mars, the 2nd one by Venus, the 3rd one by Mercury,
the 4th one by the Moon and so on and so forth.

the dasa of Jupiter and in the sub period Rahu, when
Venus moves in the 5th one will acquire money. But
the gains will be limited. If Rahu moves in the 8th
from the natal Jupiter, the dasa lord, or in the 12th
fromthe ascendant, the person and his paternal uncle
will acquire physical ailments. Yet, bis kinsfolk will
in general beget auspicious effects and celebrate
birth of children. In the months denoted by adverse
solar transits, in the said sub period, the subject will
be troubled by fever.
संपत्ताराधिपे जीवे दशायां राहुभुकितषु ॥ १७३२ ॥
HEART प्रजातस्य शुभाशुभफलं समम्
विक्रमेशदशांकालेमन्दांशग्रहभुकितषु ॥ १७३३ ॥
अतीव क्लेशकालः स्याद्भ्रातृपलीप्रपीडिता ।
भाग्ये जीवे तुलालग्ने दशायां राहुभुकितषु ॥ १७२४ ॥
दशान्ते सोदरभ्रातृसन्तानोत्मतित्ादिशेत् ।
पितृव्यस्य तथा विन्दाग्दर्भाधानमथापि च ॥ १७३५ ॥
पुण्यतीर्थफलप्रापितभ्रातृवर्े विशेषतः |
1732 - 17351/2. (a) 2nd Dasa - Rahu Bhukti:
Jupiter becoming the lord of the 2nd dasa will give a
mix of good and bad results in the sub period of Rahu,
in case of the native of Mohana Nadiamsa, Libra
ascendant.
(b) Saturn’s Navamsa: The sub period of a
planet occupying Saturn’s Navamsa will give extremely inauspicious results when the dasa of Jupiter

comes to rule the native. Additionally his sister-inlaw will also be in trouble.
(c) Rahu’s Bhukti: In case of Libra ascendant,
see if Jupiter is placed in the 9th (in Gemini). Then
the sub period of Rahu in the dasa of the said Jupiter,
will in its concluding phase, destroy the progeny of
the native’s brother being then born. So also in
respect of the progeny of his paternal uncle. This
phase could also destroy the prospects of their nuptials. - The persons’ relatives will be on visits to
shrines and acquire merits.
कुटिलावस्थगे मन्दे तुलांशे कुजवीक्षिते ॥ १७३६ ॥

कुतिसतप्रियवान् रागी कुमार्मप्रभुसेवकः।
स्व राज्यनृपतिक्रोधी पाप दुर्मागं मानतः॥ १७३८ । ।
Tent greta जौवहिंखाप्रदो भवेत् |
स्वप्रभोच्प गुणो वाच्यः कटिलावस्थगे शनौ ॥ १७३९ ॥
1736 - 1739. (a) Libra Navamsa: In case of
Libra ascendant, Saturn in aspect to Mars and obtaining Libra Navamsa will make the person given
inwardly to anger. He will be a pleased soul and will
have enmity with his kinsfolk.
(b) Scorpio Navamsa: The natal ascendant being Libra with Scorpio Navamsa will give following
effects if Saturn occupies Scorpio Rasi. He will be
attached to vile people, be passionate, will serve a
king who takes to bad ways, will incur the wrath of
the king, bea sinnerand will take to bad ways. He will
suffer from urinary disorders, be emaciated and will
torture living beings. He will sing the praise of his
king.
Notes: (a) Kutilavastha is not a standard term
ref.sloka 1736, and sloka 1739.

Chandra Kala Nadi
1740 - 17411/2. Kulaghna Shashtiamsa: The
blemish of Satur being in Kulaghna Shashtiamsa
will cause sickness to the native’s sister-in-law and
her son. Saturn in this Shashtiamsa in aspect to two
malefics will cause evils to the progeny of his brother
and paternal uncle. In the dasa of such Saturn,
children of kinsfolk will be destroyed. The native
will remarry and obtain auspicious effects.
Notes: Kulaghna Shashtiamsa extends from 3°
30' to 4° 0' of an odd sign, and from 26° 0' to 26° 30!
of anevensign. Kulaghna means ruining the family.
Generally a relevant planet in this Shashtiamsa will
be a stumbling block in obtaining progeny.
सुराशनाडिगे सूर्ये बुधे मेषागाशगे ॥ १७४२ ॥
विष्णुयोगे प्रजातस्य प्परविशात् परं सुखम् ।
लग्नशांशपदोश्षेण मोहनांशोध्रवस्य च ॥ १७५३ ॥

धने मन्दैष्टमे भोमे पष्ठरधिपतिदायके ॥ १७४४ ॥
केकयोगफलं चेति महदैश्वर्व्यषणिडतम् |
भाग्येशाशपयोगेन पच्चरविशतिवत्सरे ॥ १७४५ ॥
एकोनविशतो चैव राजप्रीतिधनागमम् |
स्वज्येष्ठप्रातृयोगेन स्वस्थानप्रापितमादिशेत् ॥ १७४६ ॥
स्वराजदर्शनं चेति नित्यं सन्तोषमादिशेत् ।
1742 - 17461/2. (a) Sun - Mercury: The Sun in
Sura Nadiamsa and Mercury in Melaapa (or Melaa,
also Melapavarga) Nadiamsa will cause happiness to
the Libra native after the age of 25.
(b) Venus’s Navamsa Dispositor: Due to the
blemish of the Navamsa dispositor of ascendant lord
Venus, the native of Mohana Nadiamsa will be
grieved on account of displacement for eight years.
(c) Saturn in the 2nd and Mars in the 8th will give
effects of what is called “Keka Yoga” and will
destroy (even) great wealth.
(d) Mercury’s Navamsa Dispositor: Saturn
joining the Navamsa dispositor of 9th lord Mercury
will cause wealth through royal favours in the 25th
year or in the 19th year. With the help of his older
brother, he will resume living in his native place.
(This effect should be linked to “b” above.) Further
he will visit the king and will be ever happy.

जीवे TER काले मन्दे मन्दान्तरेपि च ॥ १७४९ ॥

याम्यायने मोहनांशे गर्भाधानं च स्वसित्रयाम् |
पितृव्यस्य सुताप्रापितर्दशान्ते सोदरस्य च ॥ १७५१॥
सन्तानात्पतितमित्याहूर्बालाररिष्ट भयं कचित् ।
1747 - 17511/2. (a) Acquisition of Son: The
person belonging to Libra ascendant when Saturn
transits the sign indicated by his own natal Navamsa
position, or a trine thereof, will obtain birth of a son.
(b) In the beginning or ending of Saturn’s dasa,
when Saturn joins (in transit) the (natal sign position
of) 6th lord (i.e. Jupiter) or the (natal sign position of)
Navamsa dispositor of 9th lord, there will be visit to
a Shrine and (consequent) acquisition of a son.
(“Punyatheerthe Suthotsavam” means birth of a son
in a shrine. There is another version which 908
“Punyatheertham Suthotsavam”. The rendering
above 15 made keeping in view the following hints.)
(c) Bathing in Sethu: In the sub period of Rahu
during the dasa of Jupiter, or in the first sub period of
Saturn’s dasa, the native of Libra ascendant will have
dips in the Sethu and will acquire a son.
(d) One born in Mohana Nadiamsa, Libra ascendant, will have his first impregnation ceremony
with Saturn moving in Libra, during the Sun’s southerly course. His paternal uncle will also obtain
progeny. In the end ofthe dasa, his brother will beget
progeny. Children thus born will be somewhat
inflicted with Balarishta effects (infantile mortality).

1752 - 17551/2. (a) The native of Libra ascendant
having Mercury in Melaa Nadiamsa with the Sun in
the 10th will obtain a daughter through first wife.
(b) Jupiter in the 9th while the Moon is in the 5th
will cause birth of a male issue. The first wife will
give birth to three children. One of them will die.
(c) Saturn placed at birth in Kulaghna Shashtiamsa
will in his dasa, during the sub period of sine will
kill the first wife of the native of Libra ascendant.
There will however be fear of untimely death in the
sub period of Mercury and troubles from windy
disorders to the first wife. She will also incur
problems of indigestion. Suitable remedies (for
Mercury’s sub period) should be sought.
धन मन्दे सुते शुक्रे Aas संयुते ॥ १७५६ ॥
दारेशभावदोषेण ज्येष्ठभार्या विश्यति |
दोषपरिहारार्थं AH प्रपूजयेत् ॥ १७५७ ॥
देव ज्ञाय प्रदानेन दारारिषटीन विद्यते ।
1756 - 17571/2. Blemish to the Seventh House:
Incase of Libra native, Saturn in the 2nd while Venus
is in the Sth (Aquarius) with Capricorn Navamsa
causes blemish to the 7th house indication. As a
result, the person’s first wife will be destroyed.
Remedy lies in worshipping the image of the zodiac;
giving donations to the astrologer will ward off the
possible evils to the said spouse.
शोभनांशकगे भोमे तुलालग्नष्दव्स्य च ॥ १७५८ ॥
AAA कलत्र दरे दारन्तरसुख महत्

1758 - 17601/2. Spouses & Progeny: Fora Libra
native, Mars in Sobhana Nadiamsa will give two

long-living wives. The person will be happy after
second marriage. Both the wives will yield offspring.
He will have a total of seven children. Five of them
will be endowed with long life-spans. Five daughters
and (two) sons will be had by the native. These
effects will come to pass in the dasa of the lord of the
Sth (Saturn). Further he will enjoy effects of Raja
Yoga (due to the said position of Mars) and will enjoy
happiness on account of co-born.
Notes: “Aayushmathi Kalatre Dwe” is replaced
in another version by “Kalatradwayamaayushyam”
denoting two long-living wives. Similarly, the expression “Chiraayushaastheshu...” is replaced by
“Panchasamkhyaa chiraayushyam” hinting at five
long-living children.
SAMI मन्दे दशायां च स्वभक्तिषु ॥ १७६१ ॥
एजयोगं च लभते स्वस्थाने बहुभग्यवान् ।
लाभेशे Tra WET भृक्तिकाल महत् सुखम ॥ १७६२ ॥
बहुप्लसुवाहापितं पुत्रलाभं सुशोभनम् ।
भाग्येशनाडियोगेन कर्मेशांशदशा शुभा ।

1761 - 1763. (a) Navamsa of 10th Lord: If
Satur occupies the Navamsa occupied by the 10th
lord Moon (i.e. Satur and the Moon should be
together in navamsa), the dasa and sub period of
Satum will produce Raja Yoga (prosperity). The
person will make his fortunes in his native region.
(b) The sub period of the lord of the Navamsa
occupied by the 11th lord (Sun) will give great
happiness (in the dasa of Satu). Further, he will
procure many precious jewels, valuable conveyances, offspring and highly auspicious effects.
(c) One will attain merits due to numerous
charitable activities and will involve in repairing
dilapidated public places in the dasa of 10th lord’s
Navamsa dispositor (apparently Saturn dasa, vide
notes under “a”) provided Mercury, the 9th lord, is in
(the subject) Nadiamsa (that is Sobhana, vide sloka
Notes: (a) “Karmesasthamsage” as appearing in
the text only means that Saturn should be in the same
Navamsa as the 10th lord Moon is. However if the
Chandra Kala Nadi
expressionis “Karmesasyaamsage ”, itmeans Saturn
should be in the Navamsa owned by the 10th lord,
Moon, that is Cancer. The former as appearing in the
original seems to be more logical.
(b) “Yannavamsasthe Bhuktikaale” is replaced
by “Yannavamsesa Bhuktikaale” in another version.
The rendering - the sub period of the Navamsa
dispositor of the Sun - is for the second suggestion.
The first one is somewhat inconclusive.
(c) The conditions laid in sloka 1763 are not
straight but crooked. These are brought out in the
translation after some experience with the mind of
the author. One may or may not agree with me.

1764 - 1769. (a) Mercury in Melaapa Nadiamsa
causing Vishnu Yoga will in the Sth lord’s dasa cause
the Libra native perform many charitable activities.
(b) The Sun inthe 10th (Cancer)inSura Nadiamsa
while Mercury is in Melaapa Nadiamsa will produce
excellent charitable activities through the native’s
hands. He will be lustful and will construct a colony
for Brahmins (i.e. will donate houses to deserving
people). After the age of 25, in the Sth lord’s dasa, the
Said native will be happy. Following the 27th year,
one with Vishnu Yoga will be famous. He will make
his fortunes from a new king, and favours from the
queen and be close to the princess.
(c) One born in Mohana Nadiamsa, Libra as-

Deva Keralam
cendant will obtain a powerful position under the
king in the dasa of the 4th lord, Saturn. (Vishnu Yoga
seems to be a redundant condition.)
(d) In the sub period of Venus, the ascendant
lord, that of Mercury, the 9th lord, and thatof the 10th
lord Moon, the person will enjoy great prosperity (in
Saturn’s dasa).
Notes: (a) Vishnu Yoga is a redundant term
which appears in many places in this part. In my
opinion this has no relevance.
विपत्ताराधिपेन मन्दे स्वनवांशे द्वितीये ।

तद्रदशायां फलं योज्यं योगांशग्रहभुक्तिषु ।
1770 - 17701/2. Dasa of Saturn: Saturn should
be in aspect to Mars, and should be inthe sign Scorpio
with Capricorn/Aquarius Navamsa. Lastly heshould
be the ruler of the third dasa (the first being that of
Rahu). The result ofall these is gaining lordship over
treasury, wealth and precious jewels in the sub period
of the planet that is placed in a favourable Navamsa.
Notes: “Yogamsa graha bhuktishu” being a
round-about term is provided witha feasible meaning
as above.

1771 -17771/2. (8) Adversities relating toa given
Bhava will occur when transit Saturn reaches or
aspects the 81 thereof.
(b) Evils to one’s kinsmen will result the transit
of Saturn in the sign identical with the Navamsa
occupied by the 8th lord at birth, ora trine thereof, or
in the sign that is aspected by the said 8th lord.
Diseases, royal wrath and intense difficulties are also
predicted by the astrologers in this context with
(Saturn’s movement in the) sign occupied oraspected
by the lord of the 8th (Venus), ora trine thereof (will
be revealing) correlated with adverse months and
years (1.6. adverse transits of the Sun and Jupiter in
order).
(c) Agiven Bhava’s effects will be destroyed by
transit Saturn in the sign or Navamsa occupied by the
lord of the 8th from that Bhava, or a trine to either of
these. These effects declared by astrologers in Sarva
Nadi will become true in the dasa periods denoted by
the dispositors of the lord of 2nd, 8th or the 7th. The
sub periods will be of obstructive planets.
लग्नात् साप्तमगे मन्दे स्वरभातुकुजवीश्षिते ॥ १५७८ ॥

1778 - 17791/2. Diseased Spouse: The Libra
native of Mohana Nadiamsa having Saturn in the 7th
in joint aspect to Rahu and Mars will have a sickly
wife who will also be troubled by evil devils (cr
spirits) etc. Jn the absence of these, she will have
obstacles (for obtaining progeny). The person will
anyway be happy and will beget offspring in remarriage.
कलांशाधिपतो जीवे भाग्ये पूर्णबलानिवते ॥ १७८० ॥

1780 - 17821/2. Jupitershould be in the 9th in full

strength and be the lord of “Kalaamsa” (part of a
part). Then the native with Vishnu Yoga will in the
3rd dasa will become wealthy. Due to the good
offices of his father, he will enjoy prosperity from
birth to death. He will ever gain through the fortunes
of his co-born. In the dasa denoted by the lord of
“Kalaamsa” his gains will be restricted and a mix of
good and bad effects will prevail. He will be happy
in the first dasa, more especially in the 3rd dasa.
Notes: Strange terms like “Kalaamsa” and
“Kalaamsesa” appear in this part, defying scope of
translation. We can make some partial gains out of
these instructions as under:
The native of Libra ascendant having a powerful
Jupiter in the 9th house will reap wealth in the 3rd
dasa, and will obtain uninterrupted prosperity with
the help of father. His brothers will be responsible for
his progress.
As for the dasa of restriction, the secret is
unsolvable.
The next clue about Ist and 3rd dasa 15 clear.

1783 - 1788. (a) Saturn with full strength in an
angle to the Moon will in his dasa and sub period
cause great prosperity.
(b) One born in Mohana Nadiamsa, Libra ascendant, will obtain the mercy of the queen. Due to
change of the king, he will obtain wealth in the third
Chandra Kala Nadi
ce 29
dasa. In Saturn’s sub period (“a” above) during
adverse solar transits, he will be somewhat distressed
due to troubles to his spouse. There will be grief in
his in-laws’ famuly, but his brother-in-law will celebrate the birth of a son. The native will beget
auspicious effects in the months coinciding with
auspicious solar transits. In adverse months he will
be banished, will visit shrines and will beget limited
happiness. (Susobhanam is a contradictory term.)
(c) In the 3rd dasa’s first bhukti there will be fear
out of famine.
(d) Jn the dasa of Saturn placed in the 2nd, the
wives of the natives relatives will be subjected to
evils and be destroyed. His brothers will undergo a
family division. The native will however obtain
happiness and jewels (i.e. wealth) through a change
of king.
Notes: (a) Here Saturn is required to be in
“Cheshta Kendra” of the Moon, as per the original
whereas it should yust be “Kendra” or angle from the
Moon. For the term “Cheshta Kendra” does not
apply to the Moon and the Sun

1789 - 1800 1/2. Dasa of Saturn: Saturn in the
2nd in Scorpio, with Aquarius Navamsa will in his
dasa, and the sub period of a malefic, coinciding with
adverse solar transits, cause fever, distress and royal
wrath. The native will visit shrines along with his
king (?) and there will be fear from poison, thieves
and fire for his kinsfolk. The sub period of Ketu will
give these effects (or will be specially revealing as
against other malefic sub periods in the dasa of
Saturn). The sub period of Venus (in the dasa of
Saturn placed as defined) will give a mix of good and
bad effects. The adverse effects will include enmity
with people in the king’s employ. The concluding
part of the sub period of Venus will cause much
distress. The sub period of the Sun will also cause
much distress apart from expenditure beyond resources. The sub periods of the Sun and Venus will
cause diseases of the rectum and sight. In the sub
period of Venus in Saturn dasa, an auspicious function in the family will occur following which great
distress will prevail in adverse months. In the sub
period of the Sun, he will be engaged in royal duties,
and his king will be a victim of great danger. The sub
period of the Moon will give extensive diseases.
Mars sub period will promote marriage of his son,
much auspiciousness, professional advancements and
destruction of enemies. Although the sub period of
the Moon will give some happiness, there will be
perpetual mental strain. The sub period of Mars will
prove auspicious but its end will be distressful. The
sub period of the Moon will endanger the king greatly
apart from troubling.the minister and relatives. Rahu’s sub period will destroy the native’s relatives.
The sub period of Mars will destroy the good effects,
will give happiness after a spate of events like fear
from king and thieves. When Satur moves in transit
in adverse degrees (or Navamsa) during Rahu’s sub
period, one will incur evils to 115 kinsfolk in evil
months apart from distress to paternal uncle, and
destruction of close relatives. Jupiter’s sub period
will give a mix of good and bad effects.
Notes: We can rearrange the whole thing in a
nutshell as under to have a better picture:
In case of Libra ascendant, Saturn being in the
sign Scorpio, between 23° 20' and 26° 40' of arc, with
Aquarius Navamsa, will give the undermentioned
effects in the relevant sub periods.
1. Malefic’s Sub Period - Fever, distress and
professional troubles to self; Danger from poison,
thieves, fire and government to his relatives.
2. Venus’s Sub Period - Good and bad effects in
general, enmity in high circles, diseases of eyes and
anus, auspicious function in the family. The sub
period concludes with distress.
3. Sun’s Sub Period: Distress, expenditure,
diseases of eyes and anus, engagement in government duties.
4. Moon’s Sub Period: Extensive diseases,
happiness in general, but mental stressful.
5. Mars’s Sub Period: Initially troubles through
king, fire and thieves, later on marriage to son,
professional progress, destruction of enemies.
6. Rahu’s Sub Period - Destruction of relatives.
7. Jupiter’s Sub Period - Mix of good and bad
effects.

1801 - 1806 1/2. (a) Dasa of Sth Lord Saturn:
In this dasa, the native of Libra ascendant will obtain
auspicious effects in the family during the sub perods of the lords of 10th (Moon), 11th (Sun), 7th
(Mars) and 9th (Mercury). In the sub period of the
Moon, or in the end of the (Saturn’s) dasa (i.e. last
phase of sub period of Jupiter), orin the sub period of
8th lord Venus, evils to wife, mental distress to self
and to co-born will come to pass. The sub periods of
benefic planets will yield inauspicious effects while
contrary will prevail in the ones ruled by malefics (in
the dasa of the 5th lord Saturn). Jupiter’s sub period
will (also) be inauspicious.
(b) The Moon: Incase of Libra ascendant, see if
the Moon is in Aquarius (in the 5th) with a Navamsa
of Saturn, while Saturnis ina Navamsa of Jupiter. As
a result, the dasa of Saturn will give inauspicious
effects, with intermittent good effects.
(c) If the Moon is found to be in Vargottama
Navamsa in Aquarius, the sub period of Jupiter (in the
dasa of 5th lord) will give sound health and will not
be an obstacle to one’s prosperity.
(d) The Moon ina trine of Aquarius (i.e. Gemini/
Libra) will cause Saturn dasa to be even in respect of
effects. (This relevant clue is made best use of though
it is not as clear as it should be.)
Notes: General guidelines are formulated for
Saturn’s dasa with reference to Libra on the horizon.
The sub periods of Mercury, Sun and Mars will
be favourable. Venus and Moon in their sub periods
will give inauspicious effects. So also Jupiter.
It is significant that the sub periods of benefic
planets, particularly Venus and Jupiter, will be inauspicious in Saturn dasa, while the ones ruled by
malefics, viz. Sun and Mars (also by Rahu and Ketu)
will give good results for a Libra native in Saturn’s
dasa.
However, the text specially makes Mercury
auspicious, by an exception, though he is a benefic.

of loss of co-born.

ora trine thereof, the person will lose his mother. The

लाभेशस्य नवांशेशदशायां धनलाभदा।
राजयोगं विभूषाप्ि पुत्रलाभं सुशोभनम् ॥१८१२ ॥
वार्धके श्रीसुभाग्याप्ति भाग्येशस्य दशशुभा।

1812 - 1813. (a) Financial gains, Raja Yoga
(prosperity), acquisition of ornaments, progeny and
auspicious effects will result in the dasa of the

भाग्ये जीवे प्रजातस्य भाग्याधिपतिदाये।

Deva Keralam
राजामात्यप्रसादेन धनलाभं विनिर्दिशेत् ॥१८१४॥
1814. Jupiter in 9th: The dasa of the 9th lord
(Mercury) will cause financial gains through royal
ministers’ mercy provided Jupiter is in the 9th
(Gemini).
Notes: In “b” at sloka 1812-1813, Mercury dasa
is said to be auspicious in case of Libra ascendant as
for marriage of the native’s son. Further grandchildren will be born to the subject according to his
age.
But in the current sloka, an essential condition is
laid down for Mercury dasa to be favourable to the
subjectin terms of financial gains - that Jupitershould
be in the 9th at birth. This means with Jupiter in the
9th, the Libra native’s Mercury dasa will not be so
helpful for progress.
Jupiter is one of the most adverse planets for
Libra ascendant. Mercury is also the lord of the 121
though simultaneously of 9th, and can cause some
mischief in his dasa at some stage or the other. To
counter his partial evil propensities, anequally tough
evil lord is needed. And that is none other than
Jupiter.
This kind of hidden clues in the Nadi texts stand
apart and prove quite helpful in analysis ofa practical
horoscope.
Insloka 1816-1817, forexample, Mercuryas the
12th lord is said to be dangerous forthe progeny of the
native in his own dasa and own sub period. The text
does not call Mercury the 9th Jord in that context.
This shows how a “favourable planet” can also be
unfavourable.

(Mercury), the sub period ruled by the dispositor of

यद्धवेशदशाकाले TRAM FRAY |
षष्टाधिपान्तरे वापि तद्धावफलनाशनम् ॥१८१८ Il
1818. General: Note the lord of a given Bhava
and see when his dasa operates. The sub periods ruled
by the lords of the 6th/8th from that Bhava will
destroy the effects thereof.
Notes: This is a very important clue. As an
example, take Libra ascendant. The Moon rules
Cancer, the 10th, indicating one’s profession. From
Cancer, the 6th and 8th in order are Sagittarius and
Aquarius. Hence the sub periods of Jupiter and
Saturn, in the dasa of the Moon, will generally give
adverse effects about profession for a Libra native.

1879 - 1881. Irrelevant: Saturn coming to the

1882 - 1883. In the beginning and ending part of

Notes: The dasa periods denoted need some
clarifications. The dasa of Mars in its beginning and
concluding portions will cause danger in maternal
family. Second alternative is to note the planet that
will rule the 6th dasa and locate his sub period in the
dasa of Mars. Third alternative is the dasa of 5th lord
Venus.
Remember the gap between the dasa of Venus
and Mars. This warrants a judicious use of this
principle.

1884 - 1886. Capricorn ascendant - Gopriya
Nadiamsa: (a) Saturn should be in Vargottama as

(b) Incase of Capricorn ascendant (“Mandabhe”

Notes: (b) This yoga is on account of two
planets. One, Saturn in the 3rd in Pisces, and second,
Venus is in the 5th in Taurus. The yoga at “c” is
without reference to the position of Venus, but with
exclusive reference to Saturn in Pisces.
(c) The 2nd line of sloka 1886 - “Madhu Maase
Jhashe Mande Gopriyamsa Samudbhavah” - is indicative of two things. First the birth should be in
Gopriya Nadiamsa, which meaning has already been
adopted. Another clue that is expelled from the
translation is Saturn in Pisces and the month of
Chaitra to disallow incoherent and incomplete tnstructions.
‘Venus will become the lord of Aroodha ascendant, fora Capricorn native, if Saturn is in the 3rd in
Pisces. Thatis, the effects cited at“c” are for Saturn’s
placement in Pisces in case of Capricorn ascendant.
The importance is of course given to Venus ruling the
Aroodha ascendant.

1925 - 1936. (a) Father’s Health: Saturn in the

The idol made of sulfur should be of the following
description: Adoring a lotus in the navel (Damodara
does not refer to Vishnu, in this context), having a
` “long physique, bloody face, bloody eyes, six eyes,
three heads, horrible teeth, standing on a corpse,
holding a noose and axe, brandishing destructive fire

soaked inblood, resemblinga male, and wearing hair
and bones that were cast off. Such an idol should be

scents, (uncooked) yellow rice particles, flowers and
sacred water. Spending about ten gold coins, the
above worship be done. If the idol, three times of the
usual size, is installed in the southern direction
prosperity will result. Whatever diseases are there in
the father’s body will all disappear and instant recovery will come to pass. The idol should be given away

with the recitation of “Saanthim Dehi, Sukham Dehi
Twaarogyam Dehi Me Sadaa Aayurbalam Cha
Kaanthim Dehi MeSarva Sampadah’ (seeking peace,
happiness, health, life-span, vigour, splendour and
all kinds of wealth in exchange of the whole ritual).

Deva Keralam
1972 - 1977. (a) Troubles from Cats & Rats: The
planet Ketu should be in 12th (Cancer) while his
dispositor joins Mars (elsewhere). As a result, the
native will be troubled by cats, rats and poisonous
fevers. The effects stated forthe 4th dasa will be true;
for overcoming the evils effects concerned, suitable
remedies be done.
(b) The dasa of the 4th lord from the ascendant
or from the Moon sign will endanger the mother of the
native of Dhaanya Nadiamsa. Alternatively his
paternal grand-father will die in the sub period of
Mercury or of Ketu, during the rulership of the 2nd
dasa. A maternal uncle will lose bis son. In the sub
period of Mercury, there will be a family marnage,
and much happiness (on other accounts).
(c) The 2nd dasa b=‘ng that of Saturn will cause
the destruction of the “sou of paternal grand-father”
(i.e some paternal uncle will die). His sons will be
sinful and be grieved by poverty and piles. Vt ey will
be happy aftersome time. The son of paternal grandfather’s brother will be somewhat sickly, and indolent and will experience happiness through his own
efforts.

1978 - 19821/2. Irrelevant: In the dasa of the 9th
lord, the king will be destroyed. In first dasa father
will financially gain. Inthe dasa of the 10th lord, two
kings will be destroyed. The native’s relatives will be
involved in great quarrels; especially in the royal
circles, the treasury, steeds, money etc. will be
destroyed. Inthe first period, and also more specially
in Mercury’s sub period distress will come to pass.
The Moon in Aries with Taurus Navamsa will cause
quarrels in Pandya kingdom when Saturn moves in
one of the four fixed signs; kings of the respective
kingdoms will be introuble. These are the effects for
9th lord’s sub period in the 2nd lord’s dasa fora native
of Paasa Nadiamsa.

1983 - 1985. (a) Rahu in 4853 Nadiamsa will
cause increasing progress for the sons of Leo native.
Throughout his life he will enjoy wealth and increased auspicious effects.
(b) The father of the native with Sun in Dhaanya
Nadiamsa while the Moon is in Paasa Nadiamsa will
perform good acts, will follow Vedic codes due to his
association with Soodras (it should actually mean
that he will befriend Soodras, follow Vedic codes), be
righteous and will honours gods and Brahmins.

1988 - 19891/2. Much grief will be seen in the
circle of father’s friends when Saturn moves intransit
in the sign indicated by the 11th lord’s Navamsa, or

relatives’ and in-laws’ circles. In the native’s third

1990 - 1992. (a) Father’s Death: In the firstsub
period of 8th lord Jupiter’s dasa, the native of Leo
ascendant, Bahudhaanya Nadiamsa, will lose his
father with the Sun in transit in Libra, or a trine
thereof, in a dark lunar half, on a Saturday.
(a) The Sun in Dhaanya Nadiamsa denotes that
his father will attain meritorious worlds. The native
will also be in danger in the 8th lord’s dasa.

स्वपिता जननं fest स्वगर्भे न्रे dere:
1993 - 1994 1/2. The Moon inthe company of the
4th lord (Mars) in Paasa Nadiamsa will cause his next
birth intraders’ community. This yoga indicates that
in the previous birth also the person had belonged to
the same community. These are the effects for the
native and his father. Due to the good deeds, his
father will have three lucky births. The native’s
father after his death, will be reborn to the very loins
of the present native.
Notes: This relates to only Bahudhaanya
Nadiamsa, Leo ascendant. The Nadiamsa referred to
in the text is for the Moon’s position although a litera]
translation would mean birth in Paasa Nadiamsa.

तदीशे कुजसंयुक्ते agar सुलोकवान् |

1995 - 1997. If the Sun is in Dhaanya Nadiamsa
(elsewhere) while Ketu is in the 12th (Cancer), and
the Moon is with Mars, the native’s father will dwell
fora long time in other worlds including the hell (i.e.
will not take rebirth immediately). Due to the good
deeds father will enjoy prosperity in the whole of his
life (in the current birth). With Ketu inthe 12th, grief
to father on account of loss cf a son in old age will
come to pass. The effects for the Sun’s position (and
birth} in Bahudhaanya Nadiamsa are from the work
entitled “Surya Nadi”.
